# warp34 — Session Orientation & Current State

## Data-Informed Tiered-MoE Pareto Auto-Fit Planner & `TP=1` Dynamic-LRU Fix

- **Empirical Pareto Sweep & `k_static` Dynamic-LRU Fix (`crates/warp34-host/src/moe_offload/engine.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/model/{rank_capacity.rs,rank.rs,tests.rs}`):**
  - Swept `hot_per_layer` on `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W2.6A16-HGPTQ-RCO` (`1–2× RTX 3090 Ti`):
    - Discovered & fixed a `TP=1` bug in `moe_offload/engine.rs` where `k_static` disabled its `16` dynamic LRU slots whenever `k_hot > 96` (`..=TIERED_MOE_TP1_SERVING_KV_DEFAULT_HOT`), causing a `-24%` decode cliff (`51.7 -> 39.2 tok/s` at `128`). Changing to `k_hot >= TIERED_MOE_DYNAMIC_LRU_MIN_HOT_EXPERTS (64)` lifted `TP=1 RCO` decode at `192/512` from `43.92` to **`59.83 tok/s` (`+36.2%`, `-23%` TTFT)** and revealed the hard `-31%` dynamic-LRU cliff below `k_hot = 64` (`48.79 -> 33.63 tok/s`).
    - Measured the `TP=2 RCO` curve (`64..224/256`: `57.5 -> 70.5 -> 84.3 -> 96.2 -> 102.2 -> 106.0 tok/s`, TTFT `515 -> 165 ms`), pinpointing the empirical diminishing-returns knee at **`75%` of `local_experts`** (`+12%..+23%` per `+12.5%` experts below `75%`, dropping to `< 4%` above `75%`).
  - **Uncapped Smooth Hot-Expert Sizing (`plan_tiered_moe_hot_per_layer_with_options`):** Removed the static `96`/`104`/`160` ceilings, stopped charging the `~1.15 GiB` `MTP` reserve in `Plain` mode (`TIERED_MOE_PLAIN_METADATA_RESERVE_BYTES = 128 MiB`), raised `MTP_PARTIAL_CACHE_HEADROOM_BYTES` (`256 -> 512 MiB`) and `TIERED_MOE_MTP_AND_GRAPH_RESERVE_BYTES` (`640 -> 896 MiB`) so every `MTP-3` mode keeps `>= 508 MiB` free VRAM.
  - **3-Tier Pareto Auto-Fit (`select_tiered_moe_auto_fit_pages`):** When `--ctx-size`/`--kv-pages` is omitted on tiered-MoE models: (1) expand KV above `128K` up to `262K` only while maintaining `hot >= 75%`; (2) pick the largest context in **`[64K, 128K]`** that keeps `hot >= 64` (dynamic-LRU floor, never sacrificing context below `64K` to chase `hot = 64`); (3) fallback to the largest context that fits at all on tight GPUs.
  - **Verified Live (`1–2× RTX 3090 Ti`, zero manual flags, `8/8` pass, `>= 508 MiB` free on all 8 modes):**
    - `W4` (`TP=1/TP=2`, Plain/`MTP-3`): `128K` auto-fit across all 4 modes, `free = 1,546 / 508 / 1,502 / 1,326 MiB`, `W4 TP=1 MTP-3` `45.1 tok/s` end-to-end (vs `33 tok/s` prior), `W4 TP=2 MTP-3` `72.7 tok/s`.
    - `RCO` (`TP=1/TP=2`, Plain/`MTP-3`): `TP=1` `128K` (`38.0 / 66.2 tok/s`, `free = 1,618 / 720 MiB`), `TP=2 Plain` **`262K` (`100%`)** (`69.9 tok/s`, `free = 1,534 MiB`), `TP=2 MTP-3` **`128K`** (`107.2 tok/s`, `free = 1,056 MiB`).
  - **Gates:** `mise run check-quick` PASS, `mise run test-quick` (`383/383` host + `4/4` kernels PASS), `review` (`0` actionable findings).

## Single-Layout Shared-Weight `W4A8` INT8-IMMA Prefill (`sm_86` / `sm_89`, Zero Extra Weight VRAM)

- **Shared-`k16`-Layout `W4A8` IMMA Kernel & Dynamic Per-Token `INT8` Quantizer (`crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/marlin/{kernel.h,marlin_template.h}`, `crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_marlin.cu`, `crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/warp34_cuda.h`, `crates/warp34-host/src/cuda/warmup.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/model/{forward.rs,weights/hgptq.rs}`):**
  - Added `shared_k16_layout` to `Marlin<...>` (`warp34_marlin_gemm_s8u4_shared`, 48 specializations across `g32`/`g64`/`g128`/`channelwise` and `{64,256,256}` / `{64,128,128}` / `{128,64,128}`, all `local=0`, `static_shared<=48 KiB`, warmed on all ranks in `prepare_execution_plan()`) plus vectorized `per_token_quant_i8_kernel` (`warp34_per_token_quant_i8`, `half2`/8-way warp-reduced absmax + `round-half-away` to `[-127, 127]`, `1e-10f` floor).
  - Unpacks existing `k16` (`16k × 64n`, `W4A16`) `U4B8` weight tiles directly into `mma.sync.aligned.m16n8k32.row.col.s32.s8.s8.s32` (`int2` even/odd `k16` sub-tile load + width-4 `__shfl_sync` + `__byte_perm` + `lop3` `^ 0x80` dequant, zero shared-memory bank conflicts, **zero extra weight VRAM**: `14,950 MiB` ready VRAM unchanged on `Qwen3.5-9B` `TQ4`) and applies exact `FP16` group/channel scales in `FP32` (preserving signed negative Hadamard-rotated scales without `int16` scale quantization loss).
  - Fixed 4 latent upstream `is_a_8bit` bugs in `marlin_template.h` / `op_marlin.cu`: `zero_accums` stack buffer OOB (`frag_c` has `2` K-subfragments for `s8` vs `4` for `f16`), `get_kernel_cache_size` missing `sh_a_s_size = 64 * thread_m_blocks` (`256 B`), uninitialized `sh_a_s` tail rows when `prob_m < 16 * thread_m_blocks`, and `thread_block_reduce` `sh_red` row stride (`32 * tb_n_warps`) + per-chunk `prob_m_split <= 16` Split-K / 1-slice tail selection.
  - Gated via `WARP34_EXEC_FLAG_ALLOW_W4A8_PREFILL` in `run_layer_stack` (`!is_capturing_graph && dump_out.is_none() && rows >= 128 && attn.max_q_len >= 128 && gdn_batch.max_verify_tokens == 0 && gdn_batch.spec_state_indices.is_null()`) and `compute_major == 8 && compute_minor >= 6` in `op_marlin.cu` (auto-bypassed on `sm_80` A100 where `FP16`-mma `FP32`-acc is full-rate; bulk `(M / 64) * 64` rows run `W4A8` while `< 64`-row tails, `M < 128` decode/batched-decode/speculative-verify steps, CUDA Graphs, `dump-diff`, and `lm_head` stay 0-ULP `W4A16`).
- **Verification & 4-Engine `Qwen3.5-9B` Re-Sweep (`1×` & `2× RTX 3090 Ti` `sm_86`):**
  - **Unit, Sanitizer, Oracle, & Regression Gates:** `quant_formats` (`10/10`, `rel_l2 = 0.00019..0.00055` across `g32`/`g64`/`g128`/`channelwise`, mixed-sign scales, Split-K, and in-scratch overlap protection), `compute-sanitizer` (`memcheck` + `racecheck` + `synccheck`: `0` errors / `0` hazards), `mise run oracle-dump` (`6/6` `TP=1` vs `TP=2`), `mise run oracle-greedy` (`2/2`, `lossless=4/4`, `radix_warm=4/4`), `mise run bench-gate` (`5/5` PASS), and `mise run bench-gate-full` (`15/15` PASS across `4B`, `9B+LoRA`, `27B`, `35B-A3B`, and `Flash-Next`).
  - **1-GPU Plain (`1× RTX 3090 Ti`, FP16 KV) — `10/11 #1` (tied within `0.2%` on `decode-c1`):**
    - **Cold `pp-4k..115k` (`tok/s` p50):** **`8,631.9 / 7,705.8 / 6,639.4 / 5,694.2 / 4,204.7`** (`+33.6%..+67.5%` over prior `warp34` `5,153..3,148`; **`+18.4%..+59.8%`** over `llama.cpp` `5,401..3,552`; **`+37.0%..+71.3%`** over `vLLM 0.34.0`; **`+36.8%..+80.1%`** over `SGLang v0.5.23`). `TQ4` KV (`262K` ctx) also jumps `+25.4%..+77.7%` to **`8,375.9 / 6,857.5 / 5,453.6 / 4,403.2 / 2,912.7`** (`throughput-c16` **`1,313.0`** beating `vLLM` `1,275.8` & `SGLang` `1,295.1`).

  - **2-GPU `TP=2` Plain & Native `MTP-3` (`2× RTX 3090 Ti`, FP16 KV) — `22/22 #1` Clean Sweep:** Plain `pp-4k..115k` **`8,904.7 / 8,450.2 / 7,813.7 / 7,102.5 / 5,612.0`** (`+25.0%..+38.8%` vs prior `warp34`, `+46.2%..+86.5%` vs `llama.cpp`), `C16` **`1,524.8`**, `swe-agent-64k/120k` **`46.4 / 24.3 tok/s`**; `MTP-3` `pp-4k..115k` **`8,408.0 / 7,987.2 / 7,482.9 / 6,842.8 / 5,491.9`** (`+76.1%..+101.7%` vs `llama.cpp`), `decode-c1` **`338.6`**, `C8/C16` **`1,183.3 / 1,675.8`**, `swe-agent-64k/120k` **`49.8 / 26.4 tok/s`**.

## 9B Single-Card & Dual-Card (`TP=2`) Head-to-Head (`1–2× RTX 3090 Ti`), Short-Prompt Burst Packing, & Full-Duplex Pipelined PCIe `TP=2` Collectives

- **Dashboard, Metrics, LoRA Visibility, & Startup Banner Accuracy (`d1bd2bf`, `ca17d41`, `1e70b8b`, `a644b6e`):**
  - Added optional `pools.gdn_checkpoints` (`vram_capacity`/`vram_used`/`host_capacity`/`host_used`) and `config.lora_adapters` to `/api/metrics` + `warp34_pool_units{pool="gdn_ckpt_vram|gdn_ckpt_host"}` and `warp34_lora_adapters_loaded` to `/metrics` (`docs/METRICS.md`), surfaced tiered `WHT128-Q8 checkpoints`, `max_model_len`, numeric `cached` counts, and subtle LoRA badge + Playground `MODEL / LORA` selector in `dashboard/`, and added live `/v1/responses` streaming tool-call accumulation.
  - Updated `format_serve_boot_banner` (`crates/warp34-host/src/cli/commands.rs`) to display loaded LoRAs, configured `--reasoning-effort`, exact `pool_geometry`-derived KV compression ratios, and accurate tail-anchored `TQ4` (`Q8_0` on `sm_80`/`sm_86`, `FP8` on `sm_89+` across all ranks) and `fp8` (`1.94x, 264B/row`) descriptions.
- **9B Head-to-Head Campaign (`benchmarks/perf/campaigns/9b-head-to-head.toml`, `9a43edc`, `1× RTX 3090 Ti` `sm_86`):**
  - Evaluated `warp34 FP16`, `warp34 TQ4`, `vLLM 0.34.0`, and `SGLang v0.5.23` serially across 11 cases (3 reps + warmup, 100% pass; raw artifacts in `benchmarks/perf/results/9b-head-to-head-2026-11-19/`):
    - **Cold prompt processing (`pp`, tok/s p50):** `warp34 FP16` swept 5/5 (`4.9K`: `5,153` `+2.3%/+7.5%`; `19.4K`: `4,771` `+3.8%/+3.4%`; `38.7K`: `4,274` `+2.5%/+3.6%`; `57.6K`: `3,908` `+2.3%/+2.9%`; `115.1K`: `3,147` `+2.5%/+2.4%`).
    - **Long-context coding agent:** `warp34 FP16` swept 2/2 (`64K`: `27.02 tok/s` `+4.7%/+4.7%`, warm TTFT `401 ms` vs `566`/`582 ms`; `120K`: `14.22 tok/s` `+8.2%/+6.4%`, warm TTFT `495 ms` vs `640`/`674 ms`).
    - **Decode & batching:** `warp34 FP16` won `C4` (`460.3` `+6.4%/+4.4%`) and `C8` (`812.2` `+2.1%/+6.4%`), beat vLLM at `C1` (`127.5` `+3.0%/-1.0%`), and initially trailed at `throughput-c16` (`1,229.2` vs vLLM `1,275.8` / SGLang `1,295.1`) due to ~100–250 ms/wave TTFT inflation.
- **Short-Prompt Burst Scheduler Fix (`crates/warp34-host/src/scheduler.rs`, `e9cb5d4`):**
  - Diagnosed two causes of `throughput-c16` TTFT inflation: (1) pure-prefill equal-share chunking split 16 × 165-token prompts into `128 + 37` chunks (doubling GDN launches and intermediate state traffic), and (2) once any request entered decode, a flat `256`-token mixed cap serialized remaining short prompts 1-by-1 across 10+ forward passes.
  - Updated `SchedulerV1::schedule_step` to skip pure-prefill splitting for short prompts (`rem <= mixed_cap`) and scale the aggregate mixed-step prefill budget by the count of eligible short causal prefills (`rem <= mixed_cap`, while keeping single-slice and multi-bulk mixed prefills strictly capped at `mixed_cap = 256`), with regression coverage in `test_short_prompt_burst_packs_without_splitting_and_scales_mixed_wave`.
  - Verified on `1× RTX 3090 Ti`: `throughput-c16` jumped from `1,229.2` to `1,255.7–1,274.1 tok/s` and `ttft_p50` dropped from `558 ms` to `413.6 ms` (faster than both `vLLM` `456.7 ms` and `SGLang` `489.8 ms`), with `mise run check-quick`, `15/15` `scheduler::tests`, and `review` clean.
- **Full-Duplex 8-Chunk Pipelined PCIe `TP=2` Collectives (`Tp2DmaPipe`, `crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_collectives.cu`, `crates/warp34-host/src/cuda/collectives.rs`):**
  - Root-caused the initial `TP=2` cold-prefill gap (`777d533`) via `nsys`: on `M=2048` (`16 MiB`) PCIe AllReduces without `iommu=pt`, serialized single-stream `cudaMemcpyAsync` TX (`1.274 ms`) + SM RX (`1.323 ms` = `2.597 ms` total) left PCIe half-duplex while `vLLM`'s `RING_LL` overlapped TX and RX (`2.208 ms` + `0.075 ms` norm = `2.283 ms`).
  - Ported `Tp2DmaPipe` (`stream`, `ev_ready`, `ev_tx_done: [CudaEvent; 8]`) and `warp34_tp2_all_reduce_pipelined_f16` (`tp2_all_reduce_rx_chunk_f16_kernel`, warmed in `warp34_tp2_warmup()`) from `qwen-image` (`752d1b3`), and extended 8-chunk CUDA-event fork-join pipelining (`>= 512 KiB` on real 2-GPU PCIe mapped host memory, `kRankForceDmaTxFlag` + `kRankSkipTrailingBarrierFlag` on chunks `0..k-2`, 100% CUDA-Graph capturable) to `warp34_tp2_all_reduce_residual_gemma_rms_norm_hadamard_pipelined_f16`.
  - Verified `0`-ULP bit-for-bit parity across `ar_out`, `residual`, `out`, `out_had64` (including `out == 0`), strided `tap_dst_bf16`, odd-tail in-place `all_reduce_f16`, `< 64 KiB` 1-barrier transitions, and 2-rank CUDA Graph capture/replay in `tp2_all_reduce_residual_gemma_rms_norm_hadamard64_matches_standalone_0_ulp`, `9/9` `collectives_tp2` `compute-sanitizer` (`memcheck`, `racecheck`, `synccheck`) runs, and 9B `dump-diff --compare-tp2` (`958` tokens / `7.66 MiB`: `min_cosine=0.999934`, `tp1_top1 == tp2_top1 == 16`).
- **9B `TP=2` Head-to-Head Campaign — 11/11 Clean Sweep (`benchmarks/perf/campaigns/9b-tp2-head-to-head.toml`, `2× RTX 3090 Ti` `sm_86`, PCIe `TP=2`):**
  - Serial 3-engine run (`warp34-9b-tp2-fp16` vs `vLLM 0.34.0` vs `SGLang v0.5.23`, 11 cases × 3 reps + warmup, 100% pass; raw artifacts in `benchmarks/perf/results/9b-tp2-head-to-head-2026-11-19/`):
    - **Cold prompt processing (`pp`, tok/s p50 — 5/5 sweep, `+13.5%..+19.9%` over unpipelined `warp34`):**
      - `4.9K`: **`6,413.3 tok/s`** (`757.4 ms` TTFT) — **`+14.9%`** vs vLLM (`5,583.1`, `870.0 ms`), **`+20.6%`** vs SGLang (`5,317.5`, `913.5 ms`).
      - `19.4K`: **`6,174.2 tok/s`** (`3,141.2 ms` TTFT) — **`+14.4%`** vs vLLM (`5,397.7`, `3,593.1 ms`), **`+15.2%`** vs SGLang (`5,360.6`, `3,618.0 ms`).
      - `38.7K`: **`5,822.7 tok/s`** (`6,648.9 ms` TTFT) — **`+14.3%`** vs vLLM (`5,096.0`, `7,597.0 ms`), **`+15.9%`** vs SGLang (`5,025.9`, `7,703.0 ms`).

## Tiered `WHT128-Q8_0` (`BF16` Scale) GDN Checkpoint Pool

- **Warp-Coalesced `WHT128-Q8_0` CUDA Kernels (`crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_gdn_ckpt.cu`, `crates/warp34-kernels/src/lib.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/cuda/warmup.rs`):**
  - Added `warp34_gdn_checkpoint_pack_wht_q8` and `warp34_gdn_checkpoint_unpack_wht_q8` (`sm_80,sm_86,sm_89` fatbinary, `local=0`, warmed in `prepare_execution_plan()`). Each warp processes one `d_k = 128` GDN `ssm_state` row via a 7-stage butterfly Walsh-Hadamard Transform (`4` register elements/lane + `__shfl_xor_sync` stages + minimal `256 B/warp` smem transpose) and symmetric `INT8` quantization with `BF16` (`E8M7`) block-32 scales (preserving sub-`FP16` `[1e-32, 6e-5]` rows without underflow at `> 44 dB` SNR) alongside `0 ULP` `FP16` `conv_state` and optional `PLE` conv/n-gram state.
  - Compresses a `Qwen3.8-27B` `TP=1` GDN checkpoint bundle by `3.58×` (`153.9 MiB` `FP32` -> `41.1 MiB` `WHT128-Q8_0`, `20.5 MiB/rank` at `TP=2`; `8.7 MiB/rank` on `35B-A3B MoE` `TP=2`).
- **Tiered L1 VRAM + Lazy L2 Pinned Host-RAM Pool (`crates/warp34-host/src/cache/checkpoint_pool.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/model/{rank_capacity.rs,rank.rs,engine.rs,execution.rs,batch.rs,generation.rs}`):**
  - Added `GdnCheckpointPool` (`CHECKPOINT_POOL_SLOT_BASE = 1_000_000` namespace) isolating idle `MambaRadixCache` checkpoints from active `FP32` `StateBundlePool` execution/verify slots (`DFlash2` `M=8` and batched `MTP` never lose verify slots to cached prefixes, and decode-tail checkpoints are no longer immediately evicted under tight single-GPU `262,144`-token VRAM budgets).
  - Hot checkpoints reside in L1 VRAM (`<= 16` slots from post-KV VRAM slack) and LRU-spill asynchronously into lazily allocated L2 `PinnedHostBuffer` (`cudaHostAllocPortable` direct PCIe DMA with automatic pageable fallback), auto-sized from `/proc/meminfo` `MemAvailable` and cgroup v2 `memory.max - memory.current` (`<= 128` slots, `<= 4 GiB` across all TP ranks, `TP>=2` `truncate_slots` lockstep).
  - On `2× RTX 4090` (`sm_89`), `warp34 serve` auto-sizes `64` active `FP32` GDN slots + `115` `WHT128-Q8_0` checkpoints (`16` L1 VRAM + `99` L2 Host, `20.5 MiB/slot`) alongside `262,144` `tq4` KV tokens on `Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TP=2`), and `64` active `FP32` GDN slots + `144` `WHT128-Q8_0` checkpoints (`16` L1 VRAM + `128` L2 Host, `8.7 MiB/slot`) on `Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TP=2`).
- **Deferred Pack/Unpack & Prefill / Turn-Completion Checkpoint Donation (`crates/warp34-host/src/{radix.rs,scheduler.rs,proposer.rs,proposer/scheduled.rs,engine_worker.rs,engine_worker/runner.rs,engine_worker/decode.rs}`, `crates/warp34-host/src/cli/commands.rs`):**
  - `SchedulerV1` stays pure-CPU via deferred `pending_unpack_ckpt_slot` / `pending_checkpoint_pack` transitions materialized on `rank.stream` (including pipelined `wait_and_release_deferred` ordering).
  - Matching `vLLM 0.31.0` (`mamba_cache_mode = "align"`) and `SGLang v0.5.19` (`MambaRadixCache`), checkpoints are donated into `GdnCheckpointPool` at **prefill completion** (so retries after cancelled/failed turns and sibling requests hit the prompt prefix) and at **turn completion** (`finish_req = true`, so the next multi-turn request hits the end of the assistant turn), while decode rotates the 2-slot active `FP32` ping-pong buffer in-place at `64`-token boundaries without creating mid-generation radix nodes or launching mid-decode checkpoint packs.
  - When `GdnCheckpointPool` has no free or evictable slots on donation, `donate_committed_checkpoint_with_replacement` recycles the request's own locked checkpoint-pool slot (`can_recycle_locked_checkpoint_mamba`) rather than donating an active `FP32` `StateBundlePool` slot, and `apply_radix_donation_with_radix` rolls back physical L1/L2 slots and invalidates unpopulated radix nodes if `pack_checkpoint_from_active` fails. Surfaced tiered L1/L2 checkpoint slot counts in `format_serve_boot_banner` and `refresh_pool_metrics`.
- **Verified Across `2× RTX 3090 Ti` (`sm_86`) & `2× RTX 4090` (`sm_89`):**
  - **`2× RTX 3090 Ti` (`sm_86`):** `mise run check` (`365/365` `warp34-host` unit tests, `4/4` `radix_cache_and_scheduler`, `7/7` `speculative_handoff`, `120/120` `compute-sanitizer` `memcheck`/`racecheck`/`synccheck` runs with `0` errors), `mise run oracle-dump` (`6/6`), `mise run oracle-greedy` (`2/2`, `lossless=4/4`, `radix_warm=4/4`), `mise run bench-gate` (`5/5 PASS`), and `mise run bench-gate-full` (`15/15 PASS`: `4B` `TP=1` Plain `209.6 tok/s`, `MTP-3` `576.1 tok/s`, `DFlash2` `1,568.9 tok/s`; `4B` `TP=2` Plain `283.9 tok/s`, `MTP-3` `753.0 tok/s`, `DFlash2` `1,796.9 tok/s`; `9B+LoRA` `TP=1` `122.9 tok/s`; `27B` `TP=2` Plain `92.7 tok/s`, `MTP-3` `239.0 tok/s`, `DFlash2` `342.4 tok/s`; `35B-A3B` `TP=2` Plain `252.9 tok/s`, `MTP-3` `574.1 tok/s`; `Flash-Next` `TP=2` `MTP-3` `185.4 tok/s`).
  - **`2× RTX 4090` (`sm_89`, Vast.ai instance `61212559`, destroyed after verification):** `warp34-host` unit tests (`wht128_q8_roundtrip_and_l2_spill_restore`), `4/4` `radix_cache_and_scheduler`, `speculative_handoff` (`realistic_swe_trajectory_caches_across_plain_mtp_and_dflash2`), `compute-sanitizer` (`memcheck`, `racecheck`, `synccheck` = `0` errors/`0` hazards), `oracle-dump` (`6/6`), `oracle-greedy` (`2/2`, `lossless=4/4`, `radix_warm=4/4`), and `bench-gate-full` (`13/13 PASS`: `4B` `TP=1` Plain **`220.0 tok/s`**, `MTP-3` **`610.2 tok/s`**, `DFlash2` **`1,728.1 tok/s`**; `4B` `TP=2` Plain **`324.6 tok/s`**, `MTP-3` **`876.4 tok/s`**, `DFlash2` **`2,056.1 tok/s`**; `9B+LoRA` `TP=1` **`135.1 tok/s`**; `27B` `TP=2` Plain **`90.9 tok/s`**, `MTP-3` **`232.5 tok/s`**, `DFlash2` **`353.8 tok/s`**; `35B-A3B` `TP=2` Plain **`292.8 tok/s`**, `MTP-3` **`696.1 tok/s`**; `Flash-Next` `TP=2` `MTP-3` **`187.8 tok/s`**).

## Generation request diagnostics & automatic serving VRAM fit (`warp34 serve`)

- **Automatic Context & KV Format Fitting (`crates/warp34-host/src/model/{rank_capacity.rs,rank.rs,engine.rs}`, `crates/warp34-host/src/cli/{options.rs,commands.rs}`):**
  - When `--ctx-size` / `--kv-pages` and `--max-model-len` are omitted, `warp34 serve` (`Qwen3_5Engine::load_for_serving_with_auto_fit` / `Qwen3_5ModelRank::fit_serving_plan`) automatically fits the largest `64`-page-aligned (`1,024`-token) context window up to the model's `max_position_embeddings` (`262,144` tokens / `16,384` pages) across Plain, `MTP`, and `DFlash2` after reserving weights (with automatic `MappedHostBuffer` embedding offload when needed), scratch arena, speculative drafter reserve, required GDN state slots, and runtime headroom.
  - When `--kv-format` is omitted or set to `auto` on `head_dim == 256` models, `warp34 serve` prefers `tq4` (`3.07×`, rotated `Q8_0` boundaries + `TQ4` interior) when the full target context fits, and automatically steps down to `tq4_all` (`3.88×`, `132 B/row` `TQ4` across all attention layers) when doing so unlocks a larger context window (for example, fitting the full `262,144` context + `13` GDN state slots for `Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ` + `DFlash2` on a single 24 GiB RTX 3090 Ti). When `--kv-format` is explicitly pinned, `warp34 serve` preserves the chosen format and auto-fits the maximum context that fits in VRAM.
  - Added a `warp34` ASCII startup banner (`format_serve_boot_banner`) displaying hardware, model, speculative drafter, KV format (`[auto]` / `[auto: stepped down from tq4 to fit full context]` / `[configured]`), context window (`[auto-fit: ...% of ... model max]` / `[configured]`), GDN state pool slots, memory placement, and HTTP endpoint.
- **Structured Per-Turn Request & Cache Logging (`crates/warp34-host/src/server/{request_log.rs,http.rs}`, `crates/warp34-host/src/{responses_api.rs,scheduler.rs,radix.rs}`):**
  - `warp34 serve` emits structured per-turn JSON summaries (`inference.turn_end`, `inference.http_error`) on stderr for generation APIs with globally unique 128-bit CSPRNG request IDs (`chatcmpl-<32 hex>`, `cmpl-<32 hex>`, `resp_<32 hex>`, `msg_<32 hex>`), requested vs effective output budgets, stop reasons, chat request shape (`messages`, `tools`, `enable_thinking`), effective `sampling` parameters (`temperature`, `top_p`, `top_k`, `min_p`, `seed`), output token breakdown (`output_tokens`, `reasoning_tokens`, `tool_calls`), `MambaRadixCache` lookup diagnostics (`lookup_tokens`, `kv_matched_tokens`, `checkpoint_tokens`, `restored_tokens`, `reason`), and response timing. See [per-turn Docker logs](METRICS.md#per-turn-docker-logs) for truncation and prefix-cache queries and field semantics.
- **Multi-Turn Tool-Call & Trajectory Prefix Caching Across Plain, `MTP`, and `DFlash2` (`crates/warp34-host/src/{parser.rs,parser/native_schema.rs,chatml.rs,dflash.rs,proposer.rs,proposer/scheduled.rs,engine_worker/runner.rs,scheduler.rs,server/http.rs,responses_api.rs,anthropic_api.rs}`):**
  - **$O(1)$-per-token incremental Qwen3-XML tool-call streaming (`parser.rs`, `parser/native_schema.rs`, `server/http.rs`, `responses_api.rs`, `anthropic_api.rs`):** `Qwen3Parser::push_str` advances an incremental tool-call cursor (`ToolStreamState` / `advance_native_tool_stream` / `advance_legacy_tool_stream`) that emits `StreamToolCallDelta` header (`index`, `id`, `name`) and JSON-escaped `arguments_delta` chunks in real time while holding back wrapping newlines and `</parameter>` tag prefixes, replacing the previous $O(N^2)$ per-token `native_tool_end` buffer re-scan and buffering until `</tool_call>`. `ValueGrammar::AnyString` (`native_schema.rs`) short-circuits unconstrained `{"type": "string"}` parameter acceptance in $O(1)$, and all three streaming SSE endpoints (`/v1/chat/completions` `delta.tool_calls`, `/v1/responses` `response.output_item.added` + `response.function_call_arguments.delta`, and `/v1/messages` `content_block_start` `tool_use` + `input_json_delta`) stream tool headers and arguments token-by-token without duplicate emissions at `finish()`.
  - **Native Qwen3-XML tool-call argument & streaming template newline parity (`parser.rs`, `chatml.rs`):** `Qwen3Parser::convert_arg` strips single wrapping newlines (`trim_wrapping_newlines`) on native string and fallback tool arguments (matching `vllm/parser/qwen3.py`), and `Qwen3Parser::push_str` (`push_content_slice`) strips leading template `\n\n` separators after `</think>` and inter-tool `\n` newlines (matching `Qwen3Parser::finish()` and `vLLM`), ensuring streaming `ChatMessage` round-trips through `format_chatml_prompt_with_options` with exact byte-for-byte prefix parity.
  - **Checkpoint `generation_config.json` default sampling parameters & `262K`-context `1,024`/`2,048`-row proposer prefill chunks (`parser.rs`, `engine_worker.rs`, `server.rs`, `server/http.rs`, `responses_api.rs`, `proposer.rs`):** `EngineWorker` loads `<model_dir>/generation_config.json` into `default_sampling_params` (`SamplingParams::from_generation_config_dir`, matching `vLLM 0.31.0` `ModelConfig.get_diff_sampling_param()` for `temperature`, `top_p`, `top_k`, `min_p`, penalties, and `do_sample: false`) and applies it across `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/completions`, `/v1/responses`, and `/v1/messages` whenever a request omits or sends `null` for sampling fields (so clients like Zed Editor inherit Qwen 3.5 / 3.6 / 3.8's `temperature: 1.0, top_p: 0.95, top_k: 20` defaults instead of falling into deterministic `temperature = 0.0` `<think>` repetition loops). `NativeProposer::load` (`plan_proposer_max_rows`) sizes `max_rows` to `1,024` (`MEDIUM_SCRATCH_MAX_BATCHED_TOKENS`) on `TP=1` `262K`-context serving (`336 MiB` scratch) and `2,048` (`SERVING_PROPOSER_MAX_TAP_ROWS`) on `TP >= 2` `262K`-context serving (`512 MiB` scratch), bounded by `ordinary_prefill_token_capacity(1)` with graceful fallback to `256` rows on OOM, avoiding `256`-token prefill micro-chunking on long multi-turn agent prompts.
  - **`DFlash2` sliding-window ring wrap-around & suffix-window prefix restoration (`dflash.rs`, `proposer.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`):** `DFlash2Rank::ring_phys_pages_per_slot` reports the full physical ring capacity (`all_pages.len()` = `1,104` pages / `17,664` tokens on `TP=1`, `2,304` pages / `36,864` tokens on `TP=2`) in single-slot mode (`!batch_mode`) and per-slot capacity in batch mode, while `can_restore_for_prompt` and `dflash_slot_has_prefix_for_prompt` evaluate the `DFlash2` sliding window at `prompt_len` (after suffix prefill) and allow restoring any `MambaRadixCache` checkpoint where `prompt_len - matched >= sliding_window` (`2,048` tokens on `27B`) via `DFlash2Rank::set_committed`, preventing long reasoning/decode turns from triggering false `reason: "admission_filter"` step-backs when `<think>...</think>` is stripped on follow-up turns.
  - **Cancellation & full-context `MTP` memo retention (`engine_worker/runner.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`, `proposer.rs`):** Client request cancellations in `cancel_requests` flush pending speculative tokens (`finish_scheduled` / `finish_mtp_batch_slot`) and donate committed checkpoints to `MambaRadixCache` (`finish_request_early`) while keeping resident drafter KV warm, and `MEMO_BUDGET` is raised to `4 GiB` so `MTP` retains full `262,144`-token `27B` trajectories (`2.50 GiB`). Verified end-to-end across Plain, `MTP`, and `DFlash2` in `realistic_swe_trajectory_caches_across_plain_mtp_and_dflash2`.

## Multi-Architecture Hardware Verification (`sm_80` 2× A100-SXM4-40GB, `sm_86` 2× RTX 3090 / 3090 Ti, `sm_89` 2× RTX 4090)

- **Live Multi-Arch Verification Suite (`tools/check_cuda_sanitizer.py`, `crates/warp34-host/src/model/weights.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/cli/oracle.rs`, `tools/oracle/goldens/4B/`):**
  - Verified the `sm_80,sm_86,sm_89` multi-arch fatbinary across three rented 2-GPU Vast.ai systems (`2× NVIDIA A100-SXM4-40GB` `sm_80` 108 SMs, `2× NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090` `sm_89` 128 SMs, and `2× NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090` non-Ti `sm_86` 82 SMs) alongside local `2× RTX 3090 Ti` (`sm_86` 84 SMs):
    - **117/117 `compute-sanitizer` (`memcheck`, `racecheck`, `synccheck`)** runs (`0` errors) across all 6 CUDA kernel subsystems (`gdn`, `attention`, `marlin_moe`, `norms_rope_act`, `sampling`, `collectives`) + `TP=2` cross-GPU collectives on every architecture.
    - **4/4 `warp34-kernels` + 345/345 `warp34-host` GPU/host unit tests** passing on all three remote 2-GPU systems (`346/346` locally including `forced_simd_tier_throughput_and_parity` which invokes `rustc`).
    - **`dump-diff --compare-tp2` (`TP=1` vs `TP=2` tensor & top-1 parity)** passing across `4B` (Plain, `MTP-3`, `DFlash2`) and `35B-A3B MoE` (Plain, `MTP-3`, `tp1_top1 == tp2_top1 == 271`, `draft_exact = 3/3`) on `sm_80`, `sm_86`, and `sm_89`.
    - **`greedy-diff` (`TP=1` & `TP=2`, `--verify-lossless --verify-radix-warm`)** passing `first_step=4/4 adjudicated=4/4 lossless=4/4 radix_warm=4/4` on both `Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B` (`FP16`) and `nchapman/Qwen3.5-4B-W4A16-HGPTQ` across `sm_80`, `sm_86`, and `sm_89`.
  - **Multi-Arch Parity & Tooling Fixes:**
    - Raised `SMALL_N_GEMM_DECODE_MAX_DIM` (`2560 -> 9216`) and `SMALL_N_GEMM_PLE_MAX_N` (`12288 -> 18432`) in `crates/warp34-host/src/model/weights.rs` so all transformer-block `DenseF16` projections in unquantized `Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B` (`TP=1` and `TP=2` `down_proj`, `o_proj`, `out_proj`, `gate_up_proj`) dispatch to the `M`-invariant `warp34_small_n_gemm_f16` kernel across `M <= 16` decode and speculative verify rounds, eliminating `M=1` vs `M=4` `cuBLAS` reduction-order tie flips on `sm_89`.
    - Recaptured `tools/oracle/goldens/4B/{chat,agentic}*.{ids,top5.json}` from `vLLM 0.31.0` (`dtype=float16`) against `Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B@851bf6e806efd8d0a36b00ddf55e13ccb7b8cd0a` to match `.mise.toml` `oracle-greedy`, and updated `GOLDEN_MOE_TEXT_RESIDUAL_MAX_LAST_REL_L2` (`0.22`) / `GOLDEN_MOE_TEXT_RESIDUAL_MIN_LAST_COSINE` (`0.975`) in `crates/warp34-host/src/cli/oracle.rs` for `nchapman/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W4A16-HGPTQ@184b46c573e43deb31519e79ae67b3a599404515`.
    - Added `discover_prebuilt_test_executables()` (`WARP34_PREBUILT_TESTS=1` or automatic fallback when `cargo` is absent) to `tools/check_cuda_sanitizer.py` so prebuilt `target/release/deps/` binaries run `compute-sanitizer` directly on minimal CUDA images.
    - Locked in the verified `sm_80`, `sm_86`, and `sm_89` throughput, TTFT speedup, speculative acceptance, and `B=8` scheduler baselines (including `4b_tp2_plain`, `4b_tp2_mtp_synth`, and `4b_tp2_dflash2_synth`) in `tools/oracle/goldens/bench_baselines.json`, `tools/check_bench_regressions.py`, and `tools/oracle/test_bench_regressions.py` (`mise run bench-gate` / `mise run bench-gate-full`).
