## Public Release Preparation: Apache-2.0 Licensing, Documentation Sync, Strict Lints, & Unit Coverage

- **Licensing & Package Metadata (`LICENSE`, `NOTICE`, `Cargo.toml`, `crates/*/Cargo.toml`, `Dockerfile`):**
  - Added root `Apache-2.0` `LICENSE` and third-party attribution `NOTICE` (`IST-DASLab/marlin`, `vLLM`, `vllm.cpp`, `SGLang`, `llama.cpp`, `Qwen`), copied both into the runtime stage of `Dockerfile`, and populated `license = "Apache-2.0"`, `repository`, `homepage`, `readme`, `keywords`, and `categories` across workspace and crate manifests.
- **Documentation Sync & Repository Pruning (`README.md`, `docs/`, `benchmarks/MODELS.md`, `.gitignore`):**
  - Synced `README.md`, `docs/{ROADMAP,SERVING,HARDWARE}.md`, and `benchmarks/MODELS.md` with current model families, CLI flags (`chat`, `--realistic-prompt`, `--no-webui`), and verified throughput tables; pruned 32 point-in-time audit/planning/intermediate markdown files and added `/outputs/` to `.gitignore`.
- **Idiomatic Rust Pass, Strict Workspace Lints, & Unit Coverage (`Cargo.toml`, `crates/warp34-host/`):**
  - Enabled `rust_2018_idioms` and 21 curated Clippy lints (`assigning_clones`, `bool_to_int_with_if`, `checked_conversions`, `cloned_instead_of_copied`, `dbg_macro`, `explicit_iter_loop`, `implicit_clone`, `inefficient_to_string`, `large_futures`, `manual_assert`, `manual_let_else`, `map_unwrap_or`, `needless_continue`, `redundant_closure`, `redundant_else`, `semicolon_if_nothing_returned`, `todo`, `trivially_copy_pass_by_ref`, `unimplemented`, `uninlined_format_args`, `use_self`) with zero warnings across `--all-targets` (CPU and CUDA).
  - Expanded `--lib` unit test coverage (`346` CUDA/host unit tests, `191` CPU unit tests) across `hub/progress.rs`, `scheduled_vision.rs`, `server/decision_http.rs`, `cli/{oracle,commands}.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`, `dflash.rs`, `decision/scoring.rs`, `model/sampling.rs`, and `engine_worker/runner.rs`.

## Context-Bounded Output Token Budgets & Speculative Boundary Fixes

- **Removed hardcoded `4,096` output-token ceiling & hardened coding-agent context/KV semantics (`crates/warp34-host/src/{loader/config.rs,server.rs,server/http.rs,responses_api.rs,anthropic_api.rs,engine_worker.rs,engine_worker/runner.rs}`):**
  - Raised the HTTP parser bound (`MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS`) to `1,048,576` tokens (`65,536` KV pages) across `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/completions`, `/v1/responses`, and `/v1/messages` (including Anthropic `thinking.budget_tokens >= 4096`), capped default `max_model_len` by checkpoint `max_position_embeddings` (warning on explicit override), and advertised `max_model_len` on every `GET /v1/models` entry.
  - Clamps explicit `max_completion_tokens` / `max_tokens` / `max_output_tokens` to `max_model_len - prompt_len` when `prompt_len < max_model_len` (so static agent budgets like Claude Code `32000` or OpenCode `8192` never HTTP 400 mid-session), resolves omitted output budgets on `/v1/chat/completions` and `/v1/responses` to `max_model_len - prompt_len` (`min(256, remaining)` on legacy `/v1/completions`), exempts token-count utilities (`/v1/messages/count_tokens`, `/v1/chat/completions/input_tokens`, `/v1/responses/input_tokens`) from generation context limits, and returns structured `context_length_exceeded` / `"prompt is too long: X tokens > Y maximum"` HTTP 400 payloads when `prompt_len >= max_model_len`.
- **Incremental KV slab allocation & speculative (`MTP` / `DFlash2`) batching (`crates/warp34-host/src/{proposer.rs,proposer/scheduled.rs,mtp.rs,dflash.rs,engine_worker.rs,engine_worker/runner.rs,model/generation.rs,model/rank.rs,model/execution.rs}`, `crates/warp34-host/src/cli/{options.rs,commands.rs}`, `dashboard/src/{App.tsx,Playground.tsx}`):**
  - Replaced full-`max_tokens` up-front KV pre-allocation with bounded `256`-token admission lookahead and two-pass incremental `128`-token (`8`-page) physical KV decode slabs (`min_pages` reserved across all active sequences before opportunistic `slab_pages` lookahead) so large/omitted output budgets preserve warm `MambaRadixCache` prefixes, sustain full multi-sequence concurrency, and finish cleanly with `"length"` (`hit_limit`) if physical KV exhausts mid-decode.
  - Switched `NativeProposer::fits_mtp_batch_slot_capacity` and `mtp_batch_pages_needed` from `prompt_len + max_new_tokens` to current sequence length (`current_len + k + 2 <= slot_cap`) with clean boundary yield in `extend_or_yield_mtp_batch`, keeping concurrent omitted-budget and `max_tokens: 32000` coding-agent bursts on the `B=2..16` batched MTP fast path.
  - Relaxed `Qwen3_5MtpRank::batch_slot_count()` (`mtp.rs`) to expose `8` GPU MTP batch slots whenever `all_pages >= 512` (`64` pages / `1,024` tokens per slot) instead of requiring `>= 4,096` pages, and updated `step_scheduled_batched_mtp_verify_round` (`proposer/scheduled.rs`) so `B in 3..=4` uses `k_cap = min(k, 2)` regardless of `max_rows > 256` and `B >= 5` uses `k_cap = min(k, 2)` on `TP > 1` / MoE models (`MAX_BATCHED_SPEC_TOKENS = 48`), boosting `--kv-pages 512` `B=8` `MTP-3` scheduler throughput on `2× RTX 3090 Ti` from **`947.0 -> 1,353.6 tok/s` (`+42.9%`)** on `4B` (`TP=1`), **`309.3 -> 420.0 tok/s` (`+35.8%`)** on `27B` (`TP=2`), and **`799.3 -> 1,043.8 tok/s` (`+30.6%`)** on `35B-A3B` (`TP=2`).
  - Suppressed false-positive serving GDN state-pool clamp warnings on tiered-MoE checkpoints (`rank.rs` comparing `total_state_slots < effective_requested_state_slots`) and added `RankTieredMoe` GPU LRU sync/initial-slot restoration in `Qwen3_5Engine::reset_all_states()` (`model/execution.rs`) so multi-stage benchmarks start each stage from a clean hot-expert state.
  - Updated `ServeCliConfig::effective_kv_pages` (`2,048` pages / `32,768` tokens for `serve --speculative`), `run_chat` (`1,024` pages / `16,384` tokens default in both plain and speculative modes), CLI `--speculative dflash2` pre-download capacity validation (`<= 262,144` tokens / `16,384` pages), startup diagnostics when serving GDN state slots are clamped or pinned host MoE storage falls back to pageable heap, slow-consumer final-step cancellation handling, and Dashboard `Playground` (`auto` `max_tokens` placeholder, `32 MiB` payload/image guard, and `600s` non-streaming header timeout).
- **Apples-to-Apples Benchmark Modes (`Plain` vs `MTP-3`/`DFlash2`, `synthetic` vs `realistic` prompts) & Automated Regression Gate (`crates/warp34-host/src/cli/{options.rs,commands.rs,oracle.rs,tests.rs}`, `tools/check_bench_regressions.py`, `tools/oracle/goldens/bench_baselines.json`, `tools/oracle/test_bench_regressions.py`, `.mise.toml`):**
  - Added `--realistic-prompt` / `--realistic` (non-repeating 120-token ChatML coding prompt measuring natural-text speculative acceptance alongside the default periodic synthetic seed) plus `--prompt` / `-p`, `--prompt-file` / `-f`, and `--out-json` / `-o` aliases to `warp34 bench` and `warp34 profile-diff`, recording `"prompt_source": "synthetic" | "realistic" | "custom"` in the decode JSON summary.
  - Added `tools/check_bench_regressions.py` + `tools/oracle/goldens/bench_baselines.json` (`mise run bench-gate` quick 4B suite and `mise run bench-gate-full` 4B/9B+LoRA/27B/35B-A3B/Flash-Next suite), validating hardware-keyed (`sm_80`, `sm_86`, `sm_89`) throughput/speedup/acceptance floors and hard correctness invariants (`cuda_graph == true`, `radix.lossless_parity == true`, `vision.lossless_parity == true`, `vision.content_hash_isolated == true`) and rendering a Markdown comparison table that separates `Plain` vs `MTP-3`/`DFlash2` and `synthetic` vs `realistic` prompts (`5/5` passing live on `RTX 3090 Ti`: `4B` Plain `208.5 tok/s` / `1,173.5 tok/s` `B=8` sched, `MTP-3` synthetic `589.8 tok/s` (`accept=2.97`) / `1,350.1 tok/s` `B=8` sched vs `MTP-3` realistic `393.8 tok/s` (`accept=1.65`), and `DFlash2` synthetic `1,576.3 tok/s` (`accept=14.88`) vs `DFlash2` realistic `235.3 tok/s` (`accept=1.35`)).

## HTTP Exposure Policy & Optional Web UI

- **Deployment contract:** Kept loopback (`127.0.0.1`) and no API key as defaults. `/metrics`, `/api/metrics`, minimal `/health`, and enabled Web UI pages/assets remain unauthenticated; binding plus firewall/Tailscale/proxy rules control their reachability. Configured API keys still protect all inference, model-list, tokenization/count, decision, stored-Responses routes, and aliases, with existing Responses ownership checks unchanged.
- **`--no-webui`:** Added a serve-only boolean flag and `ServeOptions.webui` (default `true`). Disabled UI returns 404 for pages, favicon, and bundled assets without disabling either metrics endpoint or changing API authentication. Help, `docs/SERVING.md`, dashboard guidance, and post-bind startup notices explain the distinction; non-loopback listeners warn even with an API key configured.
- **Public health:** Worker panic readiness now returns only `{"status":"error"}` with HTTP 503; detailed diagnostics stay in server logs. Regression tests first reproduced the leaked panic detail and the previously ignored disabled-UI configuration before implementation.
- **Verification:** Dashboard checks/build and **44/44** frontend tests pass; focused CUDA-feature tests pass (**326** host library tests, **4** kernel library tests, and **3** serving-options integration tests). Full `mise run check` passed, including release tests and **117/117** CUDA sanitizer runs. Scoped review found no task-related issues.

## Dashboard: shadcn Composition Audit & Recharts Telemetry

- **Shared presentation:** Migrated Overview/Connect/Playground surfaces to shadcn `Card`, notices to `Alert`, status chips to `Badge`, and form composition to `Field`/labels/descriptions/errors. Preserved real headings and named regions, separated the preset description from its accessible name, and replaced non-control code captions with associated text. Removed about 400 lines of superseded CSS and redundant token aliases; native selects, scrolling, and reasoning disclosures remain intentional.
- **Telemetry charts (`dashboard/src/{Chart.tsx,LazyChart.tsx}`):** Replaced bespoke SVG path/area generation with shadcn/Recharts linear charts, keyboard exploration, and units/engine-time tooltips. Retained numeric five-minute windows, zeros, explicit nulls, gaps over eight seconds, collecting states, and truthful latest-observation summaries. The locally owned chart wrapper uses CSS custom properties instead of generated inline stylesheets, preserving the server CSP.
- **Bundle isolation and resilience:** Recharts loads only with Overview (about **104 KB gzip**); Connect/Playground retain the approximately **130 KB gzip** main bundle without downloading charts. A chart-local error boundary preserves navigation and the rest of the dashboard if the lazy chunk fails; its regression test first reproduced the unhandled failure. The four Geist font files remain **49,488 bytes**.
- **Verification:** Final frontend typecheck/format/build and **44/44** tests pass; full `mise run check` passed again after review fixes, including release tests and **117/117** CUDA sanitizer runs. Chrome checks under the production CSP cover all routes at 390/680/768/1050/1440px, chart keyboard tooltips, JSON validation, upload focus, mocked SSE, and graceful chart-download failure. Actual rendered fonts are Geist Medium/SemiBold (with Geist Mono for endpoint identifiers). Follow-up review has no blockers; its breakpoint suggestion was disproved by the generated exclusive media queries and browser checks at 1050/1051/1052px.

## Dashboard: shadcn Neutral Dark Controls & Self-Hosted Geist

- **Design system (`dashboard/`):** Adopted generated shadcn/ui Radix components and Tailwind v4 for buttons, inputs, textareas, native selects, switches, badges, and tabs. Replaced violet-tinted surfaces/glows with the standard neutral dark token palette, reserving bright violet/cyan for brand and telemetry signals. Kept custom chart/layout CSS in the base layer; added component/theme guidance in `dashboard/README.md`.
- **Control behavior:** Added roving keyboard tabs and associated panels in Connect and Playground, Lucide action/navigation icons, request-editor locking while requests run, stable Send/Abort focus, accessible upload controls, JSON error associations, and explicit clipboard-failure feedback. Native selectors retain browser/mobile keyboard behavior; developer API proxying now covers all Playground endpoints.
- **Typography (`dashboard/src/fonts.css`, `crates/warp34-host/src/dashboard.rs`):** Self-hosted Geist Sans 400/500/600 and Geist Mono 400, Latin-only WOFF2, `font-display: swap`, no unused weights/italics or external requests. Four font assets total **49,488 bytes (48.3 KiB)**; embedded serving adds `font/woff2` with immutable caching and a regression test enforcing a 52,000-byte budget.
- **Verification:** Dashboard typecheck/format/build and **37/37** Vitest tests pass; **2/2** CUDA-feature embedded-dashboard tests pass (font test first reproduced a 404 before adding WOFF2 serving). Chrome checks cover all three routes at 390/768/1440px, keyboard tab navigation, four loaded font faces, and a mocked SSE request with no page overflow or browser errors. Two scoped reviews report no actionable findings. Full `mise run check` passed, including release tests and **117/117** CUDA sanitizer runs; final `mise run check-quick`, frontend checks, and focused embedded-asset tests also passed after the font changes.

## Model Startup, Safetensors Archive, Pipelined GPU Upload, & Tiered-MoE Initialization (`1.5–1.9×` Faster Startup)

- **Warm-Cache HuggingFace Hub Fast Path & Transient Outage Fallback (`crates/warp34-host/src/hub.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/hub/tests.rs`):**
  - Skips redundant `snapshot_download()` tree walks on Target, Draft, and LoRA repositories when a mutable ref (`main`, tag, or branch) resolves to a commit snapshot already verified on disk, and skips `repo.info()` network round-trips altogether when `--revision` is an immutable 40-char commit SHA with a verified local snapshot manifest.
  - Automatically falls back to `validate_offline_snapshot` / verified cached LoRA snapshots (logging a brief warning) on transient network/timeout/rate-limit/HTTP 5xx failures while keeping explicit 401/403/404 and incomplete-snapshot checks fail-closed.
- **Streaming Index Parser, Parallel Shard Headers, & Single-Pass/LUT Tensor Reads (`crates/warp34-host/src/loader.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/loader/{quant.rs,config_rules.rs,tests.rs}`):**
  - Replaced `serde_json::from_str::<IndexJson>` (`HashMap<String, String>` over up to `75,000+` `weight_map` entries) with a zero-allocation `UniqueShardValues` `serde::de::Visitor`, parallelized Safetensors shard header `pread` + JSON deserialization across shards, and precomputed an O(1) `ple_shards` exclusion set so `drop_page_cache()` never linearly rescans tensor names.
  - Replaced full-tensor `Vec<u8>` buffer allocations in `read_f16_bits`, `read_f16_bits_subslice`, and `read_u32_subslice` with L2-blocked (`2 MiB`) single-pass `BF16 -> FP16` conversion and direct `pread` into `Vec<u16>`/`Vec<u32>`, added `read_exact_info_raw` (`libc::pread` directly into uninitialized pinned pointers), added `QuantTransformSet::has_transforms()` fast bypass, and replaced per-element float math in `dequant_u2b2/u4b8/u8b128_n_major_to_f16` with bit-identical per-group FP16 lookup tables (`4`/`16`/`256` entries).
- **Stream-Ordered Arenas, Bump-Packed `PinnedUploadRing`, Zero-Clone Sharding, & Overlapped Init (`crates/warp34-host/src/cuda.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/{mtp.rs,dflash.rs}`, `crates/warp34-host/src/model/{weights.rs,weights/hgptq.rs,weights/moe.rs,rank.rs,rank_capacity.rs,engine.rs}`):**
  - Added `DeviceBuffer::alloc_on_stream` / `DeviceArena::new_on_stream` (`cudaMemsetAsync` on rank stream instead of synchronous `cudaMemset`) and `MappedHostBuffer::alloc_unzeroed` (`cudaHostAllocMapped` without redundant host `write_bytes` zeroing before overwrite).
  - Replaced per-tensor pageable `upload_slice` and per-matrix `DeviceBuffer::alloc` + `stream.synchronize()` Marlin repacks across target, `MTP`, resident MoE, and `DFlash2` loaders with a 2-slot 64 MiB `PinnedUploadRing` using 256B-aligned bump packing (`host_used` / `repack_used`) so `cudaEventSynchronize` fires once per 64 MiB slab rather than per tensor.
  - Eliminated `TP=1` full-matrix clones (`into_slice_cols`), added `load_row_shard` (`read_f16_bits_subslice` / `read_u32_subslice`) so `TP >= 2` reads only `1/TP` of row-sharded `Q/K/V/gate/up/embed/lm_head` tensors from disk, collapsed resident MoE loading into a single parallel thread scope per layer, pipelined layer shard reads 1 layer ahead via `sync_channel(1)`, and overlapped `Tokenizer::from_file` + `populate_shared_vocab` (`embed_tokens`) with GPU kernel warmup (`prepare_execution_plan` + `Tp2Collectives`).
- **Tiered-MoE Unzeroed Pinned Slabs & Direct `pread` (`crates/warp34-host/src/moe_offload/{host_store.rs,engine.rs}`):**
  - Replaced `48` per-layer `cudaHostAlloc` calls + pageable staging `Vec`s in `HostExpertStore` with synchronous multi-layer `PinnedSlabPool` (`HostPackStorage::MappedSlice` over `SharedPinnedSlab` via `alloc_unzeroed`) and direct `read_exact_info_raw` (`libc::pread`) into pinned expert packs with explicit tail padding zeroing before slice exposure.
  - Parallelized Hadamard rule validation, switched `HotExpertCache` initialization to stream-ordered `new_on_stream` + `populate_initial_async`, deferred `moe_cpu_bridge::Runtime::new` (`moe-responder` Core 0 busy-poller) until after all 48 layers finish loading, and consolidated `archive.drop_page_cache()` to a single end-of-load pass.
- **Verified Model Startup Wall Times (`yeti`: Ryzen 9 7950X3D, `2× RTX 3090 Ti`, `warp34 generate ... -p "Hello" -n 1 --temp 0 --kv-pages 256`, 100% greedy token parity across `TP=1`/`TP=2`/`MTP`/`DFlash2`):**
  - **`nchapman/Qwen3.5-4B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TP=1`):** `--offline` `2.10–2.51s ->` **`1.458s`**; online warm Hub cache `2.83–3.82s ->` **`1.600s`**; `+MTP` `2.84s ->` **`1.567s`**; `+DFlash2` (`nchapman/Qwen3.5-4B-DFlash2`) **`2.129s`** (`2.534s` cold shard cache).
  - **`Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B` (`BF16`, `TP=1 --offline`):** `4.92–6.53s ->` **`2.479s`** (`2.0–2.6×` faster).
  - **`nchapman/Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`--offline`):** `TP=1` `9.50s ->` **`6.149s`**; `TP=2` `9.74s ->` **`5.815s`** (`1.68×` faster); `TP=2 + DFlash2-W4A16` **`6.877s`**.
  - **`nchapman/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TP=2 --offline`, 128 resident experts):** `7.76–10.09s ->` **`5.426s`** (`1.43–1.86×` faster).
  - **`nchapman/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`--offline`, 512 tiered experts, `~46 GiB` pinned packs):** `TP=1` `53.69–62.86s ->` **`34.439s`** (`1.56–1.83×` faster, `user=7.14s`); `TP=2` **`36.642s`**.

## Mixed W2/W4 HGPTQ-RCO Tiered-MoE Hot Cache Sizing & `warp34` Naming Standardization

- **Mixed W2/W4 HGPTQ-RCO Hot Expert Cache Planner (`crates/warp34-host/src/model/rank_capacity.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/model/tests.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/tests/qwen4_flash_next.rs`):**
  - Updated `plan_tiered_moe_hot_per_layer_with_dflash` to divide `(available_vram_after_weights_and_scratch - fixed_overhead)` and compute `full_residency_need` using `actual_per_hot_step` (summing per-layer `layer_moe_expert_pack_layout(l)` strides across `W2B2` GDN layers and `W4B8` QSA layers, e.g. `61.875–63.95 MiB/hot step` on `nchapman/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W2.6A16-HGPTQ-RCO`) instead of the pessimistic upper-bound `per_hot_step` (`48 × 2,036 KiB = 95.625 MiB/hot step` pure-W4).
  - Raised `TIERED_MOE_TP2_DEFAULT_HOT` from `128` to `160` (`320 / 512 = 62.5%` hot experts across 2 GPUs), allowing `TP=2` on 2× 24 GB GPUs at `8,192` `TQ4` KV pages (`128K` context) + `100%` VRAM-resident `MTP-3` to allocate `160` hot experts/layer/rank with `~2.45 GiB` post-pool headroom instead of stepping down to `112` and leaving `~5.5 GiB/card` idle.
- **`warp34` Repository & Casing Standardization (`crates/warp34-kernels/`, `crates/warp34-host/`, `benchmarks/perf/warp34perf/`, `dashboard/`):**
  - Standardized crate names (`warp34-kernels`, `warp34-host`), CLI binary (`warp34`), FFI header/entrypoints (`warp34_cuda.h`, `warp34_*`), environment variables/constants (`WARP34_*`), and PascalCase type/struct names (`Warp34*`), and pushed to `git@github.com:nchapman/warp34.git`.

## Embedded Web Console API Playground & Multi-Turn Wire Workbench (`dashboard/`, `crates/warp34-host/src/dashboard.rs`)

- **Interactive API Playground (`dashboard/src/{playground.ts,Playground.tsx,Playground.test.tsx}`, `dashboard/src/{App.tsx,style.css}`, `crates/warp34-host/src/dashboard.rs`):**
  - Added a routed `Playground` workbench (`react-router` `/playground`, `/playground/{*path}`, `/connect`, and `?endpoint=&preset=` query state) to the compile-time embedded React console (`Overview` | `Playground` | `Connect`).
  - Covers all 13 HTTP endpoints (`POST /v1/chat/completions`, `POST /v1/responses`, `POST /v1/messages`, `POST /v1/completions`, `POST /v1/decisions`, `POST /v1/systemone`, `POST /tokenize`, `POST /detokenize`, `POST /v1/chat/completions/input_tokens`, `POST /v1/responses/input_tokens`, `POST /v1/messages/count_tokens`, `GET /v1/models`, `GET /health`) with a sticky `276px` left endpoint sidebar on wide viewports (`> 1050px`) and a compact dropdown on narrow viewports.
  - Supports two-way synchronized `Messages & Params` $\leftrightarrow$ `JSON Body` editing, client-side image upload (`+ Image`, drag-and-drop, clipboard paste, automatic WebP/GIF/BMP $\to$ PNG `<canvas>` transcoding, thumbnail previews under `img-src 'self' data: blob:`, and native `image_url` / `input_image` / Anthropic `base64` serialization), multi-turn `Append & Send` continuation (preserving `tool_calls` + `tool_result` / `function_call_output`, `reasoning_content`, and stateful `/v1/responses` `previous_response_id`), incremental SSE frame inspection, and `Copy cURL`.
  - **Ultraviolet Warp Theme & 3-Lane Brand Mark (`dashboard/src/{tokens.css,style.css,Chart.tsx,App.tsx,Playground.tsx,playground.ts}`, `dashboard/{index.html,public/favicon.svg}`):** Replaced the warm-espresso palette and hardcoded surface tints with a semantic ultraviolet-dark token system (`#08060f` obsidian-violet canvas, `#100c1e` panels, `#c471ff` hyper-ultraviolet primary accent, `#00f0d0` laser-aqua secondary signal, `#634fb0` indigo cached pool bars, and `#ff5c8a` plasma-rose alerts), upgraded the `warp34` header mark and `favicon.svg` to a 3-lane accelerating ultraviolet slash glyph (`///`), replaced raw Unicode `◇`/`◷` characters with crisp inline vector SVGs, scaled typography (`14px` base, readable labels/pills, 2-column `Connect` layout), and added gap-preserving SVG area gradients (`chartAreaPath`) beneath live metric traces.
  - **Coding Agent & SDK Integration Guide on `Connect` (`dashboard/src/{App.tsx,App.test.tsx,metrics.ts,style.css}`, `crates/warp34-host/src/{telemetry.rs,engine_worker.rs}`):** Upgraded the `Connect` tab with live-interpolated copy-paste setup snippets for **Claude Code** (`ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` without `/v1`, `ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN`, `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` clamped within `warp34`'s `1..=4096` output ceiling, `ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_SONNET/OPUS/HAIKU_MODEL`, and `~/.claude/settings.json`), **OpenAI Codex CLI** (`~/.codex/config.toml` with `wire_api = "responses"`, `base_url = ".../v1"`, and live `model_context_window` from `config.max_model_len`), **OpenCode** (`opencode.json` with `@ai-sdk/openai-compatible` and live `limit: { context, output }`), **Pi Agent** (`~/.pi/agent/models.json`), **Aider / Continue / Cline**, and **Python & cURL** (`OpenAI` + `Anthropic` SDKs).
- **CLI Ergonomics, Token-Based Context Sizing, & Interactive `chat` REPL (`crates/warp34-host/src/cli/{options.rs,commands.rs,tests.rs}`, `docs/SERVING.md`):**
  - Added human-friendly token count parsing (`parse_token_count`: `4k`, `16k`, `32k`, `64k`, `128k`, `256k`, `1m`, or `32_768`) and token-based KV pool sizing (`-c` / `--ctx-size` / `--context-length` / `--kv-tokens`) so users never need manual 16-token KV page math.
  - Added `ServeCliConfig::effective_kv_pages` so `--max-model-len 64k` or `--speculative mtp --max-model-len 16k` auto-expands the target KV pool when `--ctx-size`/`--kv-pages` is omitted, and validates explicit `--max-model-len <= kv_tokens` before Hub downloads or CUDA initialization.
  - Added positional `<model>` (`warp34 serve <model>`, `warp34 chat <model>`, `warp34 generate <model>`, `warp34 bench <model>`), industry-standard `vLLM`/`SGLang`/`llama.cpp` flag aliases (`-hf`/`--hf-repo`, `--alias`/`-a`, `--max-running-requests`/`--parallel`/`-np`, `--kv-cache-dtype auto`, `--cache-type-k`, `--tp auto`, `--speculative-algorithm`, `--num-speculative-tokens`/`--draft-tokens`, `--model-draft`/`-md`, `--max-new-tokens`, `-f`/`--prompt-file`, `--system`), CLI sampling flags (`--temp`, `--top-p`, `--top-k`, `--min-p`, `--seed`, `--repetition-penalty`, `--presence-penalty`, `--frequency-penalty`), and an interactive multi-turn `warp34 chat` REPL with `MambaRadixCache` prefix reuse and slash commands (`/help`, `/clear`, `/system`, `/think`, `/temp`, `/stats`, `/exit`).

## Full 1–2× NVIDIA A100 (`sm_80`) Support, NVLink P2P Collectives, & 80 GiB Full-Residency Tiered MoE

- **`sm_80` Architecture Support & Static Binary Gates (`crates/warp34-kernels/build.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/cuda.rs`, `tools/check_cuda_binaries.py`, `tools/oracle/test_cuda_gates.py`, `Dockerfile`, `tests/dflash_proposer.rs`):**
  - Added `sm_80` to the default multi-architecture fatbinary (`WARP34_ARCH="sm_80,sm_86,sm_89"` + PTX), added `CudaArchFamily::AmpereSm80` (`(8, 0)`), and updated `tools/check_cuda_binaries.py` (`expected_arches = ("sm_80", "sm_86", "sm_89")`, `1,581` kernel entries = `527` per arch, `LOCAL == 0`, static `SHARED <= 48 KiB`, `zero_stack = 1,357 / 1,581`).
  - Relaxed cross-architecture `K = 9216` cuBLAS BF16 Split-K `relL2` tolerance in `dflash_proposer::real_shape_layer0_gemms_match_actual_sglang` from `3.0e-3` to `3.5e-3` on A100 (`0.00321` on 108-SM A100 vs 84-SM 3090 Ti provenance; `actual_sglang_full_model_hidden_parity` passes unmodified).
- **NVLink P2P Collective Fast-Path (`crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_collectives.cu`):**
  - Added `query_p2p_device_stage(stage0, &is_p2p)` (`cudaPointerGetAttributes` `type == cudaMemoryTypeDevice`) and `kRankP2pFlag` so `TP=2` collectives on NVLink P2P VRAM (`CommBuffer::DeviceVram`, e.g. `NV12` on A100-SXM4) bypass `>= 64 KiB` 2-barrier `cudaMemcpyAsync` DMA TX (`dma_tx = false`), disable 3/5-stage PCIe software-pipelined splitting (`num_stages = 1`), and scale up to `kSmallMaxBlocks = 36` CTAs (`tp2_comm_block_cap(true)`) while preserving the 16-CTA / 2-barrier DMA path for PCIe `CommBuffer::MappedHost` (`allow_p2p = false`).
- **High-VRAM (`40 GiB / 80 GiB`) 100%-Resident Tiered MoE (`crates/warp34-host/src/model/rank_capacity.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/moe_offload/engine.rs`):**
  - Updated `plan_tiered_moe_hot_per_layer_with_dflash` and `plan_mtp_tiered_moe_hot_experts` so that when `>= 4 GiB` post-overhead slack remains after fitting all `local_experts` (`512` on `TP=1`, `256` on `TP=2`), `k_hot` promotes to `local_experts` (100% GPU VRAM residency, releasing host expert storage) instead of clamping at 24 GiB consumer ceilings (`96`/`104` on `TP=1`, `128` on `TP=2`).
  - Added an early `cache.slots.capacity >= cache.expert_ids.len()` fast-path in `TieredMoeEngine::execute_layer` (`engine.rs`) so multi-token prefill/verify batches also execute directly on the GPU without host LRU bookkeeping.
- **100% Verification, Sanitizer Gate, & Live Benchmarks on `2× NVIDIA A100-SXM4-80GB` (`sm_80`, `108` SMs, `40 MiB` L2, NVLink `NV12` P2P):**
  - **Verification & Sanitizer Gates:** `check_cuda_binaries.py` (`1,581` entries: `sm_80=527, sm_86=527, sm_89=527`, `local=0`, `static_shared<=48KiB`), `314/314` `warp34-host` + `4/4` `warp34-kernels` lib unit tests, **36/36** integration test targets (`--tests`), and **117/117** `compute-sanitizer` runs (`memcheck` + `racecheck` + `synccheck` across GPUs `0,1`, `0` errors/hazards).
  - **Live `warp34 bench` Throughput (`1–2× A100-SXM4-80GB`, `512 -> 128` tokens):**
    - **`Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B` (`FP16`):** `TP=1` **`146.1 tok/s`** plain / **`428.9 tok/s` `MTP-3`** / **`973.0 tok/s` `DFlash2`** (`1.03 ms` ITL); `TP=2` **`215.4 tok/s`** plain / **`586.4 tok/s` `MTP-3`** / **`1,016.1 tok/s` `DFlash2`** (**`0.984 ms` ITL**).
    - **`nchapman/Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ`:** `TP=1` **`63.5 tok/s`** plain (`3,497 tok/s` prefill) / **`150.0 tok/s` `MTP-3`** / **`199.3 tok/s` `DFlash2`**; `TP=2` **`83.1 tok/s`** plain (**`5,960 tok/s` prefill**, `+8.6%` from NVLink P2P collective fast-path) / **`200.8 tok/s` `MTP-3`** / **`241.3 tok/s` `DFlash2`** (`4.14 ms` ITL, `16.98 ms/round`).
    - **`nchapman/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W4A16-HGPTQ`:** `TP=1` **`191.6 tok/s`** plain (`8,009 tok/s` prefill) / **`351.1 tok/s` `MTP-3`**; `TP=2` **`221.7 tok/s`** plain (**`10,098 tok/s` prefill**, `50.7 ms` TTFT) / **`479.6 tok/s` `MTP-3`** (`2.09 ms` ITL, `8.02 ms/round`).
    - **`nchapman/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`512` experts, `top_k=10`, 100% VRAM-resident MoE vs 24 GiB ceiling baseline):**
      - `TP=1` (`k_hot: 104 -> 512`): prefill **`733 -> 2,837 tok/s` (`3.87×`, TTFT `698.6 -> 180.5 ms`)**, plain decode **`26.5 -> 82.1 tok/s` (`3.09×`)**, `MTP-3` **`31.6 -> 137.1 tok/s` (`4.34×`, verify `93.5 -> 22.1 ms/round`)**, `DFlash2` **`32.8 -> 112.0 tok/s` (`3.42×`)**.
      - `TP=2` (`k_hot: 128 -> 256` + NVLink P2P collectives): prefill **`1,628 -> 4,495 tok/s` (`2.76×`, TTFT `314.5 -> 113.9 ms`)**, plain decode **`67.6 -> 88.0 tok/s` (`+30.1%`)**, `MTP-3` **`111.6 -> 165.6 tok/s` (`+48.3%`, verify `27.75 -> 17.84 ms/round`)**, `DFlash2` **`84.5 -> 138.5 tok/s` (`+63.9%`, verify `34.95 -> 22.93 ms/round`)**.

## Benchmark Checkpoint Correction & Optimal `vLLM 0.31.0` `cyankiwi` AWQ Head-to-Head (`Open-SWE-Traces`)

- **Policy:** [Benchmark checkpoint policy](../benchmarks/MODELS.md) selects official-base `cyankiwi` AWQ for `vLLM`/`SGLang` (`cyankiwi/Qwen3.8-27B-AWQ-INT4@e3a700906e6a73ba4290b1873dc2ba8198527ab5`, compressed-tensors; `cyankiwi/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-AWQ-4bit@f1e8415c4f29508fe281da4e256df26573213bfd`, AWQ GEMM; both asymmetric INT4 group-32). Quantized `warp34` target profiles use our `nchapman/*-HGPTQ*` checkpoints (`nchapman/Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ@ae51987e3339a2a70070702548571e10fe8bcbc2`, `nchapman/Qwen3.8-27B-DFlash2-W4A16-HGPTQ@46b87ea125d24924affa8da9c3e981a937aab216`, `nchapman/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W4A16-HGPTQ@4f5de21c886d79e17af5b8a4bfc625e226703fb6`). Unquantized correctness oracles and historical receipts remain unchanged.
- **Empirical `vLLM 0.31.0` Optimization on Hybrid GDN+Attention Models (`benchmarks/perf/campaigns/{27b,35b}-swe-multiclient.toml`):**
  - Identified and resolved four `vLLM 0.31.0` configuration bottlenecks on `Qwen3_5` / `Qwen3_5Moe`:
    1. **`--dtype bfloat16`**: Activates `GDN decode kernel: cuda` (`qwen_gdn_attention_core_fused_norm_packed` + `torch.ops._C.fused_gdn_decode_post_conv_mtp`), whereas `--dtype half` forced `GDN decode kernel: triton`.
    2. **`--max-cudagraph-capture-size 64` + `--performance-mode interactivity` + `--max-num-batched-tokens 4096`**: Replaces `--cudagraph-capture-sizes 1 2 4 8` (which left `6/8` FULL decode graphs uncaptured under MTP-3 because `query_len=4` needs `num_tokens = 4..32`), capturing `8/8` FULL decode graphs (`1..8` active seqs) and `36/36` PIECEWISE graphs (`1..64` tokens).
    3. **`--prefix-match-unit 16 --enable-mamba-shared-prefix-checkpoint`**: Sets `hash_block_size = 16` instead of `block_size` (`1,056` tokens on 35B, `784`/`800` tokens on 27B) and enables Mamba prefix checkpoints, raising `vLLM` multi-turn prefix cache hit rate from `63.2%` (`MTP`) / `79.9%` (`plain`) to **`88.3–93.9%`**.
    4. **`"disable_eagle_block_drop": true`** inside `--speculative-config '{"method":"mtp","num_speculative_tokens":3,"disable_eagle_block_drop":true}'`: Prevents Eagle/MTP from dropping the trailing prefix-cache block.
  - Together, these flags boosted `vLLM 0.31.0` `TP=2` throughput on `Open-SWE-Traces` (`swe-c4-shared` / `swe-c8-burst`) from `214.07 / 245.58 tok/s` to **`319.49 / 458.46 tok/s` (`+49.2% / +86.7%`)** on `35B` and from `84.88 / 82.65 tok/s` to **`112.36 / 154.52 tok/s` (`+32.4% / +87.0%`)** on `27B` (`/tmp/tune_vllm_cyankiwi/results.json`, `/tmp/tune_vllm_round2/results.json`).
- **Verification:** `mise run test-prep-all`, `mise run perf-check` (132 tests plus manifest validation), `mise run quality-check` (19 tests), and full `mise run check` passed, including release CUDA tests and **117/117** compute-sanitizer runs.

## Multi-Agent Batching, `BatchedMtpProposeIo` (`B=2..=8`) CUDA Graphs, & `MambaRadixCache` Prefix-Cache Scaling

- **Batched Multi-Slot MTP Ingest+Propose & `B=2..=8` Speculative Verify CUDA Graphs (`mtp.rs`, `mtp/batched.rs`, `proposer.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`, `model/graph_io.rs`, `model/graphs.rs`, `model/batch.rs`, `engine_worker/runner.rs`):**
  - Fixed `try_batched_spec_verify_graph` (`model/batch.rs`), which previously gated target CUDA Graph capture/replay on `total_q <= speculative_logits_rows` (`4` when MTP is attached), silently disabling target verify graphs on 100% of `B >= 2` rounds; replaced with `total_q <= max_tap_rows`, scaled `BatchedSpecVerifyGraphIo` to `MAX_BATCHED_SPEC_REQS = 8`, `MAX_BATCHED_SPEC_TOKENS = 48`, and `MAX_BATCHED_SPEC_CACHED_GRAPHS = 24`, and raised `step_scheduled_batched_mtp_verify_round` `max_verify_rows` (`18 -> 48`) with uniform `k_cap = min(k, 2)` across `B = 3..=8` on both dense and MoE checkpoints.
  - Added `BatchedMtpProposeIo` + `ingest_kv_only_multi_slot_no_sync` (`mtp/batched.rs`) and `NativeProposer::try_ingest_and_propose_mtp_batch` (`proposer.rs`), collapsing `B` sequential `M=1` `draft_lm_head` + dense/MoE MTP passes (`48` `TP=2` collectives and `B` host stream syncs at `B=8, k=3`) into a single `B`-row batched KV ingest + captured `(B, k, long_ctx)` CUDA Graph (`6` `TP=2` collectives and `1` stream sync, overlapped with `memo_pinned_staging` async D2H and zero `Vec<i32>` page-table clones).
  - Relaxed `is_batched_mtp_eligible` (`engine_worker/runner.rs`) so stochastic (`temperature > 0`, `top_p`/`top_k`, penalties) and pre-validated constrained/tool requests stay in batched speculative verify instead of falling back to plain `M=1` decode, added `1:1` multi-chunk prefill/decode interleaving (`interleave_decode_next`) so long newcomer prefills do not starve active decode peers, and added partial-LCP GPU MTP KV slot reuse (`prepare_mtp_partial_lcp_restore`, `>= 16` common prefix tokens) so diverged/evicted multi-turn sessions skip re-ingesting shared system/history prefixes.
  - **Multi-Session Overload (`swe-c12-overload`) & `DFlash2` Ring-Buffer Fixes (`dflash.rs`, `mtp.rs`, `proposer.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`, `radix.rs`, `scheduler.rs`, `engine_worker/decode.rs`, `engine_worker/runner.rs`):**
    1. **16-Slot `DFlash2` Ring Buffer & `--max-model-len 16384` Fix (`dflash.rs`):** Fixed `phys_kv_pages = logical_pages.min(...)` clamping `phys_kv_pages` to `1,025` (`128` pages/slot = `2,048` tokens <= `window + block_size = 2,056`) whenever `--max-model-len 16384` was passed, which disabled the `DFlash2` ring buffer (`ring_pages_dev_buf = None`), shrank `batch_slot_count()` to `4`, and forced turns past `4,088` tokens into plain `M=1` decode. Enabled `slot_count * ring_phys_per_slot` whenever `cfg.window > 0 && logical_pages >= ring_phys_per_slot` and scaled `MAX_DFLASH_BATCH_SLOTS` / `MAX_MTP_BATCH_SLOTS` (`MAX_PROPOSER_BATCH_SLOTS`) from `8` to `16` (`2,304` `DFlash2` ring pages = `16 × 144` pages/slot, and `262,144` `TP=2` `MTP` KV tokens = `16 × 16,384` tokens/slot) so up to `16` rotating agent sessions retain warm GPU draft KV slots without eviction.
    2. **Non-Destructive `PrefixMemo` Sibling Restore (`proposer.rs`):** Stopped `NativeProposer::restore` from truncating a longer sibling session's `PrefixMemo` in place when a lagging sibling matched an intermediate checkpoint (`memo.tokens.len().saturating_sub(n) > 256`), while keeping `O(1)` in-place reuse when `memo.tokens.len().saturating_sub(n) <= 256` (avoiding `20–50 MiB` `Vec<u8>` page-fault stalls).
    3. **Ancestor Checkpoint Fallback (`scheduler.rs`):** Updated `SchedulerV1::admit_waiting_with_filter` to walk up ancestor checkpoints in `MambaRadixCache` when `allow_prefix_hit` rejects the deepest radix match instead of resetting `matched_tokens` to `0`.
    4. **Locked Mamba Slot Recycling & Superseded Internal Cleanup (`radix.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`, `engine_worker/decode.rs`):** Added `can_recycle_locked_mamba`, `reclaim_internal_mamba`, `is_superseded_linear_internal`, and ancestor leaf walks so advancing sessions recycle their own locked previous-turn Mamba slot in place when the GDN state pool is full.
- **Verified Multi-Agent `Open-SWE-Traces` Head-to-Head (`warp34` `nchapman/*-W4A16-HGPTQ` vs Optimized `vLLM 0.31.0` `cyankiwi/*-AWQ`, `1–2× RTX 3090 Ti` `sm_86`, `16k` max context, `0` failed requests across all cases, receipts in `/tmp/swe_h2h_cyankiwi/` and post-fix `/tmp/swe_h2h_v2/`):**
  - **`Qwen3.6-35B-A3B` (`TP=1`, 1× 24 GB GPU):** `warp34` (`MTP-3`, `TQ4` `4,096` pages = `65,536` tokens, `max_num_seqs=8`) serves **`359.27 tok/s`** (`smoke-warmup`, `354.32` pre-fix), **`350.92 tok/s`** (`swe-c4-shared`, `353.37` pre-fix, `89.4%` hit, warm TTFT p50 `106.9 ms`), **`326.21 tok/s`** (`swe-c4-unique`, `337.35` pre-fix, `88.8%` hit), **`397.98 tok/s`** (`swe-c8-burst`, up from `358.13 tok/s`, **`+11.1%`**, `88.5%` hit, warm TTFT p50 `137.5 ms`), and **`383.00 tok/s`** (`swe-c12-overload`, up from `324.94 tok/s`, **`+17.9%`**, `88.1%` hit, warm TTFT p50 `831.9 ms`). By contrast, `cyankiwi/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-AWQ-4bit` has `22.03 GiB` of weights alone (unquantized shared experts and attention) and **OOMs KV cache initialization (`-0.85 GiB`) on 1× 24 GB GPU without CPU weight offloading**; 1-GPU `warp34` outperforms 2-GPU `vLLM TP=2 plain` across all 5 cases (`359.27–397.98` vs `179.14–348.72 tok/s`) and beats 2-GPU `vLLM TP=2 MTP-3` on `smoke-warmup`, `swe-c4-shared` (`350.92` vs `319.49 tok/s`, **`1.10×` faster on half the GPUs**), and `swe-c4-unique`.
  - **`Qwen3.6-35B-A3B` (`TP=2`, 2× 24 GB GPUs, `max_num_seqs=8`):** `warp34` (`MTP-3`, `TQ4` `8,192` pages = `131,072` tokens, `16` GPU MTP KV slots) vs optimized `vLLM TP=2 MTP-3` (optimized `vLLM TP=2 plain` in parentheses):
    - `smoke-warmup`: **`444.11 tok/s`** (`450.29` pre-fix) vs `260.43 tok/s` (`179.14` plain) — **`1.71×`** (**`2.48×`** vs plain), TTFT p50 **`30.5 ms`** vs `96.7 ms` (**`3.17×` lower**).
    - `swe-c4-shared`: **`411.22 tok/s`** (`415.99` pre-fix) vs `319.49 tok/s` (`261.08` plain) — **`1.29×`** (**`1.58×`** vs plain), warm TTFT p50 **`104.5 ms`** (`94.1 ms` pre-fix) vs `174.9 ms` (**`1.67–1.86×` lower**), TPOT p50 **`6.21 ms`** vs `7.63 ms`, hit `89.2%` vs `88.4%`.
    - `swe-c4-unique`: **`446.91 tok/s`** (`433.97` pre-fix) vs `307.14 tok/s` (`253.13` plain) — **`1.46×`** (**`1.77×`** vs plain), warm TTFT p50 **`109.2 ms`** (`95.3 ms` pre-fix) vs `186.6 ms` (**`1.71–1.96×` lower**), TPOT p50 **`6.04 ms`** vs `7.58 ms`, hit `88.9%` vs `87.9%`.
    - `swe-c8-burst`: **`471.93 tok/s`** (`462.83` pre-fix) vs `458.46 tok/s` (`338.94` plain) — **`1.03×`** (**`1.39×`** vs plain), warm TTFT p50 **`125.7 ms`** vs `253.9 ms` (**`2.02×` lower**), hit `88.4%` vs `88.6%`.
    - `swe-c12-overload`: **`450.80 tok/s`** (up from `420.37 tok/s`, **`+7.2%`**) vs `478.42 tok/s` (`402.96` plain) — **`1.12×`** vs plain (`0.94×` vs `vLLM MTP-3`), warm TTFT p50 **`685.3 ms`** vs `825.6 ms` (**`1.20×` lower**), hit `88.2%` (up from `86.8%`).
  - **`Qwen3.8-27B` (`TP=1`, 1× 24 GB GPU):** `warp34` (`MTP-3`, `TQ4` `4,096` pages = `65,536` tokens) vs optimized `vLLM TP=1 plain` (`--gpu-memory-utilization 0.96`, `18.37 GiB` weights leaving `1.58 GiB` = `19,418` tokens of BF16 KV cache; `vLLM TP=1 MTP-3` OOMs 24 GB VRAM due to `3.3 GiB` BF16 MTP + `2.37 GiB` temporary `ParallelLMHead`):
    - `smoke-warmup`: **`126.20 tok/s`** (`126.28` pre-fix) vs `63.74 tok/s` (**`1.98×`**), TTFT p50 **`122.7 ms`** vs `298.5 ms` (**`2.43×` lower**).
    - `swe-c4-shared`: **`101.66 tok/s`** (`102.16` pre-fix) vs `27.97 tok/s` (**`3.63×`**), hit **`89.2%`** vs `49.0%`, TPOT p50 **`23.50 ms`** vs `59.02 ms` (**`2.51×` lower**).
    - `swe-c8-burst`: **`117.96 tok/s`** (`124.10` pre-fix) vs `20.64 tok/s` (**`5.71–6.01×`**), hit **`90.1%`** vs `15.3%`, warm TTFT p50 **`471.1 ms`** (`452.5 ms` pre-fix) vs `10,437.5 ms` (**`22.2–23.1×` lower**).
    - `swe-c12-overload`: **`117.31 tok/s`** (up from `82.93 tok/s`, **`+41.5%`**) vs `20.87 tok/s` (**`5.62×`**), hit **`91.0%`** (up from `84.0%`) vs `19.1%`, warm TTFT p50 **`2,661.1 ms`** vs `15,260.2 ms` (**`5.73×` lower**).
  - **`Qwen3.8-27B` (`TP=2`, 2× 24 GB GPUs, `max_num_seqs=8`):** `warp34` (`MTP-3` and `DFlash2 M=8`, `TQ4` `8,192` pages = `131,072` tokens, `16` GPU draft KV slots) vs optimized `vLLM TP=2 MTP-3` (optimized `vLLM TP=2 plain` in parentheses):
    - `smoke-warmup`: **`196.79 tok/s`** (`204.49` pre-fix, `MTP-3`) / **`181.58 tok/s`** (`182.50` pre-fix, `DFlash2`) vs `107.79 tok/s` (`84.95` plain) — **`1.83–1.90×`** (**`2.32–2.41×`** vs plain), TTFT p50 **`98.4 ms`** vs `267.4 ms` (**`2.72×` lower**).
    - `swe-c4-shared`: **`141.45 tok/s`** (`141.36` pre-fix, `MTP-3`) / **`128.96 tok/s`** (`132.25` pre-fix, `DFlash2`) vs `112.36 tok/s` (`92.75` plain) — **`1.26×`** (**`1.53×`** vs plain), warm TTFT p50 **`318.7 ms`** (`337.5 ms` pre-fix) vs `525.1 ms` (**`1.65×` lower**), TPOT p50 **`16.06 ms`** vs `17.54 ms`, hit `89.2%` (`MTP-3`) / `88.8%` (`DFlash2`) vs `88.3%`.
    - `swe-c8-burst`: **`179.86 tok/s`** (up from `173.42 tok/s`, `MTP-3`) / **`144.61 tok/s`** (`147.89` pre-fix, `DFlash2`) vs `154.52 tok/s` (`142.79` plain) — **`1.16×`** (**`1.26×`** vs plain), warm TTFT p50 **`414.7 ms`** (`376.5 ms` pre-fix) vs `837.0 ms` (**`2.02–2.22×` lower**), hit **`90.8%`** (`MTP-3`) / **`89.5%`** (`DFlash2`) vs `89.6%`.
    - `swe-c12-overload`: **`191.06 tok/s`** (`MTP-3`, up from `150.29 tok/s`, **`+27.1%`**) / **`135.80 tok/s`** (`DFlash2`, up from `38.41 tok/s`, **`3.54×` / `+253.6%`**) vs `179.29 tok/s` (`182.81` plain) — **`warp34 MTP-3` now beats both `vLLM TP=2 MTP-3` (`1.07×`) and `vLLM TP=2 plain` (`1.05×`) on `swe-c12-overload`** with warm TTFT p50 **`1,599.2 ms`** vs `1,887.1 ms` (**`1.18×` lower**) and hit **`92.1%`** (`MTP-3`) / **`89.5%`** (`DFlash2`, up from `42.7%`).

## `sm_89+` Hardware `FP8 E4M3` KV Cache (`--kv-format fp8` & `TQ4` `FP8_E4M3` Tail Anchors)

- **Descriptor-Isolated `WARP34_KV_FORMAT_FP8_E4M3` (`warp34_cuda.h`, `tq4_kv_common.cuh`, `op_kv_cache.cu`, `op_paged_attn.cu`, `op_qsa.cu`, `ffi.rs`, `cache.rs`, `cuda.rs`, `warmup.rs`, `engine_worker.rs`, `cli/`):**
  - Added `WARP34_KV_FORMAT_FP8_E4M3 = 4` (`block_fp8_e4m3_256`, `264 B/row` at `d = 256`: contiguous `uint8_t qs[256]` payload at offset `0..256` + two FP32 per-128-channel dequant scales `float d0, d1 = amax / 448.0f` at offset `256..264`, `8`-byte row aligned) across paged KV write, single-token/Split-KV scalar decode, `16q`/`64q`/`128q` Tensor Core `mma.sync` chunked prefill & speculative verify, and `Qwen4` QSA sparse paged attention.
  - Gated all `FP8 E4M3` device code under `#if __CUDA_ARCH__ >= 890` (`__nv_cvt_float2_to_fp8x2` with `__NV_SATFINITE` and `__nv_cvt_fp8x2_to_halfraw2` + FP32 scale multiply avoiding FP16 subnormal loss, `1` coalesced `uint2` `LDG.E.64` / `STG.E.64` transaction per warp) with zero instruction/register overhead on `sm_86`, warmed `WARP34_KV_FORMAT_FP8_E4M3` write + paged attention in `prepare_execution_plan()` on `sm_89+`, and rejected `--kv-format fp8` cleanly before weight loading on `< sm_89` (`CudaDeviceProps::supports_fp8_kv()`).
  - Exposed `--kv-format fp8` (`KvFormatPolicy::Fp8`, `Identity` transform across all target full-attention/QSA layers) and upgraded `KvFormatPolicy::Tq4` (`--kv-format tq4`) on `sm_89+` so its 4 tail-anchor boundary layers use unrotated `FP8_E4M3` (`264 B/row`, zero WHT-256 launches) instead of rotated `Q8_0` (`272 B/row`), shrinking `27B` `TP=2` KV from `21,376 -> 21,120 B/tok` (`3.10×` compression vs `FP16`).
- **Verified Gates, Accuracy, & Live `1–2× RTX 4090` (`sm_89`) Throughput:**
  - **Verification & Sanitizer Gates:** `mise run check-quick` (`cuda-bin-check`: `1,054` entries `sm_86=527, sm_89=527`, `local=0`, `static_shared<=48KiB`), `314/314` `warp34-host` lib tests on both `sm_86` and `sm_89` (including `test_prefill_64q_zero_ulp_parity_across_kv_formats` and `test_gpu_hc_ple_qsa_kernels_parity` live `FP8_E4M3` parity + `< sm_89` fail-fast rejection), `9/9` `tq4_vision_and_moe`, `10/10` `speculative_decoding`, `qwen4_flash_next` QSA `Fp8` (`cos >= 0.999`) & `Tq4` (`cos >= 0.99`) parity, and `compute-sanitizer` (`memcheck` + `racecheck` + `synccheck`) `0` errors/hazards across `FP8_E4M3` KV write, `16q`/`64q`/`128q` prefill, scalar decode, and QSA sparse attention.
  - **Real-Model Accuracy (`FP8` & `TQ4` with `FP8` Tail vs `FP16` Ground Truth):** `96/96` exact greedy token match vs `FP16` on `Qwen3.8-27B` (`TP=2`); `80/80` exact greedy token match vs non-speculative `FP16` ground truth on `Qwen3.6-35B-A3B` (`TP=2` `Open-SWE-Traces`, where `Q8_0` diverges at token 11); `3/3` exact 192-token trajectory match vs `FP16` on `Qwen3.5-4B` `DFlash2` `Open-SWE-Traces` (`2/3` on `Q8_0`); `100%` exact `7,116`-token needle retrieval (`739184-COBALT`) across `fp16` / `q8_0` / `fp8` / `tq4` on `27B` `TP=2`; and `lossless_parity: true` across Radix and Vision stages.
  - **`warp34 bench` & `Open-SWE-Traces` Highlights (`1–2× RTX 4090`, `--kv-format fp8` & upgraded `tq4`):**
    - **`Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TP=2`):** `--kv-format fp8` `MTP-3` **`660.69 tok/s`** (**`1.514 ms` ITL**, `2.969/3` accepted, `+24.2%` vs `tq4` `526.76 tok/s` due to matching `FP16` MTP draft distribution); `4k` context decode `237.48 tok/s` (`12,483.8 tok/s` prefill).
    - **`Qwen3.5-4B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TP=1`):** `--kv-format fp8` **`218.88 tok/s`** plain, **`615.65 tok/s` `MTP-3`** (`1.624 ms` ITL), **`1,734.08 tok/s` `DFlash2`** (**`0.577 ms` ITL**, `14.875/15` accepted vs `1,531.10 tok/s` on `q8_0`), **`1,340.85 tok/s`** `B=8` scheduler (`5,698.59 tok/s` total); upgraded `--kv-format tq4` **`1,731.28 tok/s` `DFlash2`** (`0.578 ms` ITL, `12,672 B/tok`).
    - **`Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TP=2`):** `--kv-format fp8` **`88.06 tok/s`** plain, **`417.35 tok/s`** `B=8` scheduler (`1,773.76 tok/s` total, fastest of `q8_0`/`fp8`/`tq4`), and **`285.41 tok/s`** mean `DFlash2` decode across real `Open-SWE-Traces` (`+6.9%` vs `fp16` `266.89 tok/s`, `+2.9%` vs `q8_0` `277.24 tok/s`); upgraded `--kv-format tq4` **`326.87 tok/s` `DFlash2`** (`3.059 ms` ITL, `5.35/7` accepted) and **`234.17 tok/s` `MTP-3`** on `Open-SWE-Traces`.

## Full 1–2× RTX 4090 (`sm_89`) Hardware Qualification, Sanitizer Gate, & Flagship Benchmarks

- **`sm_89` Portability, CUDA Binary Gate, & Ada `24,082 MiB` Tiered-MoE Capacity Fixes (`op_moe.cu`, `model/rank_capacity.rs`, `model/rank.rs`, `tools/check_cuda_binaries.py`, `tools/prep_test_models.py`, `tests/backbone_and_collectives.rs`, `tests/speculative_handoff.rs`):**
  - Tightened `moe_w4a16_gate_up_silu_f16_kernel` launch bounds to `__launch_bounds__(128, APPLY_HAD64 ? 4 : 8)` (`STACK: 24 B -> 0 B` across all `APPLY_HAD64 = true` specializations under CUDA 13.0/13.4 `ptxas` for both `sm_86` and `sm_89`), updated `tools/check_cuda_binaries.py` for Cargo `1.99+` `target/<profile>/build/warp34-kernels/*/out/libwarp34_kernels.a` layout and `vision_qkv_bias_rope_inplace_f16_kernel` (`32 B` `libdevice` `__nv_sinf`/`__nv_cosf` call frame), fixed `tools/prep_test_models.py` (`Qwen3.5-4B-W4A16-HGPTQ` sharded `model.safetensors.index.json`), skipped config-only snapshots in `test_loader_all_five_cached_checkpoints`, and replaced an exact `1`-FP16-ULP (`0.015625`) prompt tie on `sm_89` TQ4 in `speculative_handoff::native_speculation_yields_to_late_peer_losslessly`.
  - Added `plan_tiered_moe_hot_per_layer_with_dflash` and aligned `TP >= 2` pre-`RankTieredMoe` GDN state-slot protection (`18` slots for `MTP` at `>= 2,048` pages; `max(dflash_min_state_slots, 14)` for `DFlash2`, while preserving `TIERED_MOE_STAGE_RESERVE_SLOTS * exp_bytes` for `TieredMoeWorkspace` + mapped-host GPU page-table overhead) so `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` `TP=2` `MTP-3` (`>= 18` GDN slots) and `DFlash2` (`>= 13` GDN slots) at `4,096` KV pages fit cleanly within RTX 4090's `24,082 MiB` usable VRAM (`482 MiB` Ada GSP/ECC carveout vs `369 MiB` on `sm_86`).
- **100% Verification & Sanitizer Gates Passed on `2× RTX 4090` (`sm_89`, `128` SMs, `72 MiB` L2, Driver `590.48.01`, CUDA `13.0`):**
  - Passed `mise run check-quick` (`fmt-check`, `clippy`, `clippy-cpu`, `doc-check`, `simd-check` `15` CPU kernels / `14` `llvm-mca` targets, `cuda-bin-check` `1,054` entries `sm_86=527, sm_89=527`, `local=0`, `static_shared<=48KiB`), `312/312` `warp34-host` + `4/4` `warp34-kernels` lib tests, all `36` integration test binaries (including real-checkpoint `pinned_hgptq_{dflash2_lossless,moe_35b_text_vision_mtp,text_graph_mtp}_and_tp2`, `qwen4_flash_next` 128-tok `Plain == MTP-3 == DFlash2` exact parity on `TP=1` & `TP=2`, and `7/7` `worker_capacity`), `dump-diff --compare-tp2` (`4B`, `27B`, `35B-A3B`, and `Flash-Next` all `match = true`), and **`114/114` `compute-sanitizer` runs (`memcheck` + `racecheck` + `synccheck` in `481.3s`, `0` errors/hazards across all 6 CUDA subsystems + `TP=2` collectives)**.
- **Verified Live `warp34 bench` Throughput (`1–2× RTX 4090`, `TQ4` KV, `128 -> 128` tokens unless noted, `lossless_parity: true` across all Radix/Vision stages):**
  - **`nchapman/Qwen3.5-4B-W4A16-HGPTQ`:** `TP=1` **`218.21 tok/s`** plain (`4.58 ms` ITL, `10,917.8 tok/s` prefill, `3.56×` radix / `3.33×` vision TTFT speedup, **`1,336.93 tok/s`** `B=8` scheduler / `5,682.0 tok/s` total), **`615.52 tok/s` `MTP-3`** (`1.625 ms` ITL, `2.969/3` accepted), **`1,697.34 tok/s` `DFlash2`** (`0.589 ms` ITL, `14.875/15` accepted); `TP=2` **`1,896.64 tok/s` `DFlash2`** (**`0.527 ms` ITL**, `8.37 ms/round`).
  - **`nchapman/Qwen3.5-9B-W4A16-HGPTQ`:** `TP=1` **`137.96 tok/s`** plain (`7.25 ms` ITL, `7,038.7 tok/s` prefill, `3.45×` radix / `2.98×` vision TTFT speedup, **`881.73 tok/s`** `B=8` scheduler), **`390.47 tok/s` `MTP-3`** (`2.561 ms` ITL, `2.969/3` accepted), and **`134.11 tok/s`** with `nchapman/Qwen3.5-9B-Heretic-LoRA` (`cuda_graph = true`, `7.46 ms` ITL).
  - **`nchapman/Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ`:** `TP=1` **`53.35 tok/s`** plain (`18.74 ms` ITL, `2,431.4 tok/s` prefill) / **`111.70 tok/s` `MTP-3`** / **`112.93 tok/s` `DFlash2`**; `TP=2` **`87.65 tok/s`** plain (`11.41 ms` ITL, `5.20×` radix / `2.87×` vision TTFT speedup, **`413.88 tok/s`** `B=8` scheduler), **`237.60 tok/s` `MTP-3`** (`4.209 ms` ITL, `2.735/3` accepted), **`327.23 tok/s` `DFlash2`** (`3.056 ms` ITL, `5.35/7` accepted).
