## Kernel-Glue & Host-Device Orchestration Audit (Batches `A`–`D`)

- **Speculative & Sampler Seams (`Batch A` — `op_sampler.cu`, `warp34_cuda.h`, `ffi_ext.rs`, `cuda/warmup.rs`, `model/sampling.rs`, `model/graphs.rs`, `model/batch.rs`, `proposer.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`, `parser.rs`):**
  - Added 16-CTA split-K `warp34_sampler_greedy_top5_f16` (`top5 == null && num_rows <= 8 && vocab_size >= 4096`) and shard-aware `warp34_sampler_shard_fast_greedy_f16` (`vocab_offset` masked/penalized local argmax + `8 B/row` `tp2_argmax_exchange`, eliminating `248 KiB/row` `gather_vocab_f16` on `TP>=2` greedy rows without soft bans), both warmed in `prepare_execution_plan()` and verified clean in `tools/check_cuda_sanitizer.py`.
  - Keyed 4-entry LRU `spec_verify_graphs` by `(m_verify, long_ctx, DecodeGraphSampleMode, GraphLoraMode)` (`PlainGreedy` vs `LogitsOnly`), raised `PINNED_SAMPLER_BYTES` from `96 KiB` to `576 KiB` (`16` `31 KiB` bitmasks in one pinned DMA), added `PreparedSpecSamplerRows` + `O(1)` `truncate_drafts` so speculative grammar bitmasks and sparse penalties are computed once per round, and suppressed discarded `lm_head` + greedy sampling on non-final speculative prefill chunks.
- **Unified Pinned Metadata, Standalone CLI Parity, & Batched Speculative Graphs (`Batch B` — `model/graph_io.rs`, `model/execution.rs`, `model/generation.rs`, `model/graphs.rs`, `model/batch.rs`, `proposer.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`):**
  - Decoupled `BatchedDecodeGraphIo` `816 B` pinned metadata from `max_blocks_per_req` pitch via dense dirty-row `upload_block_tables_slot` (preserving pinned DMA even at `262k` context `B=16`), packed `DecodeGraphIo` into single-H2D / single-D2H pinned slabs with `cached_block_table` tracking, eliminated 10 per-call `DeviceBuffer::alloc`s in `step_tokens` (`batch_meta_arena`), removed redundant post-D2D stream syncs in batched MTP, added `256 KiB` pinned `PrefixMemo` staging, and added `BatchedSpecVerifyGraphIo` (`B in {2, 3, 4}`, `total_tokens <= 24`) CUDA Graph capture/replay.
- **Tiered-MoE, Multi-LoRA Graph Modes, & Persistent `TpWorkerPool` (`Batch C` — `op_moe_router.cu`, `moe_offload/`, `moe_cpu_bridge.rs`, `model/graph_io.rs`, `model/graphs.rs`, `model/rank_runtime.rs`, `coordinated.rs`, `cuda.rs`):**
  - Bounded `warp34_moe_pull_mapped_experts_with_count` `grid.y` by `multi_processor_count` (`5,120 -> 128` CTAs on 0-miss `M=8` steps on `sm_86`), decoupled pure GPU-LRU token cap (`TIERED_MOE_GPU_LRU_MAX_DECODE_TOKENS = 16`), coalesced dirty LRU D2H across 48 layers into `1` sync (`sync_all_dirty_from_device`), and overlapped Rayon CPU `StreamedPrefill` tasks + pinned staging with GPU cold pulls.
  - Added `GraphLoraMode::{BaseOnly, Uniform(i32), Mixed}` + dynamic `supports_cuda_graph_for_tokens_with_lora` (preserving 0-LoRA `BaseOnly` graphs and causal `Uniform` graphs even when `midloop` / `noncausal` Decision LoRAs are loaded), reused 7 PLE staging `Vec`s, gave `HostFailures` a lock-free `count == 0` path + `Arc<[_]>` snapshot, and wired futex-parked `TpWorkerPool` across all 10 `TP > 1` target/proposer call sites.
- **Multimodal Vision, Decision Serving, & `MambaRadixCache` Recycling (`Batch D` — `vision.rs`, `vision/linear.rs`, `scheduled_vision.rs`, `model/decision.rs`, `engine_worker/decode.rs`, `scheduler.rs`, `radix.rs`):**
  - Added persistent `VisionWorkspace` + 8-entry GPU `pos_cache` + pinned `d_patches` / `replicate_to_peers_pinned` + reusable splice buffers, deferred quantized vision bias (`forward_bias_maybe_defer`, saving `97` standalone launches per 24-block ViT encode at `0`-ULP parity), added 8-entry / `64 MiB` `vision_embed_cache` on `Qwen3_5Engine`, suppressed non-final vision `lm_head` (`step_prefill_with_emit`), and enforced `effective_mixed_prefill_budget()` in `step_vision_prefills`.
  - Reserved `1` GDN slot (`admit_waiting_by_shareability`) instead of `3` for unshareable `prefix_bidir_len` requests, skipped `72 MiB/rank` `midloop` GDN state snapshots on cold `has_initial_state == 0` slices, coalesced batch `CandidateLogits` (`read_batch_candidate_logits`) and `HiddenPositions` pinned D2H to `1` sync/rank, coalesced contiguous `selected_rows`, and recycled evicted `MambaRadixCache` nodes via `retire_dead_node` + `free_nodes`.
  - **Verified Live (`2× RTX 3090 Ti`):** `4B-HGPTQ` `TP=1` **`589.33 tok/s` `MTP-3`** / **`1,574.20 tok/s` `DFlash2`** (`0.635 ms` ITL) / **`1,171.79 tok/s` `B=8` scheduler**; `27B-HGPTQ` `TP=2` **`216.70 tok/s` `MTP-3`** / **`215.15 tok/s` `DFlash2`** / **`364.01 tok/s` `B=8` scheduler**; `35B-A3B-HGPTQ` `TP=2` **`553.81 tok/s` `MTP-3`** (`1.806 ms` ITL) / **`1,067.30 tok/s` `B=8` scheduler**; `Flash-Next-HGPTQ` `TP=2` **`105.73 tok/s` `MTP-3`** / **`67.28 tok/s` `DFlash2`** (`+7.7%`) / Vision `588.4 -> 35.2 ms` (`16.7×` warm); `9B-HGPTQ + Heretic-LoRA` `cuda_graph = true` **`126.07 tok/s`**; `Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B` multimodal `dump-diff --compare-tp2` `match = true` (`287` tensors, `min_cosine = 0.999225`).

## Full CUDA Kernel Correctness & `sm_86`/`sm_89` Performance Audit (`C1`–`C4`, `I1`–`I10`, `P1`–`P10`)

- **Clean-Room Audit & Binary Resource Analysis (`147904e`):** Audited all 37 CUDA C++/header files (`~28.5k` LOC), FFI bindings, and cold-start `prepare_execution_plan()` warmup across `1,043` compiled `sm_86`/`sm_89` kernel specializations.
- **Critical & Important Correctness Fixes (`213173d`, `bfea69c`, `d6e88d6`):**
  - **Sanitizer-Verified Fixes:** Fixed `PagedKVManager::load_page` partial-tile clamp OOB read in `op_paged_fa2.cu` (`compute-sanitizer --tool memcheck`: **`152 -> 0` errors**), missing trailing `__syncthreads()` in `op_sampler.cu` shared reductions (`--tool racecheck`: **`1,500+ -> 0` hazards**), Marlin Split-K CTA barrier + `.release.gpu` lock init in `marlin_template.h` (`--tool racecheck`: **`60 -> 0` hazards**), communicator-wide `next_comm_bank_odd` staging parity across changing CTA counts in `op_collectives.cu`, and `-104.0f` cumulative-decay clamping against `-inf - (-inf) = NaN` in `op_gdn_wy*.cu` and `op_gdn.cu`.
  - **Boundary, Warmup, & Multi-GPU Fixes:** Added 64-bit `size_t` Marlin repack offsets and `int64_t` host dimension checks (`op_marlin.cu`), cold-start warmup of `{128,128,128}` / `{64,256,256}` / `thread_m_blocks=2` Marlin tiles (`warp34_marlin_warmup_specializations()`), `(group_size & 63) == 0` MoE grouped-MMA scale/ZP hoist guard (`op_moe.cu`), multi-checkpoint GDN WY emission + `1 <= kernel_size <= 4` conv1d guard (`op_gdn*.cu`), rank-invariant `TP=4` collective grid sizing + 4-device SM clamp (`op_collectives.cu`, `op_init.cu`), `valid_req_mask` & GQA divisibility checks (`op_kv_cache.cu`, `op_qsa.cu`), strided fallback for `GemmaRMSNorm` `hidden_size > 12288` + `grid.x = num_tokens` (`> 65535` safe) + alignment/aliasing fixes (`op_norms_rope_act.cu`, `op_hc.cu`, `op_ple.cu`), and validate-before-mutate MoE router/LRU/bridge checks (`op_moe_router.cu`, `op_moe_cpu_bridge.cu`).
- **`sm_86`/`sm_89` Stack-Spill Elimination & Occupancy/Throughput Optimizations (`7c0ffaa`, `ca63f67`, + follow-up A/B tuning):**
  - **Zero-Stack (`STACK:0`) Across Hot Kernels:** Added warp-specialized 512-expert `top_k=10` router (`REG:53, STACK:0`, `8.70 -> 4.57 µs`, `1.90×`) and generic 512-expert warp router (`REG:90, STACK:0`) while eliminating generic router stack (`STACK:256 -> 0`), templated `norm_bf16_vec8_4warp<1..=12>` (`STACK:384 -> 0`, `1.43×–1.58×`), moved hybrid MoE CPU-bridge scratch to `__shared__` (`STACK:2048 -> 0`), eliminated all 24 `op_collectives.cu` spills (`tp4_*` `STACK:64..112 -> 0`, `tp2<512,12>` `STACK:16 -> 0`) while scaling `tp2`/`tp4_all_reduce_hc_combine_tap_f16` to `512` threads (`1` PCIe read wave for `hidden_size > 2048`, `11.50/13.71/17.51 -> 9.72/12.09/16.06 µs` at `M=1/4/8`) + `TP=4` `uint4` vectorization, eliminated all `op_lora.cu` spills (`STACK:32/16 -> 0`), eliminated `moe_sparse_vec4_kernel` spills (`STACK:16 -> 0`), and accelerated `sampler_step_f16_kernel` (`1024` threads, `REG:64, STACK:32, SHARED:28492`) with `kSamplerCandCap = 2048` shared-memory candidate compaction, `chosen_cnt == 1` early exit, `HalfVec8` loads, and `__all_sync` 5-step `FP64` warp butterfly (`248k` vocab `top_k=50`: **`2,548 -> 750 µs` (`3.4×`)**; `top_k=0` nucleus: **`33,998 -> 2,836 µs` (`12.0×`)**; `memcheck=0`, `racecheck=0`).
  - **Dynamic Shared Memory, Bank Conflicts, Sub-Warp GEMV, & 2D Grids:** Computed exact per-tile Marlin dynamic smem (`get_kernel_cache_size`) with cached per-device `cudaFuncSetAttribute` (`g_marlin_attr_warm_mask`), padded `gdn_wy_chunk64` `s_H` stride to `BV + 8 = 72` (`101,120 B <= 101,376 B`) to eliminate 32-way `wmma` bank conflicts, packed 2–4 output columns per warp in `moe_w4a16_down_accum_f16_kernel` (`n_interm = 256/512/640`, `2.16×–2.86×` on Flash-Next & `1.33×–1.62×` on 35B-A3B), vectorized `fused_qk_rmsnorm_rope_gate_f16_kernel` head loads/stores (`uint4`, `1.09×–1.14×`), and tiled `op_hc.cu` / `op_ple.cu` across 2D `(num_tokens, col_blocks)` grids (`hc_combine_tap` `4.64×`, `ple_dequant_f8_hadamard` `3.14×`).
  - **Automated CUDA Binary & Sanitizer Regression Gates (`tools/check_cuda_binaries.py`, `tools/check_cuda_sanitizer.py`, `.mise.toml`):** Added `mise run cuda-bin-check` (`~150 ms`, wired into `check-quick` and `check`) enforcing `sm_86`/`sm_89` parity (`524 == 524` kernels after removing stale `ar rcs` archive members in `build.rs`), `LOCAL == 0`, static `SHARED <= 48 KiB`, `STACK == 0` outside the explicit call-frame allowlist, and launch-bound `REG` ceilings; and added `mise run cuda-sanitize` (also executed sequentially as the final phase of `mise run check` after `test` releases GPU VRAM) running `compute-sanitizer --error-exitcode=1` (`memcheck` + `racecheck` + `synccheck`, `108/108` runs) concurrently across all 6 CUDA subsystems + `TP=2` cross-GPU collectives (catching and fixing an intra-warp `s_work`/`s_head` `__syncwarp()` hazard in `qsa_sparse_paged_attn_gqa_f16_kernel<true>` and `hc_count` MTP buffer sizing in `qwen4_flash_next.rs`).
  - **Verified Live (`2× RTX 3090 Ti`, `mise run check` green, `review` 0 findings):** `nchapman/Qwen3.5-4B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TP=1`, `TQ4`, `MTP-3`) **`583.5 tok/s`** decode (`1.71 ms` ITL); `nchapman/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TP=2`, `TQ4`, `MTP-3`) **`548.6 tok/s`** decode (`1.82 ms` ITL) and `TP=1` vs `TP=2` `dump-diff` `match=true` (`min_cosine = 0.999929`, `draft_exact = 3/3`); `nchapman/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TP=2`, `TQ4`, 512-expert tiered MoE) **`66.1 tok/s`** plain decode (`+17.0%`), **`25.94 ms/round`** `MTP-3` verify (`+10.1%` vs `28.57 ms/round`), **`102.4 tok/s`** English `MTP-3` generation (`+10.3%` vs `92.8 tok/s`), and **`112.6 tok/s`** `MTP-3` (`64/64` exact parity across Plain, `MTP-3`, and `DFlash2`).

## Multi-LoRA / PEFT Adapter Support (`FP16` & `W4A16-HGPTQ` Base Models, Mixed-Adapter Batching, & Hadamard Pre-Rotation)

- **CUDA Multi-LoRA Shrink+Expand Kernels (`crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_lora.cu`, `op_init.cu`, `warp34_cuda.h`, `ffi_ext.rs`, `warmup.rs`, `graphs.rs`):**
  - Implemented `warp34_lora_shrink_expand_f16` with deterministic Split-K `lora_shrink_splitk_f16_kernel<kRStep>` (`kRStep ∈ {1,2,4}`, 64-thread 2-warp CTAs writing disjoint `[split_k, m, rank_pad]` FP32 partials with zero `memset` or `atomicAdd`) paired with warp-coalesced sliced `lora_expand_add_coalesced_f16_kernel<kBlockThreads, kGroupLanes>` (`kGroupLanes ∈ {1,2,4,8,16,32}` with 8B `uint2` / 16B `uint4` contiguous `lora_B` loads, dynamic SM-aware `tiles_per_cta` capping, parallel `(t, r)` shared-memory Split-K reduction, zero-barrier `Uniform` fastpath, and direct CTA mapping onto non-zero `b_slices4` blocks including partially adapted fused projections such as `["q_proj", "v_proj"]` or `["v_proj"]`-only), alongside `warp34_cublas_lora_shrink_expand_f16` (homogeneous-batch Tensor Core `cublasGemmEx` `U = X @ A^T` (`beta = 0`) + per-slice `Y[:, n_off..n_off+slice_n] += U[:, r_off..r_off+slice_r] @ B_s^T` (`beta = 1`) for aligned `rows >= 32` or `rows >= 8 && rank_pad >= 32`).
  - Supports per-token `token_lora_ids[t]` routing across up to 62 simultaneously loaded adapters (`lora_id >= 0`) and base model (`lora_id = -1`, 0-ULP unchanged output, preserving `in_proj_ba.can_fork_on_aux_stream() == true` when `lora.is_currently_noop()`) within the same prefill, decode, or speculative verify batch, expands `decode_graph` (`8 -> 16`) and `spec_verify_graphs` (`4 -> 16`) capacities with `BaseOnly` eviction protection when LoRA is attached, and is CUDA-Graph capturable (`RankLoraState` binds graph-static device pointers via `SpecVerifyGraphIo::meta_i32` and `BatchedDecodeGraphIo::meta_i32`).
  - Registered all `warp34_lora_shrink_expand_f16` (`<1>/<2>/<4>` shrink + `<64,1>/<64,2>/<128,4>/<128,8>/<128,16>/<256,2>/<256,8>/<256,16>/<256,32>` expand, all `STACK=0, LOCAL=0`) and `warp34_cublas_lora_shrink_expand_f16` (`s1688` + `s16816`) specializations in `prepare_execution_plan()` warmup for cold-start `TP=2`/`TP=4` safety. Verified live (`1× RTX 3090 Ti`): `0.8B-W4A16-HGPTQ` + `GLiDE-LoRA` (`r=128`, 12 modules) decode **`228.9 -> 346.6 tok/s` (`+51.4%`)** (`lora_expand` `18.58 -> 4.21 µs`); `4B-W4A16-HGPTQ` + `CAI` (`r=8`, 7 PEFT modules) decode **`186.3 -> 191.1 tok/s`** and `B=8` scheduler **`1010.5 -> 1032.0 tok/s`** (`512`-tok prefill LoRA GPU time `4.83 -> 1.78 ms`); `9B-W4A16-HGPTQ` + `Heretic` (`r=3`) `B=8` scheduler **`705.6 -> 736.5 tok/s`** (`125.5 tok/s` `B=1` decode).
- **PEFT Adapter Loader, Hub Subfolder Resolution, & Hadamard-Fused `LinearProj::WithLora` (`crates/warp34-host/src/lora.rs`, `hub.rs`, `model/weights.rs`):**
  - Parses standard HuggingFace PEFT `adapter_config.json` (`r`, `lora_alpha`, `use_rslora`, `rank_pattern`, `alpha_pattern`) and `adapter_model.safetensors` (`F16`, `BF16`, `F32`) across local paths, HF repo IDs (`owner/repo`), and subfolder repo specs (`owner/repo/sub/folder[@rev]`).
  - Supports all Qwen 3.5 / 3.6 / 3.8 projections (`q_proj`, `k_proj`, `v_proj`, `qkv_proj`, `o_proj`, `in_proj_qkv`, `in_proj_z`, `in_proj_qkvz`, `in_proj_b`, `in_proj_a`, `in_proj_ba`, `out_proj`, `gate_proj`, `up_proj`, `gate_up_proj`, `down_proj`, `lm_head`) with automatic per-adapter packing for fused projections (`qkv_proj`, `in_proj_qkvz`, `in_proj_ba`, `gate_up_proj`), shared-`lora_A` deduplication in `pack_multi_slice_lora` (collapsing joint-SVD `q_proj`/`k_proj`/`v_proj`, `gate_proj`/`up_proj`, and `in_proj_qkv`/`in_proj_z` exports from `3r`/`2r` down to `r`, cutting LoRA kernel time and VRAM by 2×–3× and fitting `r = 256` joint-SVD QKV within `MAX_LORA_RANK_PAD = 512`), and column-parallel (`TP=2` / `TP=4`) or row-parallel (`o_proj`, `out_proj`, `down_proj` with `lora_A` sliced along `K` and pre-scaled `lora_B` replicated across ranks so the TP all-reduce sums $\sum_p (x_p A_p^T)(s B^T) = s(x A^T)B^T$) sharding.
  - Nests `LinearProj::WithLora` inside `LinearProj::Hadamard` and pre-rotates `lora_A` rows on the host by $H_B$ (`hadamard_rows_f32` using orthogonal $H_B^T H_B = I$), preserving `can_fuse_h64_from_producer() == true` (`RMSNorm+H64` and `SiLU*Mul+H64`) on `W4A16-HGPTQ` checkpoints with **zero extra Hadamard launches and zero `ex.scratch_ptr` overhead**.
- **Per-Request Multi-LoRA Routing, Per-Adapter `DecisionModelProfile`, `MambaRadixCache` Prefix Isolation, CLI, & HTTP Serving (`radix.rs`, `scheduler.rs`, `decision.rs`, `cli/`, `server/`):**
  - Salts `MambaRadixCache` root token keys (`salt_radix_key_for_lora`) when `lora_id >= 0` so base-model (`-1`) and per-adapter (`0..N`) KV pages and GDN `StateBundle` checkpoints are strictly isolated in the shared radix tree.
  - Loads an optional per-adapter `DecisionModelProfile` (`CodeReadout` for `d3` / `GLiDE` or `CausalLm`) onto `LoraAdapterEntry` when a LoRA directory contains `decision_config.json` (+ optional `readout.safetensors`, or falls back to `lm_head.weight[token_ids]` on the base model when `readout.safetensors` is omitted), routes `/v1/systemone` and `/v1/decisions` through the target adapter's `DecisionModelProfile` (falling back to the base model's profile when `lora_id == -1`), and activates per-adapter `noncausal_full_attention` (`q.prefix_bidir_len = Some(q.token_ids.len())`, `q.prefix_cap = Some(0)`) and damped-Euler `midloop` recurrence (`active_midloop_specs()` on `Qwen3_5ModelRank`, e.g. `blocks: [[12,17],[18,23]], passes: 2` on `GLiDE-9B` and `blocks: [[24,35],[36,47]], passes: 2` on `GLiDE-27B`) so bidirectional/midloop Decision LoRA requests execute in isolated noncausal waves alongside concurrent 100%-causal 1-pass base-model traffic.
  - Added repeatable `--lora-modules` / `--lora` (`<name>=<path_or_hf_repo>[/subfolder][@rev]`) and `--active-lora <name>` CLI flags across `warp34 serve`, `generate`, `bench`, `chat`, `dump-diff`, `greedy-diff`, and `profile-diff`.
  - Listed loaded LoRA adapters in `GET /v1/models` (`parent: <base_model>`) and routed requests by `"model": "<adapter_name>"` (or `"lora"` / `"adapter"`, plus family/repo aliases in `LoraAdapterRegistry::resolve_name`) across `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/completions`, `/v1/responses`, `/v1/messages`, `/v1/messages/count_tokens`, `/v1/systemone`, and `/v1/decisions` (with unspecified `"model"` / `"lora"` defaulting to the base model's `CausalLm` profile), and updated the embedded Overview dashboard (`dashboard/src/App.tsx`) to display prompt evaluation throughput (`tokens.computed`) and E2E request latency (`latency.e2e`) on zero-generated-token Decision workloads.
  - Added unified pre-allocation LoRA memory planning (`LoraCapacityReserve`, `estimate_lora_reserve_from_specs`, `plan_scratch_arena_bytes_with_lora`, `plan_target_kv_pages`) so `--lora-modules` VRAM (`lora_A`, `lora_B`, `lowrank_ws`) and execution traits (`has_midloop`, `requires_atomic_prefix`) are accounted for up front when sizing `scratch_arena`, `host_embed` offload (enabling zero-flag `TP=1` 27B + `glide-27b` on a single 24 GB GPU), `RankTieredMoe`, `StateBundlePool`, and `KvPool` (auto-sizing serving `kv_pages` up to `2,048` pages = `32,768` tokens when `--kv-pages` is omitted).
  - **Verified Live on `nchapman/Qwen3.5-9B-W4A16-HGPTQ` + `nchapman/Qwen3.5-9B-Heretic-LoRA`:** **`141.55 tok/s`** single-stream decode with CUDA graphs enabled, 0-ULP identical base-model output when `lora_id = -1`, exact mixed-batch (`base` + `heretic`) continuous-batching parity, and `TP=1` vs `TP=2` `dump-diff` parity (`tp1_top1 == tp2_top1`, `min_cosine = 0.998927`).
  - **Verified Live Decision LoRA JevBench (`128` Public + `64` MultiQ, `0` errors) on `W4A16-HGPTQ` Base Models:**
    - **`nchapman/Qwen3.5-0.8B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`3a51478079`) + `glide-0.8b=nchapman/Qwen3.5-0.8B-GLiDE-LoRA` (`3f1915bc51`, `r=128`):**
      - Base model (`CausalLm`): Public **`67 / 128` (`52.34%`)** at **`100.2 dec/s`** (`1.28s`), MultiQ **`26 / 64` (`40.62%`)** at **`234.7 dec/s`** (`0.27s`).
      - `glide-0.8b` (`CodeReadout` + `noncausal_full_attention`): Public **`106 / 128` (`82.81%`, `+30.47 pp`)** at **`99.0 dec/s`** (`1.29s`), MultiQ **`63 / 64` (`98.44%`, `+57.81 pp`)** at **`176.8 dec/s`** (`0.36s`).
    - **`nchapman/Qwen3.5-2B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`a9b3c78230`) + `glide-2b=nchapman/Qwen3.5-2B-GLiDE-LoRA` (`ea5b1407c3`, `r=128`):**
      - Base model (`CausalLm`): Public **`82 / 128` (`64.06%`)** at **`49.2 dec/s`** (`2.60s`), MultiQ **`34 / 64` (`53.12%`)** at **`114.9 dec/s`** (`0.56s`).
      - `glide-2b` (`CodeReadout` + `noncausal_full_attention`): Public **`113 / 128` (`88.28%`, `+24.22 pp`)** at **`51.6 dec/s`** (`2.48s`), MultiQ **`62 / 64` (`96.88%`, `+43.75 pp`)** at **`87.3 dec/s`** (`0.73s`).
    - **`nchapman/Qwen3.5-9B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`332dad698d`) + `glide-9b=nchapman/Qwen3.5-9B-GLiDE-LoRA` (`948c7d02ca`, `r=128`, 2-pass `midloop` `[[12,17],[18,23]]`):**
      - Base model (`CausalLm`): Public **`110 / 128` (`85.94%`)** at **`11.4 dec/s`** (`11.25s`), MultiQ **`61 / 64` (`95.31%`)** at **`26.7 dec/s`** (`2.40s`).
      - `glide-9b` (`CodeReadout` + `noncausal_full_attention` + 2-pass `midloop`): Public **`122 / 128` (`95.31%`, `+9.38 pp`)** at **`9.1 dec/s`** (`14.09s`), MultiQ **`64 / 64` (`100.00%`, `+4.69 pp`)** at **`14.9 dec/s`** (`4.31s`).
    - **`nchapman/Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`cc31dcc085`, `TP=2`) + `glide-27b=nchapman/Qwen3.5-27B-GLiDE-LoRA` (`5ea3c159de`, `r=128`, 2-pass `midloop` `[[24,35],[36,47]]`):**
      - `glide-27b` (`CodeReadout` + `noncausal_full_attention` + 2-pass `midloop`, 88 layer evals): Public **`121 / 128` (`94.53%`)** at **`3.14 dec/s`** (`c=1`, `p50 = 127.9 ms`) / **`3.43 dec/s`** (`c=16`, `37.37s`), MultiQ **`64 / 64` (`100.00%`)** at **`5.49 dec/s`** (`c=8`, `11.65s`).

## Model Checkpoint Standardization (`Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B` Core Tests, `nchapman/*-W4A16-HGPTQ` `warp34` Targets, & Mainstream Competitor Baselines)

- **Standardized Model References Across Code, Tests, Oracles, Benchmarks, & Docs:**
  - Standardized all core 4B correctness tests (`axum_serving`, `backbone_and_collectives`, `decode_graphs`, `dflash_proposer`, `generative_apis`, `gpu_sampler`, `http_constraints`, `mtp_proposer`, `radix_cache_and_scheduler`, `serving_options`, `speculative_decoding`, `speculative_handoff`, `tq4_vision_and_moe`, `worker_capacity`), CLI defaults (`warp34 dump-diff` / `greedy-diff` / `profile-diff`), and `tools/prep_test_models.py` (`CORE_TEST_MODELS`) on unquantized `Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B` (`851bf6e806efd8d0a36b00ddf55e13ccb7b8cd0a`).
  - Extended `SMALL_N_GEMM_PLE_MAX_N` (`10240 -> 12288`) in `crates/warp34-host/src/model/weights.rs` so unquantized 4B `TP=1` GDN `in_proj_qkvz` (`12288 × 2560`) routes through `M`-invariant `warp34_small_n_gemm_f16` for `M <= 16`, preserving 0-ULP `ssm_state` parity between eager and padded CUDA graph buckets across all `n in 1..=16`.
  - Recaptured `tools/oracle/goldens/vllm_moe_layer0_m8.{json,safetensors}` on `nchapman/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`4f5de21c886d79e17af5b8a4bfc625e226703fb6`) and verified `moe_sparse` (`4/4` tests) against it.
  - The current benchmark policy uses `nchapman/*-HGPTQ*` for quantized `warp34` targets and cyankiwi AWQ for quantized `vLLM` / `SGLang` baselines, including `cyankiwi/Qwen3.5-9B-AWQ-4bit`, `cyankiwi/Qwen3.8-27B-AWQ-INT4`, and `cyankiwi/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-AWQ-4bit`. Earlier third-party GPTQ selections were superseded; their historical results must not be relabeled. See [checkpoint policy](../benchmarks/MODELS.md).

## Unified `CodeReadout` (`GLiDE` + `vllm-sr/d3`) & `CausalLm` Decision Models (`#12`)

- **Simplified `DecisionModelProfile` & Removed Legacy Gen-1 Decision Profiles (`decision.rs`, `decision/batch.rs`, `decision/scoring.rs`, `server/decision_http.rs`):**
  - Replaced `Clef`, `Kev`, `Nimble`, `OpenJev`, and `Xor`/`Lev` profiles and removed `crates/warp34-host/src/clef*` and `crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_clef.cu` (`warp34_clef_*`) in favor of two unified profiles:
    - **`DecisionModelProfile::CodeReadout`** for `fastino/GLiDE-*` (`GLiDE-0.8B`, `GLiDE-2B`, `GLiDE-9B`, `GLiDE-27B`) and `vllm-sr/d3-*` (`d3-flash`, `d3-turbo`, `d3-ultra`), projecting post-final-`GemmaRMSNorm` hidden states via `CodeReadoutHead` (`readout.weight` `[num_codes, hidden_size]` + optional `readout.bias` from `readout.safetensors`).
    - **`DecisionModelProfile::CausalLm`** fallback for standard Qwen 3.5 / 3.6 / 3.8 generative scoring (`Tev1` ChatML prompt + candidate-token `lm_head` logits).
  - Matched upstream `glide/prompt.py` (`<|im_start|>user\nContext:\n...\n\nThink it through briefly, then answer with the option letter only.<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n\n</think>\n\nAnswer: (`) and `d3_format.py` (`<|im_start|>system\nYou are a decision engine...<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>user\n[Questions in this request:\n...\n\n]State:\n...\n\nQuestion:\n...\n\nOptions:\n...\n\nReply with only the code of the best option.<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n\n</think>\n\n`), plus upstream `decision_config.json` `attention_mode` (`"noncausal_full_attention"` / `"prefix_bidir"`), object-form `midloop` (`{"blocks": [[a, b], ...], "passes": 2}`), and object-form `temperature_by_questions` (`{"max_questions": [...], "temperature": [...]}`).
  - Added `kind = "decision"` (`POST /v1/systemone`) benchmarking and ground-truth accuracy scoring to `warp34perf` with the public MIT-licensed JevBench fixtures (`benchmarks/perf/fixtures/jevbench_public.json` 128 items, `benchmarks/perf/fixtures/jevbench_multiquestion.json` 16×4=64 items, `benchmarks/perf/campaigns/decision-models.toml`).
  - Added generic `order_passes: 1 | 2` forward+reverse option permutation probability averaging across both `CodeReadout` and `CausalLm`.
  - Added explicit fail-fast rejection for deferred `perplexity-ai/pplx-decider-v1.2` (`joint_config.json`, `format_version >= 2`, `format_id == "joint-question-inline-readout-v1"`).
- **Loader, Hub, & Optional `lm_head` Support (`hub/files.rs`, `loader.rs`, `loader/config.rs`, `model/rank.rs`, `mtp.rs`, `dflash.rs`):**
  - Updated Hub download allowlist for `decision_config.json` and `readout.safetensors`.
  - Added `language_model.*` bare prefix resolution in `SafetensorsArchive::resolve_name` alongside `model.language_model.*` and `model.layers.*`.
  - Made `Qwen3_5ModelRank::lm_head` optional (`Option<LinearProj>`), skipping vocab head allocation when `tie_word_embeddings == false` and no `lm_head` tensor is present in the checkpoint, and suppressing `selected_rows` (`emit_mask = false`) on `HiddenPositions`-only prefill batches.
- **Engine Execution Seams (`noncausal_full_attention`, `prefix_bidir` State-Once, & `midloop` Damped-Euler Recurrence):**
  - Added `warp34_midloop_damped_euler_f16` in `op_norms_rope_act.cu` (registered in `prepare_execution_plan()` warmup) and implemented `midloop` damped-Euler recurrence (`h <- x_i + (1 / passes) * (f(x_i) - x_i)`) in `model/forward.rs` with active-batch-slot-only GDN `conv_state`/`ssm_state` snapshot/restore (`last_batch_state_slots`, reused across non-overlapping `midloop` spans) on intermediate passes and input norm re-evaluation on loop wrap and loop exit.
  - Wired `noncausal_full_attention` and `prefix_bidir_len` (`WARP34_ATTN_MASK_SYMMETRIC_WINDOW` with `sliding_window = 0` on full-attention layers, atomic `0..bidir_end` prefill scheduling in `SchedulerV1::schedule_step` and `ScheduledVision::step_prefill`, fail-closed admission when `prefix_bidir_len` exceeds single-chunk prefill capacity or when token generation is requested on a headless checkpoint, and disabled `MambaRadixCache` prefix reuse/donation for `noncausal_full_attention`, `midloop`, and bidirectional prefixes).
- **Decision Hot-Path Optimizations (`model/decision.rs`, `engine_worker/decode.rs`, `engine_worker/runner.rs`, `model/rank_runtime.rs`, `server/decision_http.rs`, `decision/batch.rs`):**
  - Batched multi-position hidden readout D2H transfers (`read_norm_hidden_rows_f32(&[usize])`) in `capture_decision_tap` so `d3` `state_once: true` queries synchronize the CUDA stream once per chunk instead of $K$ times.
  - Combined `order_passes == 2` forward and reverse `CodeReadout` queries into a single synchronous worker wave with a `100 µs` `coalesce_idle_burst` window for `decision_tap` waves (`GLiDE` 4B `midloop 2×`, 3 questions, `order_passes = 2` => 6 queries: **`140.28 ms`**; `d3` 4B `state_once + prefix_bidir`, 4 questions: `order_passes = 1` **`46.70 ms`**, `order_passes = 2` **`75.48 ms`**).
  - Skipped charging `local_vocab_size` in `ordinary_prefill_token_capacity` when `lm_head.is_none()`, raised single-chunk atomic prefill capacity to `4,096` tokens (`64` chunks, clamped to `Tp2Collectives::max_stage_bytes_per_epoch`) for `requires_atomic_prefix_prefill()` checkpoints so all `128` JevBench items (including ~3.5k-token `hard-opus-*` long-policy documents) execute with `0` errors, and matched `d3_runtime.py` `block_attention` on single-position `prefix_bidir` suffix steps.
  - **Verified Live JevBench Results (`128` public single-question items + `64` multi-question items, `0` errors) across `nchapman/*-W4A16-HGPTQ` checkpoints on RTX 3090 Ti (`sm_86`):**
    - **`nchapman/d3-edge-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`8216b11ee1`, `TP=1`, 12L `d3` `noncausal_full_attention` + quantized Vision):** Public **`90 / 128` (`70.31%`)** at **`149.9 dec/s`** (`c=1`, `p50 = 3.91 ms`) / **`232.1 dec/s`** (`c=16`); MultiQ **`46 / 64` (`71.88%`)** at **`341.0 dec/s`** (`c=1`) / **`399.0 dec/s`** (`c=8`).
    - **`nchapman/d3-lite-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`91e438356c`, `TP=1`, 24L `d3` `noncausal_full_attention` + quantized Vision):** Public **`101 / 128` (`78.91%`)** at **`77.4 dec/s`** (`c=1`, `p50 = 8.01 ms`) / **`115.6 dec/s`** (`c=16`); MultiQ **`58 / 64` (`90.62%`)** at **`169.3 dec/s`** (`c=1`) / **`190.7 dec/s`** (`c=8`).
    - **`nchapman/GLiDE-0.8B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`7d9469893e`, `TP=1`, 24L `GLiDE` `noncausal_full_attention`):** Public **`113 / 128` (`88.28%`)** at **`82.3 dec/s`** (`c=1`, `p50 = 7.07 ms`) / **`125.1 dec/s`** (`c=16`); MultiQ **`63 / 64` (`98.44%`)** at **`163.9 dec/s`** (`c=1`) / **`213.8 dec/s`** (`c=8`).
    - **`nchapman/GLiDE-9B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`11218cc42a`, `TP=1`, 32L `GLiDE` `noncausal_full_attention` + `midloop` `[[12,17],[18,23]], passes=2`):** Public **`124 / 128` (`96.88%`)** at **`9.0 dec/s`** (`c=1`, `p50 = 43.95 ms`) / **`9.8 dec/s`** (`c=16`); MultiQ **`64 / 64` (`100.00%`)** at **`15.5 dec/s`** (`c=1`) / **`15.7 dec/s`** (`c=8`).
    - **`nchapman/d3-ultra-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`6a1f655fc7`, `TP=2` & `TP=1`, 64L 27B `d3` `prefix_bidir` + `header-v1` + `temperature_by_questions` + quantized Vision):** Public **`122 / 128` (`95.31%`)** at **`3.5 dec/s`** (`c=1`, `p50 = 155.65 ms`) / **`3.9 dec/s`** (`c=16`); MultiQ **`64 / 64` (`100.00%`)** at **`3.5 dec/s`** (`c=1`) / **`3.6 dec/s`** (`c=8`); `TP=1` 16-item subset **`16 / 16` (`100.00%`, `p50 = 160.00 ms`)**.

## Multi-client Open-SWE-Traces 27B, 35B-A3B & Flash-Next serving optimization (`MTP`, `DFlash2` & `Plain`, `TP=1` & `TP=2`) and 1-hour intact SWE soak

- Built the intact single-instance `nvidia/Open-SWE-Traces` (`4fa4c2ae9afc6b397bc50391d43450c79993c138`) multi-turn trajectory fixture [`open_swe_intact_16k.json`](../benchmarks/perf/fixtures/open_swe_intact_16k.json) (`6` permissive-license resolved trajectories across Claude Code, OpenCode, and OpenHands; `7–15` turns, `519 → 12,354` prompt tokens; recorded tool observations replayed with `tool_choice: "none"`), [`27b-swe-multiclient.toml`](../benchmarks/perf/campaigns/27b-swe-multiclient.toml), [`35b-swe-multiclient.toml`](../benchmarks/perf/campaigns/35b-swe-multiclient.toml), [`flash-next-swe-multiclient.toml`](../benchmarks/perf/campaigns/flash-next-swe-multiclient.toml), [`soak-swe-27b.toml`](../benchmarks/perf/campaigns/soak-swe-27b.toml), and [`soak-swe-27b-1hr.toml`](../benchmarks/perf/campaigns/soak-swe-27b-1hr.toml).
- Profiled and eliminated multi-client long-trajectory bottlenecks across `27B` (`MTP` & `DFlash2`), `35B-A3B` MoE (`MTP`), and `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` (`MTP`, `DFlash2` & `Plain`):
  - **Prompt-frontier preservation in `MambaRadixCache` (`radix.rs`, `model/batch.rs`, `engine_worker/decode.rs`):** Tagged unverified decode-tail donations with `is_decode_tail: true` and tightened `evict_mamba` / `evict_full_pages` / `alloc_state_slot_preserving_leaves` so Priority 1 tombstones only single-child internal nodes that have a non-decode-tail Mamba descendant (`has_non_decode_mamba_descendant`), Priority 2 reclaims unverified decode-tail leaves whose parent prompt frontier still holds a live Mamba snapshot (`is_reclaimable_decode_tail_leaf`), and Priority 3 falls back to standard LRU—stopping `12k`-token multi-turn trajectories from tombstoning active prompt frontiers (`hit_ratio`: `13.5% → 88.5%` on `TP=1 swe-c8-burst`).
  - **Multi-slot `DFlash2` KV cache & GPU D2D cross-slot KV copy (`dflash.rs`, `mtp.rs`, `proposer.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`):** Partitioned `DFlash2` KV pool into `max_num_seqs.clamp(2, 8)` independent batch slots (`256` physical pages = `4,096` ring tokens per slot, `2,048` pages total) with ring-buffer window validity checking (`dflash_slot_has_prefix`) and GPU D2D `copy_kv_from_slot` for both `MTP` (including QSA `qsa_raw_k_buf` and `qsa_comp_k_buf` side caches on `qwen4_exp`) and `DFlash2` when a session resumes in or shares a prefix with another slot.
  - **3-Tier LRU-aware slot affinity (`proposer/scheduled.rs`, `proposer.rs`):** Ranked batch slot candidates as Tier 2 (exact session continuation, `prefix_len >= tokens.len() / 2`), Tier 1 (empty slot), and Tier 0 (diverged slot ordered by oldest `last_used_tick` LRU), preventing stale long sessions from pinning slots across workload phases (`TP=1 DFlash2 swe-c8-burst`: `23.63 → 83.04 tok/s`).
  - **Serialized prefill admission & multi-client `TP=2` GDN state pools (`engine_worker/runner.rs`, `dflash.rs`, `mtp.rs`, `model/engine.rs`):** Required `sched.active.iter().all(|s| s.is_prefill_complete())` before admitting a second long prefill so concurrent `12k`-token admissions pipeline cleanly with `MambaRadixCache` prefix reuse instead of interleave-thrashing `2048`-token chunks; scaled `TP=2` serving state pools to `64` (`MTP`) and `80` (`DFlash2`) GDN slots.
  - **Bounded `PrefixMemo` growth (`proposer.rs`):** Replaced `shrink_to_fit()` + doubling `resize()` on `100+ MiB` `PrefixMemo.features` buffers with `512`-row (`~5 MiB`) chunked `reserve_exact` (`grow_memo_features`), cutting 10-minute 27B soak host RSS growth from `3,168 MiB` to `1,774 MiB` and stabilizing 1-hour RSS at `-760.10 MiB` post-warmup.
  - **Resident-MoE (`35B-A3B`) MTP GDN state-pool & verify-row budgeting (`model/rank.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`):** Added `resident_moe_mtp_serving_reserve` (`768 MiB` for 4-bit MTP experts on `HGPTQ`, `1,600 MiB` for unquantized 16-bit MTP experts on `GPTQ`) and protected `requested_state_slots` before carving out `VISION_ENCODER_LAZY_RESERVE_BYTES` (`1.5 GiB`), lifting `35B-HGPTQ` (`--kv-pages 8192 --max-num-seqs 8`) on `TP=1` from **29 → 41 GDN state slots** (with the 4-bit `draft_lm_head` enabled) and raising `k_cap` at `batch_len >= 5` on MoE models to `self.k.min(2)` (bounded to `max_verify_rows = 18` on `TP=1`), lifting `warp34-35b-tp1` on `swe-c8-burst` from `156.58 → 353.78 tok/s` (`+126%`).
  - **Tiered-MoE (`Qwen3.8-Flash-Next`) pool budgeting, full-cache `StreamedPrefill` Pass 0, `TP=1` hybrid LRU & `MTP` hot cache (`model/rank.rs`, `moe_offload/engine.rs`, `dflash.rs`, `mtp.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`, `engine_worker/runner.rs`):** Replaced stacked `dflash_reserve_bytes + post_pool_moe_reserve` with `dflash_reserve_bytes` when `DFlash2` is active, matched `dflash::mandatory_bytes` KV accounting to the `2,048`-page sliding-window physical pool, avoided doubling `mtp_capacity` on `is_tiered_moe_mtp`, protected `18` (`TP=2`) / `20` (`TP=1`) GDN state slots across ranks, scheduled `cfg.uses_tiered_moe()` one active sequence at a time across warm `mtp_batch_slots` + `MambaRadixCache` (`<= 8` tokens in `TieredMoeEngine::can_execute_device_lru`), used the full synced `cache.slots.capacity` (`k_hot`) in `StreamedPrefill` Pass 0 on `>= 256`-token serving prefill chunks (`+80` resident hot experts/layer across 2 GPUs), enabled `16` dynamic GPU LRU slots (`k_static = k_hot - 16`) on `TP=1` serving target caches with `M=1` single-token PCIe pull throttling (`0.14`), and scaled `TP=1` `MTP` `k_hot` from `192` to `320` (`62.5%` of `512` experts in VRAM while retaining the `MarlinW4A16` `draft_lm_head`).
- **Verified `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` (`qwen4_exp` tiered MoE + QSA + PLE + HyperConnection) multi-client `Open-SWE-Traces` benchmark (`flash-next-swe-multiclient.toml`, `max_tokens = 192`, `0` failed requests across all `warp34` runs):**
  - **`TP=2` (`2× RTX 3090 Ti`) — `smoke-warmup` / `swe-c4-shared` / `swe-c4-unique` / `swe-c8-burst`:**
    - **`warp34-flash-tp2` (`HGPTQ`, `MTP-3`, `18` GDN slots):** **`56.34 tok/s`** (`tpot` `11.22 ms`, `hit` `56.1%`) / **`40.72 tok/s`** (`tpot` `13.79 ms`, `hit` `88.9%`) / **`42.35 tok/s`** (`tpot` `13.80 ms`, `hit` `88.6%`) / **`42.93 tok/s`** (`tpot` `13.83 ms`, `hit` `86.9%`) — **beats `strata-flash-tp2` across all 4 workloads (`+63.1% / +0.1% / +3.4% / +44.8%`) at 100% lossless exact verification**.
    - **`warp34-flash-tp2-dflash2` (`HGPTQ`, `DFlash2`, `13` GDN slots):** **`55.83 tok/s`** (`tpot` `11.57 ms`, `hit` `56.1%`) / **`36.80 tok/s`** (`tpot` `16.36 ms`, `hit` `87.4%`) / **`36.10 tok/s`** (`tpot` `16.76 ms`, `hit` `87.5%`) / **`35.24 tok/s`** (`tpot` `18.60 ms`, `hit` `86.8%`).

- **Verified 1-hour (`3,675.22 s`) and 10-minute (`649.95 s`) 27B `4` sustained / `8` burst intact Open-SWE soaks (`soak-swe-27b-1hr.toml`, `soak-swe-27b.toml`):**
  - **1-hour soak (`/tmp/warp34-soak-swe-27b-4x8-1hr/soak_summary.json`, `status: "passed"`, `targets_met: true`):** **2,920 completed measured responses** + **153 expected local disconnects** (`3,073` measured requests across `326` multi-turn sessions, `16,221,154` prompt tokens, `365,786` completion tokens = **`99.53 tok/s` sustained over 1 hour on 1 GPU**), **`0` unexpected errors / invalid usage / SLO breaches**, final post-drain inference probe `"ok"`. GPU memory **22,719 → 22,727 MiB (`+8.0 MiB` <= `256 MiB` budget)**; host RSS **4,510.26 → 3,750.16 MiB (`-760.10 MiB` <= `2,048 MiB` budget)**.
  - **10-minute soak (`/tmp/warp34-soak-swe-27b-4x8-10min/soak_summary.json`, `status: "passed"`, `targets_met: true`):** **537 completed measured responses** + **28 expected local disconnects** (`565` measured requests across `61` trajectories, `2,967,708` prompt tokens, `63,840` completion tokens = **`98.2 tok/s` sustained on 1 GPU**), **`0` unexpected errors / invalid usage / SLO breaches**, GPU memory `+2.0 MiB`, host RSS `+1,774.23 MiB`.
  - **Verification:** `review` findings fixed and re-verified; strict `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0,1 WARP34_STRICT_TESTS=1 CUDA_DISABLE_PTX_JIT=1 mise run check` passed, plus `mise run perf-check` and `mise run doc-check`.
- **Upgraded primary benchmarks (`warp34 bench`, `.mise.toml` `bench-all` / `perf-check`, `suite.toml`, and `{27b,35b,flash-next}-swe-multiclient.toml`) to exercise all 4 flagship model families (`4B`, `27B`, `35B-A3B`, `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next`) across `Plain`, `MTP-3`, `DFlash2`, and multi-turn multi-client serving paths:**
  - **`EngineWorker::run_batch_in_place` & speculative `generate_batch_scheduled` (`engine_worker.rs`, `engine_worker/runner.rs`, `model/generation.rs`, `cli/commands.rs`):** Added `run_batch_in_place` to drive `runner::run_loop` (`admit_requests`, `mtp_batch_slots`, `step_mtp_batch`, `step_single_speculation`, `MambaRadixCache` multi-turn continuation) synchronously on an existing `&mut Qwen3_5Engine`, gated shutdown `abort_scheduled()` on non-empty in-flight requests so normal completion preserves `last_accepted` and slot-0 KV residency, routed `generate_batch_scheduled` through `run_batch_in_place` when speculative mode and serving state slots (`>= 12`) are active, and upgraded `warp34 bench` (`--stage scheduler` / `all`) to load via `load_for_serving_with_speculative` and execute a 2-turn multi-client agentic schedule verifying both Turn 2 `MambaRadixCache` continuation (`turn2_cached_prefix_tokens >= batch_size * 64`) and speculative verification (`verify_rounds > 0`).
  - **Uncovered and fixed two additional serving bugs via `warp34 bench --stage all` (`mtp.rs`, `dflash.rs`, `model/rank.rs`, `model/generation.rs`):** Lowered `batch_slot_count()`'s 8-slot threshold from `>= 8192` to `>= 4096` pages (`65,536` tokens = `8,192` tokens/slot) so `--kv-pages 4096 --max-num-seqs 8` runs 8-slot speculative verification at `c=8` instead of falling back to plain decode; capped `TP=2` tiered-MoE `native_mtp` serving state slots at `16` (`4 × 4`) and cleared cached decode/verify CUDA graphs before lazy `ensure_vision_encoder()` when GPU 0 free VRAM `< 512 MiB` so `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` (`TP=2`, `MTP-3`, `--stage all`) completes `decode` (`102.71 tok/s`), `radix` (`25.52×` TTFT speedup), 2-turn `scheduler` (`149` verify rounds, `347` accepted drafts, `369.84 tok/s`), and `vision` (`11.15×` TTFT speedup, `lossless_parity: true`) without OOM.
  - **Expanded `suite.toml`, `{27b,35b,flash-next}-swe-multiclient.toml`, `test_campaigns.py`, and `.mise.toml` (`bench-all`, `perf-check`):** Added all 4 flagship model families (`Qwen3.5-4B`, `Qwen3.8-27B`, `Qwen3.6-35B-A3B`, `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next`) across `TP=1` & `TP=2` and `Plain` / `MTP-3` / `DFlash2` plus the `swe-c4-shared`, `swe-c4-unique`, and `swe-c8-burst` `Open-SWE-Traces` cases to `suite.toml`, added `quick` / `full` / `release` presets and missing `Plain` / `HGPTQ` profiles to `{27b,35b,flash-next}-swe-multiclient.toml`, and enforced manifest + coverage invariants in `test_campaigns.py` and `mise run perf-check` (`128` tests passing).
  - **Updated `compressed-tensors` HGPTQ loader for per-layer `w4_g64` target regex lists and `w8_head` 8-bit `lm_head` / `mtp.fc` groups (`loader/config_rules.rs`, `loader/config.rs`, `model/rank.rs`):** Raised `MAX_COMPRESSED_TENSORS_PATTERNS` (`64 -> 1024`) for per-layer `w4_g64` regex lists (`150–400` patterns across `0.8B–27B`), enabled static symmetric `W8` (`g32/g64/g128` or channel) groups alongside `W4` in `CompressedTensorRules` while enforcing that Hadamard transform rules target `W4` tensors only, resolved per-tensor `QuantScheme` in `estimate_weight_arena_bytes`, and verified `nchapman/Qwen3.5-9B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`332dad698db78c26b44c64b51a5b59008f6e30a8`) across all 4 `warp34 bench --stage all` stages (`TP=1 Plain`: `150.2 tok/s` decode, `850.7 tok/s` scheduler, `5.26×` radix / `1.77×` vision speedup; `TP=1 MTP-3`: `349.5 tok/s` decode, `2.875/3` accepted, `486.1 tok/s` scheduler, `5.59×` radix / `1.80×` vision speedup; `TP=2 MTP-3`: `535.5 tok/s` decode, `603.0 tok/s` scheduler, `6.98×` radix / `1.78×` vision speedup, all `lossless_parity: true`).
  - **Implemented native 2-bit (`W2A16` / `kU2B2`, `w2_g64`) Marlin + GPU-resident MoE + tiered MoE (GPU + SIMD CPU bridge) and mixed `W2`/`W4`/`W8` + `lut4-gptq` HGPTQ-RCO support (`marlin/scalar_type_compat.cuh`, `marlin/dequant.h`, `marlin/marlin_template.h`, `ops/op_marlin.cu`, `ops/op_moe.cu`, `ops/moe_sparse_inline.cuh`, `ops/op_moe_router.cu`, `loader/config_rules.rs`, `loader/config.rs`, `loader/quant.rs`, `model/weights.rs`, `model/weights/hgptq.rs`, `model/weights/moe.rs`, `moe_offload.rs`, `moe_offload/host_store.rs`, `moe_offload/engine.rs`, `moe_cpu_bridge.rs`, `moe_cpu_bridge/eval.rs`, `model/rank.rs`):** Added `vllm::kU2B2` (`ST_ID_U2B2`, `size_bits = 2`, bias `2`, `pack_factor = 16`, `0.25 B/weight`), `warp34_marlin_repack_int2`, and `warp34_marlin_gemm_f16u2` (`lop3.b32` + `__hsub2` / `__hfma2` with `SUB=0x64026402`, `MUL=0x34003400`, `ADD=0xdc08dc08` and `int2` shared-to-register loads), native 2-bit MoE CUDA kernels (`BITS = 2`, `unpack_u2b2_pair_scaled_h2` with `uint2` 8-byte weight loads across all 5 GPU regimes: `M=1`, `M=2..8` verify, `M=9..64` MMA, `M>64` prefill MMA, and `moe_sparse_inline.cuh` tiered decode/prefill/PCIe staging), `MoeExpertStorage::W2A16`, per-layer `bits = 2 | 4` `ExpertPackLayout` (`246,272 B`/expert vs `385,024 B`/expert = `-36.0%` expert footprint), and AVX-512 / AVX2 / scalar 2-bit CPU bridge kernels (`_mm512_cvtepu16_epi32` / `_mm256_cvtepu16_epi32` + `srli{512,256}_elem::<2, B>`), all verified with 0-ULP bit-for-bit parity against dilated `U4B8` (`marlin_w2a16_and_hadamard_w2a16_match_dilated_w4a16_zero_ulp`, `w2a16_u2b2_zero_ulp_parity_with_dilated_u4b8_across_all_regimes_and_tiered_modes`, `expert_u2b2_g64_hadamard64_scalar_and_tile_agreement`) and across dense (`9B`), resident-MoE (`35B-A3B`), and tiered-MoE (`Qwen3.8-Flash-Next`) HGPTQ-RCO checkpoints:
    - **`nchapman/Qwen3.5-9B-W2.6A16-HGPTQ-RCO` (`57c3ae51f5229a1ab46bfc1e404dcb6d4e0006a8`):** 100% greedy token parity (`128/128` bench + `64/64` natural text) against `vLLM 0.31.0` (`HummingLinearKernel`) and `1.18–1.25×` higher single-stream throughput (`TP=1 Plain`: `175.0 tok/s` vs `vLLM` `147.8 tok/s`, `943.8 tok/s` `B=8` scheduler; `TP=1 MTP-3`: `389.1 tok/s` vs `vLLM` `310.7 tok/s`, `2.969/3` accepted; `TP=2 Plain`: `250.5 tok/s` decode, `981.3 tok/s` `B=8` scheduler; `TP=2 MTP-3`: `584.2 tok/s` decode, `1.71 ms` ITL, `627.1 tok/s` `B=8` scheduler; `vLLM` fails `TP=2` init on `Qwen3_VisionMLP.linear_fc2`).
    - **`nchapman/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W2.6A16-HGPTQ-RCO` (`a7f5d6f37c0f519f2e4fe95af1d24eb4fe9d5c7f`, 256-expert resident MoE):** `-6.00 GiB` total GPU VRAM (`19.22 -> 13.21 GiB` on `TP=1`, `21.22 -> 15.22 GiB` across `TP=2`, `-36.0%` MoE expert footprint), 100% greedy token parity (`128/128` bench + `64/64` natural text) against `vLLM 0.31.0` (`HummingIndexedExperts` + `MarlinExperts`), `dump-diff --kv-format fp16 --compare-tp2` `match=true` (`min_cosine=0.999747`), **`1.19×` higher `TP=1 Plain` bench throughput** (`TP=1 Plain`: `223.4 tok/s` vs `vLLM` `188.4 tok/s`, `866.2 tok/s` `B=8` scheduler decode / `3,681.3 tok/s` total; `TP=1 MTP-3`: `435.7 tok/s` decode, `2.29 ms` ITL, `2.969/3` accepted; `TP=2 Plain`: `264.2 tok/s` decode, `583.3 tok/s` scheduler decode / `2,479.1 tok/s` total; `TP=2 MTP-3`: `565.8 tok/s` decode, `1.77 ms` ITL, `2.969/3` accepted, all `lossless_parity: true`), and `+4.2% to +7.8%` higher interactive `warp34 generate` throughput (`TP=1 Plain`: `195.8 -> 211.1 tok/s`, `TP=1 MTP-3`: `249.4 -> 266.7 tok/s`, `TP=2 Plain`: `235.1 -> 248.1 tok/s`, `TP=2 MTP-3`: `362.5 -> 377.6 tok/s`).
    - **`nchapman/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W2.6A16-HGPTQ-RCO` (`f6a1f0f608610cf553009d7fcad1df43390c4e38`, 512-expert tiered MoE + QSA + `lut4-gptq` PLE + HyperConnection):** `-3.40 GiB` pinned host RAM (`9.45 -> 6.05 GiB`) and `-36.0%` GPU `HotExpertCache` + PCIe H2D staging bandwidth across `45 / 48` `w2_g64` MoE layers, 100% greedy token parity (`128/128`), and **`+10.3% to +37.9%` higher decode throughput** across all 6 `TP=1` / `TP=2` `Plain`, `MTP-3`, and `DFlash2` (`M=8`) stages (`TP=1 Plain`: `30.5 -> 38.1 tok/s` decode / `39.2 tok/s` warm radix decode, `+25.0%`; `TP=1 MTP-3`: `46.5 -> 62.9 tok/s` decode, `+35.3%`; `TP=1 DFlash2`: `59.7 -> 82.3 tok/s` decode / `83.7 tok/s` warm radix decode, `+37.9%`; `TP=2 Plain`: `74.1 -> 81.7 tok/s` decode / `94.2 tok/s` warm radix decode, `+10.3%`; `TP=2 MTP-3`: `140.3 -> 161.0 tok/s` decode / `176.8 tok/s` warm radix decode, `+14.8%`; `TP=2 DFlash2`: **`199.3 -> 237.7 tok/s` decode** / **`291.4 tok/s` warm radix decode**, `4.21 ms` ITL, `6.938/7` accepted, `+19.3%`, all `lossless_parity: true`).

## Concurrent 27B MTP serving fixes & 4-sustained / 8-burst soak verification

- Resolved the three concurrent serving bottlenecks exposed by the `0105610` 27B `4` sustained / `8` burst soak (`--kv-pages 4096 --max-num-seqs 4 --max-model-len 16384`, TQ4, MTP-3 on one RTX 3090 Ti), with regression-first tests (`concurrent_27b_mtp_serving_allocates_verify_and_burst_state_slots` in `tests/worker_capacity.rs`, `batched_mtp_max4_handles_8_burst_and_reuses_warm_prefixes` in `tests/speculative_handoff.rs`, and `serving_budget_offloads_embed_when_concurrent_mtp_state_slots_exceed_gpu_embed_slack` in `src/model/tests.rs`):
  - **24-slot GDN pool via serving-state-aware embedding placement (`mtp.rs`, `model/engine.rs`, `model/rank.rs`):** `mtp::serving_state_slots(4, 3)` requests `24` GDN slots (`12` active private + `4` locked radix checkpoints + `4` batched verify extras + `4` cached burst checkpoints), and `plan_host_embed_with_serving_budget` offloads `2.37 GiB` `embed_tokens` to `MappedHostBuffer` when GPU-resident embeddings would squeeze desired serving state/vision reserves below target (lifting the live 27B pool from **12 → 24 GDN slots** while lowering steady VRAM from `23,113` to `22,447 MiB`).
  - **Burst-safe batched MTP routing (`engine_worker/runner.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`):** Bounded `yield_single_speculation`, `extend_or_yield_mtp_batch`, and `admit_requests` by `desired_active = (active + waiting).min(max_num_seqs)` and `take(open_slots)` instead of total waiting queue depth so 8-client bursts stay in 4-slot batched MTP (`B <= 4`) while preserving `B >= 5` plain-decode fallback when `max_num_seqs > 4`. Batched verify also reserves extra GDN state slots before running draft forwards.
  - **Multi-slot `PrefixMemo` & warm MTP KV reuse (`proposer.rs`, `proposer/scheduled.rs`, `engine_worker/runner.rs`):** Preserved host `PrefixMemo`s across single/batch mode switches (`MAX_MEMO_ENTRIES = 32`), swapped per-slot `current: Option<PrefixMemo>` in `select_mtp_batch_slot`, flushed trailing verified tokens and committed memos on slot completion (`finish_mtp_batch_slot`), and enabled `proposer.can_restore_prefix` + longest-prefix free-slot affinity (`pick_free_mtp_batch_slot`) in batched MTP admissions.
- **Re-verified 10-minute 27B `4` sustained / `8` burst soak (`soak-coding-27b.toml`, `614.80 s` measured):**
  - **550 completed measured responses** (up from **340**, **+61.8%**) + **61 expected local disconnects**, **0** unexpected errors / invalid usage / SLO breaches, **86,798 measured output tokens** (**~141.2 tok/s** aggregate vs **~87.2 tok/s** on `0105610`, **+61.9%**).
  - **Active batch occupancy:** `running == 4` in **586 / 630** 1 Hz samples (**93.0%**, up from **16 / 642 = 2.5%**; `running == 3` dropped from **608 / 642** to **25 / 630**).
  - **Latency & speculation:** Sustained (`4` clients) p50 / p95 TTFT **0.372 s / 1.434 s** (down from **8.20 s** p95), p95 E2E **12.53 s** (down from **17.50 s**); burst (`8` clients behind `4` active slots) p95 TTFT **6.27 s** (down from **9.95 s**), p95 E2E **16.55 s** (down from **19.76 s**). Whole-server speculation counters rose from `4,959 / 1,043 / 791` to **46,817 rounds / 47,390 drafted / 40,508 accepted tokens (85.5% acceptance)**.
  - **Prefix-cache reuse:** `coding-agent-replay` reused **144,384 / 305,011 prompt tokens (47.3% overall; turn 2: 27.9%, turn 3: 60.0%, turn 4: 65.0%, up from 0 / 0 / 5.3%)**; `coding-warm-prefix` reused **5,376 / 81,142 (6.6%, up from 0.5%)**.
  - **Memory, pool conservation & caveats:** GPU memory held flat at **22,447 → 22,447 MiB (+0.0 MiB)**; post-probe idle pools conserved all capacity (`kv`: `436 cached + 3660 free = 4096`; `gdn`: `16 cached + 8 free = 24`). Host RSS grew **4358.73 → 4866.40 MiB (+507.67 MiB)** within the 512 MiB gate (mapped `embed_tokens` + `256 MiB` `PrefixMemo` cache + heap warmup, with late-window drift to qualify over multi-hour runs). Fixtures remain synthetic coding/replay (`182–1585` tokens), not intact long-context SWE qualification.
  - **Verification:** `review` reported no actionable findings; strict `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0,1 WARP34_STRICT_TESTS=1 CUDA_DISABLE_PTX_JIT=1 mise run check` passed (**508 Rust tests passed, 0 failed, 13 ignored**, plus `mise run perf-check` 126 tests and `mise run doc-check`). See [`soak-coding-27b.toml`](../benchmarks/perf/campaigns/soak-coding-27b.toml).

## Single-client 27B MTP soak after capacity fixes

- Unchanged engine `0105610` passed a **600.106-second** synthetic/replay soak on one RTX 3090 Ti, TQ4/MTP-3, 196,608-token allocated capacity and one client: **332 completed responses + 36 injected local disconnects**, no unexpected errors or configured latency breaches, final inference probe passed.
- GPU memory stayed at **23,257 MiB**. RSS grew **261.83 MiB** (4225.89 → 4487.72 MiB), within the 512 MiB gate. Most growth was early, but later drift remains; ten minutes does not establish a permanent plateau.
- Agent replay turns 3–4 reused about **81%** of prompt tokens; separate shared-prefix traffic reported **zero reuse**, still unexplained. Successful measured prompts were only **185–1588 tokens**: this is not long-context or intact-SWE qualification.
- Configuration: [`soak-coding-27b-single-client.toml`](../benchmarks/perf/campaigns/soak-coding-27b-single-client.toml). Server stopped cleanly. Next: pinned intact single-task replay and longer-duration RSS/cache checks, rather than the existing assembled Open-SWE fixtures.

## Single-GPU production-target capacity investigation: 27B + MTP

> The earlier 8k per-request recommendation below is **withdrawn**: it worked around
> an embedding-placement allocation defect, rather than establishing a hardware
> context limit. The single-client 262k regression failed before the placement fix
> and passed afterward; a 262k TQ4 server with MTP-3 completed 261,750- and
> 261,840-token prompts (64 output tokens each). Only 99 MiB free VRAM remained
> at peak, and neither request reused cached tokens. Subsequent MTP-aware budgeting
> passes target+MTP startup regressions at both 229,376 and 262,144 tokens. Selected
> next-qualification setting: **196,608 tokens / 12,288 pages / one active client**;
> a 196,155-token prompt generated 64 tokens successfully with **909 MiB minimum
> observed free VRAM** (428.13 s TTFT, 429.47 s E2E). This is a text-only capacity
> probe, not an interactive SLO or soak qualification.
> DFlash2-aware preflight is implemented and the pinned 27B HGPTQ draft passed a
> two-turn smoke at 32k allocated capacity (3050/3332-token prompts, 256 output
> tokens each, 3264 cached follow-up tokens, 1443 MiB free at peak). Its requested
> 24 state slots remain a preference; the verified single-client floor is block
> size + 3, including a locked radix checkpoint. Final strict `mise run check`
> passed after this correction in **373.40 s: 505 Rust tests passed, 0 failed,
> 13 ignored**, plus the non-GPU checks. Review findings were addressed; the
> final state-floor correction also passed its failing-before/passing-after GPU
> regression and live cached-follow-up smoke. Verification log:
> `/tmp/warp34-speculative-budget-final-check-2.log`, SHA-256
> `56487354157aafe1d23ee87e025196b6cb5b7871add888f831ca6ecae7e8d484`.
> Preserve the pre-fix numbers below as historical observations, not defaults.

- Tested pinned `nchapman/Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ@ae51987e3339a2a70070702548571e10fe8bcbc2`, MTP-3, TQ4 on one RTX 3090 Ti. **262,144 shared KV tokens / max 8 sequences failed startup** for insufficient serving state/headroom; 131,072 started with only 191 MiB free and 12 actual GDN slots. A 65,536-token pool / max 4 sequences completed 26 pressure-test responses but retained only ~587 MiB minimum free; not selected as the conservative default.
- **Memory-tested starting point:** `--kv-pages 2560 --max-num-seqs 4 --max-model-len 8192` = **40,960 shared token slots, 8192 prompt+output per request, four active requests**, eight burst clients queue rather than execute simultaneously. Full 16-slot GDN pool; 26/26 two-turn responses passed at client concurrency 1/4/8 with 7455/7737 prompt tokens and 256 generated tokens. GPU peak **23,099 MiB**, free **1067 MiB**; server RSS peak **1956.43 MiB**. This is text-only short pressure evidence, not a proven production-safe maximum or long-duration 27B soak.
- **Usability blocker to investigate:** single-client follow-up reused 7680 tokens and had ~0.10 s TTFT, but four-/eight-client follow-ups reported **zero cached tokens**. Eight-client follow-up TTFT **34.93–54.06 s**, median E2E **60.23 s** even at the chosen shorter context. Separate cache/state retention, prefill/decode interference and queueing investigation is needed; memory fit alone is not interactive readiness. No engine fix or root-cause claim made here. Servers stopped cleanly; GPUs idle.

## Extended workload harness (`feat/extended-workload-soak`)

- Added `python3 -m warp34perf soak` in [`benchmarks/perf/`](../benchmarks/perf/SOAK.md): conjunctive minimum duration/successful-response targets, maximum measured workload duration, completion-progress watchdog, bounded sustained/burst admission, multi-turn replay, every-Nth-request local disconnect injection, per-response SLO/error gates, optional per-PID RSS/per-UUID GPU endpoint growth checks, and a quiet drain followed by a real noncancelled inference probe. Evidence is append-only with source/config/policy hashes and a separate `soak_summary.json` receipt; it is not the finite campaign report schema.
- Added the configurable [`soak-coding.toml`](../benchmarks/perf/campaigns/soak-coding.toml) example using existing sanitized technical-task and coding-agent fixtures. It rotates mixed output/context shapes, generated multi-turn assistant history and shared-prefix traffic; fixtures remain synthetic/replay, not production traces or autonomous tools. Regression tests cover workload rotation/cache semantics, deadlines, accounting, missing telemetry, failure/interrupt receipts, real local SSE transport and CLI wiring.
- **Final real-server smoke passed:** local RTX 3090 Ti, 4B GPTQ snapshot `d5874365f7093b28b69379722d2f45d660fcbca6`, TP1/TQ4, plain generation, 4096 KV pages, max 16 sequences / 8192 context. Requested 60 seconds and 100 successful responses; actual measured/drain-of-admitted-work duration **63.83 s**, **313 measured requests = 282 completed + 31 injected local disconnects**, zero unexpected errors, invalid usage, incomplete sessions or configured SLO breaches. Peak client in-flight sessions **16**; all three cases exercised sustained and burst phases, and mixed output budgets 64/256/512 all ran. Post-warmup to post-drain growth: RSS **8.91 MiB**, GPU **0 MiB** (example budgets 512/256 MiB); final inference probe passed. This second smoke used the already-warm server from the initial smoke. Server stopped cleanly, GPU resources released before the full test campaign.
- Local final artifacts: `/tmp/warp34-soak-smoke-final/`; `soak_summary.json` SHA-256 `c669f16f8a8e4dcbeaa88910277b8d757f1ba63314d9f41ad9dafbb0abd4e37f`. All inventory hashes verified and recorded runner source hashes matched the final implementation. Review identified a peer-close/disconnect-injection race; a new regression failed before the guarded shutdown fix and passed after it. `mise run perf-check` passed **126 tests**.
- **Approved single-GPU 4/8 follow-up:** fresh server with max 8 active sequences, same 4B/TQ4 checkpoint; 600-second target with 60-second sustained / 15-second burst phases. **603.89 s measured, 1926 completed responses + 214 injected disconnects**, zero unexpected errors/invalid usage/stopped sessions, final inference probe passed. RSS grew **11.53 MiB**, with only ~0.31 MiB between the minutes 5–7 and final-minute medians; GPU grew **20 MiB**, then held at 6371 MiB. Client p95 TTFT **263 / 336 ms** and E2E **2.66 / 3.49 s** for sustained-/burst-admitted sessions; later agent turns reused **83–85%** of prompt tokens. These are observational admission groups, not isolated concurrency trials. The example configuration now defaults to single-GPU **4/8**; dual-GPU **8/16** is a separate qualification starting point, not an assumed scaling result.
- **Full verification:** strict `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0,1 WARP34_STRICT_TESTS=1 CUDA_DISABLE_PTX_JIT=1 mise run check` passed in **329.66 s**, **491 Rust tests / 0 failures / 13 ignored**, plus **126 performance-tooling**, 69 oracle-tooling and 18 dashboard tests. Local log `/tmp/warp34-soak-harness-check.log`, SHA-256 `b31e0f299b043856ffac6f621a35468a385147538bc4d40b2755edc5636d384f`. The subsequent 4/8 example-config change was exercised by the 10-minute run and rechecked with the performance-tooling suite; runner source was unchanged.
- **Qualification limits:** no eight-hour/10,000-response campaign run or G3 sign-off. Local disconnects do not acknowledge server cancellation, final probes do not prove pool conservation, endpoint growth does not prove a leak-free plateau, and PID reuse is not detected. Images/grammar/Responses-cancel mixtures, semantic scoring, internal resource assertions and external restart/fault qualification remain separate work. All policy budgets in the example require operator approval. Existing untracked `outputs/` preserved.

## Focused production lifecycle hardening (`fix/production-lifecycle-hardening`, verified)

- Fixed deferred pipeline donation/resource loss when waiting for the next decode step fails: abort/synchronize first, drain transferred release records exactly once, and preserve the original error. Added CPU ordering/failure regressions.
- Serialized Responses active cancellation and terminal publication under one store lock. Explicit cancellation now wins consistently across sync JSON, terminal SSE and stored results; already-published completion cannot be retroactively cancelled. Transport cancellation remains distinct from explicit API cancellation. Added race, ownership/retention, formatter, handler failure-path and live HTTP regressions; documented the `response.cancelled` extension and persistence limits in [`GENERATIVE_APIS.md`](GENERATIVE_APIS.md).
- Fixed native sampled generation continuing beyond terminal grammar state when the terminal token is absent from sampling stop IDs. CPU tests and a real-model native MTP regression pass. Corrected legacy pool budgeting to charge allocated arena capacity rather than used bytes; live serving arithmetic and intentional minimum MoE-plan fallback are unchanged.
- `/health` now returns 503 while stopping, retaining panic diagnostics. CPU and live route coverage pass. [`SERVING.md`](SERVING.md#readiness-shutdown-and-bounded-recovery) documents the distinction between the 30-second HTTP drain and unbounded safe worker join, plus external hard-stop/restart qualification; that operator rehearsal was **not performed**.
- **Verification:** strict `mise run check` on the local 2× RTX 3090 Ti / PHB host passed in 346.83 seconds: **491 Rust tests, 0 failures, 13 ignored**, plus 69 oracle-tooling, 91 performance-tooling and 18 dashboard tests. Formatting, both Clippy modes and rustdoc passed. The initial check caught test-module placement lint failures, corrected before this successful rerun. Review reported no actionable findings.
- **Benchmark smoke:** 4B GPTQ/TQ4 TP1, 128 prompt / 128 generated tokens, plain/MTP-3/DFlash2 `--stage all` passed radix/vision parity, image-cache isolation and B=8 scheduling (256 generated tokens each). Single-run decode rates: **211.68 / 537.66 / 1427.58 tok/s** respectively; not a matched before/after or competitor performance claim.
- **Remaining limits:** sampled stateful-grammar late-peer handoff needs deterministic request-specific synchronization; no timing-dependent test or broad test hooks added. No new device-fault, sustained soak, external-oracle, ignored-model, container, Ada, NVLink or TP4 qualification. Readiness grades and release gates remain provisional. Existing untracked `outputs/` preserved.

Historical Ada, NVLink/TP4, real-model and long-context results remain credited, not mislabeled as untested or broken. No A grades or reproduced D blockers are asserted by this audit. Current hosted CI builds CUDA but does not execute GPU qualification.
