Commands, ignored cases and raw-log hash are in the checklist. No fresh external-oracle, ignored real-model/format, Ada/NVLink/TP4, container or soak qualification performed. Production scope/SLOs, owners, durable release artifacts and gate sign-off remain open. Existing untracked `outputs/` preserved.

## MoE Offloading Subsystem Consolidation (`refactor/moe-offload-consolidation`, verified)

- Consolidated `warp34`'s Qwen3.8-Flash-Next (`qwen4_exp`) tiered MoE offloading subsystem (`crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/{op_moe_router,op_moe_cpu_bridge}.cu`, `crates/warp34-host/src/{moe_offload,moe_cpu_bridge}.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/moe_offload/{host_store,engine,tests}.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/model/weights/moe.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/mtp{,/propose}.rs`) to eliminate duplicated kernels, tables, and dispatch seams while preserving 64/64 0-ULP greedy token parity across `Plain`, `MTP-3`, and `DFlash2` on both `TP=1` and `TP=2`:
  - **Unified Mapped PCIe Expert Pull Kernel (`op_moe_router.cu`, `op_moe_cpu_bridge.cu`, `warp34_cuda.h`, `ffi_ext.rs`):** Replaced three duplicate mapped-host-to-VRAM pull implementations (`moe_gpu_lru_pull_mapped_experts_kernel`, `moe_pull_mapped_experts_kernel`, and `moe_hybrid_pull_mapped_experts_kernel`) with a single `moe_pull_mapped_experts_kernel` + `warp34_moe_pull_mapped_experts_with_count` shared across host-planned `GpuLru`, on-device `warp34_moe_lru_update`, and `warp34_moe_hybrid_submit_and_pull`.
  - **Smooth BIP-LRU `k_protected` Floor for `M=8` Verify (`op_moe_router.cu`):** Replaced the `total_pairs <= 40` cliff in `moe_gpu_lru_plan_and_remap_kernel` (`k_protected = (k_hot > 40 && total_pairs <= 40) ? (k_hot - 40) : 0`) with `tail_budget = (total_pairs <= 40) ? 40 : min(40, k_hot / 2); k_protected = (k_hot > tail_budget) ? (k_hot - tail_budget) : 0`, protecting anchor hot slots during `M=8` DFlash2 verify (`total_pairs = 80`) even when `k_hot <= 80`.
  - **`HotExpertCache` & `MoeOffloadMode` Cleanup (`host_store.rs`, `moe_offload.rs`, `engine.rs`, `moe_cpu_bridge.rs`):** Unified duplicate `slot_to_expert: Vec<usize>` + `slot_to_expert_i32: Vec<i32>` into a single `slot_to_expert: Vec<i32>` (`-1` when empty) with shared `upload_lru_metadata()`, removed unused `layer_id` from `warp34_moe_hybrid_submit_and_pull` / `MoeBridgeReqSlot` / `HotExpertCache::register_cpu_bridge` and unused `step_clock_buf` from `HotExpertCache`, retired the unreachable host-driven `MoeOffloadMode::CpuHybrid` variant, and gated test-only scalar CPU reference helpers (`eval_expert_u4b8_cpu` / `eval_cold_tasks_cpu`) behind `#[cfg(test)]`.
  - **Unified `LayerTieredMoe`, `TieredMoeEngine::execute_auto`, & SME Seam Hardening (`engine.rs`, `host_store.rs`, `moe_cpu_bridge.rs`, `model/weights/moe.rs`, `model/rank_runtime.rs`, `mtp.rs`, `mtp/propose.rs`):** Extracted `LayerTieredMoe` (`host_store` + `hot_cache`) and `TieredMoeEngine::{can_execute_device_lru, execute_auto}` so `RankTieredMoe`, `MtpTieredMoe`, `FfnBlockWeights::execute_tiered`, `MtpEngine::forward_single_step_into_logits`, and `Qwen3_5ModelRank::supports_cuda_graph_for_tokens` share a single `M <= 8` device-LRU / hybrid-bridge vs `M > 8` `StreamedPrefill` dispatch implementation; centralized `ExpertPackLayout::supports_cpu_bridge()`; guarded `HotExpertCache::prefill_hot_limit()` for fully VRAM-resident layers (`capacity >= expert_ids.len()`); added `0..lru_start` spill to host `GpuLru` eviction; and scoped `mark_tiered_moe_device_lru_dirty()` to layers that actually mutate device LRU state.
  - **Verified:** `mise run check-quick`, `mise run test-quick` (`272` unit tests), `test_qwen4_exp_synthetic_2layer_tp1_tp2_radix_and_cuda_graph`, and `test_qwen4_flash_next_128tok_plain_mtp3_dflash2_exact_parity` (`64/64` exact token match across `Plain`, `MTP-3`, and `DFlash2` on both `TP=1` and `TP=2`).

## `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-HGPTQ` 100% Lossless `Plain` / `MTP-3` / `DFlash2` Parity Across `TP=1` and `TP=2`

- Resolved all `M=1` (`Plain`) vs `M=4` (`MTP-3`) vs `M=8` (`DFlash2`) greedy token divergences on `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`128` prompt / `64` generated tokens, `tq4`, `TP=1` and `TP=2`), achieving **64/64 bit-exact token parity across `Plain`, `MTP-3`, and `DFlash2` on both `TP=1` and `TP=2` (and identical 64-token sequences between `TP=1` and `TP=2`)**:
  - **QSA Split-K Invariance (`crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_qsa.cu`):** Unified `target_splits = 8` across all decode and speculative verify batch sizes (`num_tokens <= 16`) on both `TP=1` and `TP=2`, eliminating Split-K partition drift (`32` vs `8` vs `4` splits across `M=1/4/8`).
  - **MoE GEMV vs Grouped-Vec4 FP32 Reduction-Tree & Regime Unification (`crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_moe.cu`):** Unified `moe_w4a16_gate_up_silu_f16_kernel` and `moe_w4a16_down_accum_f16_kernel` (guarded by `(words_per_group & 3) == 0`) to use the exact same `uint4` 32-element group loads, pair FMAs, and `lpr`-lane butterfly reduction order as `moe_sparse_vec4_kernel`, and extended `regime == 1` (`moe_sparse_vec4_kernel`) through `num_tokens <= 8` when `group_size == 64 && top_k >= 10` so `M=1`, `M=4` (`MTP-3`), and `M=8` (`DFlash2`) share a 0-ULP identical MoE reduction tree.
  - **`TP=1` Hybrid CPU-Bridge `M <= 8` Coverage & Host Reference FP16-Intermediate Parity (`crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_moe_cpu_bridge.cu`, `crates/warp34-host/src/moe_cpu_bridge.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/moe_cpu_bridge/eval.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/moe_offload.rs`):** Extended `TIERED_MOE_CPU_BRIDGE_MAX_DECODE_TOKENS` from `4` to `8` (`128` token-expert pairs, `32,768` FP16 elements) so `DFlash2` (`M=8`) uses the same hybrid bridge path as `M=1` and `M=4`, rounded intermediate `gate`/`up`/`silu(gate)*up` products and down-projection accumulators to FP16 in `ExpertPackLayout::eval_single_expert_f16` (`moe_offload.rs`), and kept `k_static = k_hot` on `TP=1` (with `device_lru_safe` gated on `!layer_has_cpu_bridge` when `num_tokens > 8`) so cold misses do not mutate static hot slots across steps.
  - **`StreamedPrefill` Pass-0 Static Slot Determinism (`crates/warp34-host/src/moe_offload/engine.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/moe_offload/host_store.rs`):** Added preallocated `d_prefill_expert_to_slot` to `HotExpertCache` so `< 1024`-token `StreamedPrefill` Pass 0 maps only the static non-evictable hot slots (`0..k_hot - 40` on `TP=2`), keeping prefill 0-ULP deterministic across requests while leaving adapted decode LRU slots (`k_hot - 40..k_hot`) warm in VRAM.
  - **2560-Dim & PLE Unquantized `DenseF16` M-Invariant Small-N GEMM Routing (`crates/warp34-host/src/model/weights.rs`, `crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_norms_rope_act.cu`):** Raised `SMALL_N_GEMM_DECODE_MAX_DIM` from `2048` to `2560` and added `SMALL_N_GEMM_PLE_MAX_N = 10240` (with an unbranched `rows == 1 && cols == 4` fast path in `small_n_gemm_4x4_f16_kernel` / `small_n_gemm_dual_4x4_f16_kernel` for `tiles_n >= 128`) so `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next`'s unquantized `DenseF16` projections (`router_gate`, `shared_expert.gate_up`, `index_qk_proj`, `ple.key_proj`, and `ple.value_proj`) use the M-invariant `warp34_small_n_gemm_f16` reduction tree across `M=1..16` instead of switching cuBLAS kernels between `M=1` and `M=4/8`.
  - **Verified `test_qwen4_flash_next_128tok_plain_mtp3_dflash2_exact_parity` (`128 -> 64` tokens, `tq4`) and full `mise run check`:** 64/64 exact token match across all 6 runs (`TP=1` `Plain` / `MTP-3` **`52.87 tok/s`** / `DFlash2` **`31.41 tok/s`**; `TP=2` `Plain` **`58.70 tok/s`** / `MTP-3` **`108.10 tok/s`** / `DFlash2` **`62.49 tok/s`**), and **477 Rust tests passed, 0 failed** in `mise run check`.

## Post-merge benchmark audit (`1503ac5`) and persistent Marlin recovery

- Compared `db6c65e`, merged `1503ac5`, and the final optimization on the local **2× RTX 3090 Ti** host: **26 native configurations per sweep**, covering 4B GPTQ and 9B/27B/35B-A3B/Flash-Next HGPTQ at TP1/TP2, plain/MTP-3/available DFlash2, 128 prompt / 64 generated tokens, TQ4. Six 4B multistage runs also passed radix/vision parity, image-cache isolation, and B=8 scheduling. All 26 final output arrays exactly match their merged-HEAD counterparts.
- Confirmed a **9B regression** with three alternating old/merged repetitions: TP2 plain median **213.89 → 198.81 tok/s** and MTP **563.73 → 535.50 tok/s**. Kept the recent one-slice correctness policy; instead of restoring arbitrary SM-count Split-K grids, selected an exact tile-count divisor (at least half the SM count) for whole-tile persistent CTAs. Final TP2 results recover to **204.85 plain / 544.76 MTP tok/s**, with exact output parity. **9B remains ~2–4% below the old build**; recovery is partial, not a claim of regression-free performance. Added scalar-reference and bit-exact row checks for wide Marlin shapes, groups 64/128, and M=1/4/8/9/16. 9B HGPTQ/TQ4 TP1-vs-TP2 dump-diff passed (`rel_l2=0.041857`, cosine `0.999131`, matching top-1).
- Final native rates: 4B TP1 **211.51 / 534.20 / 1288.02 tok/s** and TP2 **305.51 / 766.01 / 1488.17** (plain/MTP/DFlash); 27B TP2 **86.31 / 232.95 / 266.11**; 35B TP2 **266.42 / 569.46** (plain/MTP). Dense and 35B plain/speculative token parity passed. **Flash-Next plain/speculative divergence root-caused** via per-layer `ForwardDump` inspection at the exact benchmark prefixes: Layer 0 (`embed` + `PLE` + `HyperConnection` + `GDN` conv1d/recurrent) is **0-ULP (`0.000000`) bit-identical across all 4 rows (`r = 0..4`)** between sequential `M=1` and `M=4`; the first `1`-ULP (`1.5e-5`–`2.4e-4`) differences arise in the 512-expert tiered MoE FFN (`layer_0_hidden_after`, from CPU-bridge FP32-intermediate vs GPU-slot FP16-intermediate evaluation across dynamic LRU promotions in `k_static..k_hot` and `M=1..3` GEMV vs `M=4..7` grouped-vec vs `M>=8` sparse-MMA) and QSA Split-K (`num_splits = 32/8/4` at `M=1/4/8`). Both observed benchmark flips occur at near-tied top-2 logits: **TP1 index 15** (`279` `' the'` `27.062500` vs `328` `' "'` `27.046875`, **1 FP16 ULP = `0.015625`**, which even flips on a second `M=1` pass from the same state slot due to dynamic expert promotion; single-step `M=1` vs `M=4`/`M=8` `final_norm` `rel_l2 = 0.0022`, `cos = 0.9999976`) and **TP2 index 29** (`28279` `' twelve'` `23.468750` vs `220` `' '` `23.390625`, **5 FP16 ULPs = `0.078125`**, single-step `M=1` vs `M=4`/`M=8` `rel_l2 = 0.0017`, `cos = 0.9999987`, plus occasional 10th-expert router boundary flips across multi-token steps). No Flash assertions were weakened, and no new competitor or Ada qualification is claimed.
- Reproduced the known B=5 handoff failure, then corrected its oracle: compare complete outputs against non-speculative **B=5**, assert peak concurrency five, zero verify rounds, five completed 64-token streams, duplicate-slot equality, and no cancellations. Removed hardware-specific B=1 prefix exceptions; retained B=2/B=4 and late-peer parity checks. Three extra B=5 repeats passed. Final exact-tree **`mise run check` passed: 476 Rust tests, 0 failures, 12 ignored**, plus all non-GPU checks. The former B=5 baseline exception is no longer needed. Review found no kernel issues; retained the same-geometry oracle rather than reinstating invalid cross-geometry prefix expectations.
- Explicit XGrammar CPU benchmark: cold schema **1.194 ms**, cached compile **9.709 µs**, mask **0.509 µs/token**, accept/materialize **2.879 µs/token** (one five-token microbenchmark). HTTP 4B TP1 FP16 smoke/B=8 passed **198/198 measured requests**, with median aggregate **185.19 / 1099.93 tok/s**; generic publication warnings remain. An initial port-8000 collision was preserved separately and resolved by using port 18834 without stopping the existing service. Our server stopped; GPUs idle. Existing untracked `outputs/` preserved.

## PR #8 Copilot follow-up

- Reproduced all nine review findings and added regressions before fixes. Schema-aware server parsing now requires canonical, grammar-covered envelopes; compact/altered auto-mode text is not executable. Parameter-aware scanning preserves raw outer-delimiter text and JSON shielding in mixed string/object schemas, including streaming splits and EOS-disambiguated raw values.
- Preserve null padding semantics, exact integer-valued JSON numbers, raw string newlines, and schema-valid enum/const alternatives. Request-owned native byte grammars discriminate candidate values with full property constraints and original reference roots; additional regressions cover bounded numeric unions, literal parameter delimiters in const/pattern strings, and `$ref`-shaped literal data. No handwritten full-schema validator was added.
- Forward `parallel_tool_calls` through Responses translation, restrict named-tool parser metadata to the selected tool, and detect EBNF operators outside quoted strings, regexes, and both comment styles. Review follow-up returned **no actionable findings**. Final exact-tree `mise run check` passed non-GPU checks and GPU coverage until the same pre-existing B=5 handoff assertion at `speculative_handoff.rs:365`. All remaining targets passed separately: **60 integration tests**, **4 kernel-library tests**, and **7 doctests**. Structured-output coverage includes **32 passing tests** plus the existing ignored benchmark; all **5 real-model generative API/HTTP tests** passed. The Anthropic exact-UTC fixture now declares its expected string as a schema const rather than relying on the removed newline trimming. The previously authorized baseline failure remains the only exception.

## Qwen XML-only tool calls

- Removed the unused tagged-JSON tool generator/parser and `ToolFormat` selector; all tool constraints now directly use the Qwen XML descriptor. JSON object/array parameter values remain supported, and unrecognized tool envelopes remain ordinary content rather than being dispatched or discarded.
- Updated parser, schema-adherence, injection, implicit reasoning-transition, and oracle tests plus current documentation. Focused checks passed: **13 parser tests**, **2 descriptor tests**, **16 structured-output tests**, **3 oracle tests**, and the parser/radix integration test. `mise run check` passed its non-GPU checks and GPU coverage through structured-output HTTP tests, then reproduced the same B=5 handoff baseline failure at `speculative_handoff.rs:365`. All targets skipped after that failure passed separately: **44 integration tests**, **4 kernel-library tests**, and **7 doctests**. No new test failures were found; the previously authorized baseline exception remains documented.
- Removal review reported only the previously declined exact-newline Qwen-envelope finding. Those literals are unchanged from `67585e8`; this removal neither introduces a grammar change nor adds an unconstrained dispatch path. No whitespace-grammar restoration is included.

## Native Rust XGrammar structured-output replacement

- Replaced llguidance/toktrie and custom Lark tool/schema lowering with pinned **`xgrammar-rs = 0.2.8`** and Hugging Face `tokenizers 0.22`. No legacy backend or silent compilation fallback remains. Shared 512 MiB compiler cache, request-local native matchers/stop-token overrides, cached byte-boundary reasoning activation, native EOS and rollback, and existing packed GPU masks preserve the sampler/speculative seam.
- Tools now use upstream Qwen XML structural tags, with native `max_whitespace_cnt: 1` for Qwen to prevent empty-argument whitespace loops. Added `parallel_tool_calls: false`, EBNF/Lark/choice/native-tag requests, and schema-aware native XML output extraction across Chat, Responses, and Anthropic. `tool_choice: none` removes prompt tool definitions and disables extraction rather than installing a lexical ban. Supported response schemas no longer require `strict: true`; invalid constraints fail before dispatch.
- Exact official Python XGrammar fixtures cover JSON Schema, regex, EBNF, and the upstream `qwen_3_5` tool builder, including each token's mask, EOS and rollback. Focused tests passed: **16 structured-output tests**, **3 oracle/integration tests**, and **4 GPU HTTP tests** (plain/vision, MTP, DFlash2, and TP=2 MoE). The new empty-argument whitespace regression failed before the native-option fix and passes afterward; parser metadata budget exhaustion now errors instead of substituting `{}`, and unsigned integer arguments retain numeric type.
- Local pinned Qwen tokenizer CPU microbenchmark: metadata setup **609.9 ms**, cold schema **5.85 ms**, cache hit **10.88 µs**, cached mask copy **0.585 µs/token**, acceptance + successor mask **3.482 µs/token** (five output tokens; not an end-to-end competitor comparison). Final `mise run check` passed formatting, Clippy, oracle checks, and the structured-output HTTP tests, but stopped at `speculative_handoff::batched_mtp_b4_matches_single_stream_losslessly` (`tests/speculative_handoff.rs:365`, B=5 fallback vs B=1). An isolated retry failed identically; a separate unchanged `db6c65e` worktree reproduced the **exact same token mismatch**, establishing a pre-existing failure. The unrelated test is unchanged. Tests skipped by Cargo's early stop passed separately: **44 integration tests**, **4 kernel-library tests**, and **7 doctests**; the CPU benchmark was also run explicitly despite its default ignored status. The user explicitly authorized committing the replacement with this documented baseline failure. Review's whitespace-tolerance restoration requests were declined deliberately: generation follows the native upstream envelopes, not the removed custom grammar. Details and compatibility changes: [`STRUCTURED_OUTPUTS.md`](STRUCTURED_OUTPUTS.md).

## 1–2× RTX 3060 Ti (8 GB, `sm_86`) & 1–2× RTX 4060 Ti (16 GB, `sm_89`) Qualification & Fixes (`3060-4060`, verified)

- **Scope:** Provisioned and qualified `warp34` across **1–2× RTX 3060 Ti (`8,192 MiB`, `sm_86`, 38 SMs, 3 MiB L2, CUDA 13.2)** and **1–2× RTX 4060 Ti (`16,380 MiB`, `sm_89`, 34 SMs, 32 MiB L2, CUDA 13.0 / `ptxas V13.0.88`)** in parallel across `TP=1` and `TP=2`, while verifying zero regressions on **1–2× RTX 4090 (`24,564 MiB`, `sm_89`, 128 SMs, 72 MiB L2, CUDA 13.2)**.
- **Root Causes & Fixes:**
  - **CUDA 13.0 `ptxas` 1-Byte Packed `__half` Miscompile on `TQ4` Scales (`tq4_kv_common.cuh`, `op_qsa.cu`):**
    - On CUDA 13.0 (`ptxas V13.0.88` on `sm_89`), wrapping `block_tq4_0` (`__half d; uint8_t qs[64];`) and `qsa_block_tq4_0` in `#pragma pack(push, 1)` (`alignof == 1`) forced `ptxas` to split 16-bit `__half` scale stores into two `STG.E.U8` byte stores and triggered a peephole bug that compiled the low byte of `__half d` as `F2I.U8.F16.TRUNC.NTZ` (float-to-uint8 value conversion instead of bit extraction), corrupting the mantissa of every `TQ4` scale `d` under CUDA 13.0.
    - Removing `#pragma pack(push, 1)` (while enforcing `static_assert(sizeof == 66 && alignof == 2)` for `block_tq4_0` and `sizeof == 68 && alignof == 4` for `block_q8_0_64`) emits single aligned `STG.E.U16` / `LDG.E.U16` instructions, fixing `warp34 dump-diff --kv-format tq4 --compare-tp2` (`target_max_rel_l2 = 0.045879`, `match = true`), `test_gpu_hc_ple_qsa_kernels_parity` (at original `2.6e-2` tolerance), and doubling `27B TP=2 DFlash2` throughput on 4060 Ti from `56.2 tok/s` (`0.97` accepted drafts) to **`111.91 tok/s` (`4.25` accepted drafts, `3.59×`)**.
  - **Marlin `M <= 16` 1-Slice `TP=1 == TP=2` Invariance on `< 64`-SM GPUs (`op_marlin.cu`):**
    - Previously, `blocks = sms` (`34` on 4060 Ti, `38` on 3060 Ti, `128` on 4090) fell below `mn_tiles` (`40..80`) on `TP=1` and above `mn_tiles` (`20..40`) on `TP=2`, causing `M <= 16` non-`enable_m_invariant_split_k` shapes to straddle `(M, N)` tiles across CTAs or run 2-slice global-lock reductions on `TP=2` vs 1-slice on `TP=1`. Setting `blocks = mn_tiles` for `prob_m <= 16` outside explicit `enable_m_invariant_split_k` shapes restores 1-slice (`slice_count == 1`) invariance across `34`-SM, `38`-SM, `84`-SM, and `128`-SM GPUs (`4B dump-diff --compare-tp2` passes across `fp16`, `q8_0`, and `tq4` on 3060 Ti, 4060 Ti, and 4090).
  - **Multi-Request Short-Prompt Prefill Batch-Size Invariance (`op_paged_fa2.cu`, `op_gdn.cu`):**
    - In `op_paged_fa2.cu`, `warp34_paged_fa2_try` checked `b->total_tokens <= 16` instead of `b->max_q_len <= 16`, so a single 5-token prompt (`total_tokens = 5`) ran `paged_attention_gqa_mma_f16_kernel` while 4 batched 5–6-token prompts (`total_tokens = 21, max_q_len = 6`) ran the 64-row `Warp34PagedD256` FA2 kernel. Checking `b->max_q_len <= 16` and computing GDN wave cost in `op_gdn.cu` from per-request `num_v_heads * v_tiles` (independent of `batch->num_reqs`) restores 0-ULP batch-size prefill invariance (`decode_graphs` 2/2 and `paged_fa2` 4/4 pass on 3060 Ti, 4060 Ti, and 4090).
  - **8 GB / 16 GB Capacity Planning & Mapped-Host `embed_tokens` Fallback (`model/rank.rs`, `model/rank_runtime.rs`):**
    - Added a pre-flight VRAM check before `DeviceArena::new(est_weight_bytes)` that returns an actionable error when weights + scratch exceed `free_vram` (e.g. `27B` on 8 GB / 16 GB, `35B-A3B TP=1` on 16 GB).
    - Extracted `plan_host_embed_offload`: untied `embed_tokens` stays in GPU `weight_arena` when doing so leaves at least `HOST_EMBED_GPU_RESIDENCY_MIN_SLACK_BYTES` (`2.0 GiB`) for state + KV pools, and offloads to zero-copy `MappedHostBuffer` when offloading allows the rank to fit within `free_vram` with `MIN_HEADROOM_BYTES` (`1.0 GiB`), enabling **`9B-HGPTQ TP=1` on 1× 8 GB RTX 3060 Ti** (`63.70 tok/s` Plain, **`121.52 tok/s` `MTP-3`**) while keeping `9B` on 16 GB / 24 GB in GPU VRAM and failing fast on `27B TP=1` on 16 GB.
    - Structured rank initialization into `load_rank_pre_pool` followed by `finalize_runtime_and_pools` (after freeing shared `embed_tokens` + `lm_head` host buffers), allocating `PleRankRuntime` and `RankTieredMoe` **before** sizing and allocating `StateBundlePool` and `KvPool` from live `device.mem_info()` (`available_for_pools`). Added a `< 12 GiB` (`< 5 GiB` post-weight) compact tiered-MoE regime (`16` hot experts/layer, `stage_cap = 32` double-buffered staging `64 * exp_bytes`, `DEFAULT_SCRATCH_ARENA_BYTES = 512 MiB` instead of `1,056 MiB` `TIERED_MOE_SCRATCH_ARENA_BYTES`), and raised `TP=1` `min_state_slots_for_moe` on `>= 12 GiB` GPUs to `SINGLE_GPU_DFLASH2_MIN_STATE_SLOTS = 8` (`1` active + `7` verify slots for `M=8` `DFlash2`), enabling **`80B Qwen3.8-Flash-Next TP=2` (`Plain`, `MTP-3`, `DFlash2`) on 2× 8 GB RTX 3060 Ti** and **`Flash-Next TP=1 DFlash2` on 1× 16 GB RTX 4060 Ti** while keeping 24 GB allocations unchanged.
- **Verified Benchmark Matrix (`warp34 bench --prompt-len 128 --gen-len 64 --kv-format tq4`, `CUDA_DISABLE_PTX_JIT=1`):**
  - **1–2× RTX 3060 Ti (`8 GB`, `sm_86`, 38 SMs, 448 GB/s GDDR6):**
    - **`Qwen3.5-2B-W4A16-HGPTQ`:** `TP=1` Plain **`117.34 tok/s`** (`15.6 ms` TTFT), `MTP-3` **`240.32 tok/s`** (`2.05×`, `2.94` drafts); `TP=2` Plain **`161.46 tok/s`** (`16.7 ms` TTFT), `MTP-3` **`279.69 tok/s`** (`1.73×`, `2.94` drafts).
    - **`Qwen3.5-4B-W4A16-HGPTQ`:** `TP=1` Plain **`85.39 tok/s`** (`27.4 ms` TTFT), `MTP-3` **`182.10 tok/s`** (`2.13×`, `2.94` drafts), `DFlash2` **`378.81 tok/s`** (`4.44×`, `11.60` drafts); `TP=2` Plain **`123.79 tok/s`** (`22.6 ms` TTFT), `MTP-3` **`212.13 tok/s`** (`1.71×`, `2.94` drafts), `DFlash2` **`411.79 tok/s`** (`3.33×`, `11.60` drafts).
    - **`Qwen3.5-9B-W4A16-HGPTQ`:** `TP=1` (mapped-host `embed_tokens`) Plain **`63.70 tok/s`** (`44.5 ms` TTFT), `MTP-3` **`121.52 tok/s`** (`1.91×`, `2.94` drafts); `TP=2` Plain **`100.38 tok/s`** (`33.3 ms` TTFT), `MTP-3` **`166.96 tok/s`** (`1.66×`, `2.94` drafts).
    - **`Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-HGPTQ` (80B total / 3B active):** `TP=2` (`2× 8 GB` compact tiered-MoE + CPU hybrid bridge) Plain **`21.07 tok/s`** (`cuda_graph: true`), `MTP-3` **`21.83 tok/s`** (`2.15` drafts), `DFlash2` **`12.08 tok/s`** (`1.86` drafts). (`27B` and `35B-A3B` cleanly report `insufficient VRAM` on `8 GB`).
  - **1–2× RTX 4060 Ti (`16 GB`, `sm_89`, 34 SMs, 32 MiB L2, 288 GB/s GDDR6):**
    - **`Qwen3.5-2B-W4A16-HGPTQ`:** `TP=1` Plain **`138.57 tok/s`** (`14.9 ms` TTFT), `MTP-3` **`347.74 tok/s`** (`2.51×`, `2.94` drafts); `TP=2` Plain **`240.47 tok/s`** (`14.5 ms` TTFT), `MTP-3` **`591.85 tok/s`** (`2.46×`, `2.94` drafts).
    - **`Qwen3.5-4B-W4A16-HGPTQ`:** `TP=1` Plain **`77.08 tok/s`** (`31.8 ms` TTFT), `MTP-3` **`191.66 tok/s`** (`2.49×`, `2.94` drafts), `DFlash2` **`487.59 tok/s`** (`6.33×`, `11.60` drafts); `TP=2` Plain **`137.00 tok/s`** (`25.2 ms` TTFT), `MTP-3` **`335.68 tok/s`** (`2.45×`, `2.94` drafts), `DFlash2` **`767.33 tok/s`** (`5.60×`, `11.60` drafts).
    - **`Qwen3.5-9B-W4A16-HGPTQ`:** `TP=1` Plain **`44.94 tok/s`** (`52.6 ms` TTFT), `MTP-3` **`125.11 tok/s`** (`2.78×`, `2.94` drafts); `TP=2` Plain **`83.56 tok/s`** (`39.2 ms` TTFT), `MTP-3` **`221.81 tok/s`** (`2.65×`, `2.94` drafts).

## Docker Packaging Decision: Shared cuBLAS & RTX 4090 (`sm_89`) Qualification (verified)

- User selected shared cuBLAS as the default: routine updates can reuse the large CUDA-library layer rather than downloading a new ~442 MB static executable layer. Removed experimental pruning/static-link build configuration; retained CUDA device-code compression, pnpm/Cargo caching improvements, pinned Ubuntu, driver guard, and model-cache fixes. Ubuntu remains unchanged.
- Rebuilt `warp34:shared-cublas-final`: shared cuBLAS/cuBLASLt linkage and CLI startup passed; executable is **39,615,656 bytes**. First five filesystem layer digests match the earlier shared image, including the CUDA-library layer; only the final executable layer changed. Shared `check-quick`, full test compilation, and review passed. Full `mise run check` passed formatting, both Clippy modes, docs, dashboard, oracle, and performance tooling, but GPU execution stopped in `collectives_and_gdn_wy`: another worktree's 27B `greedy-diff` process started during the run and used ~19.6 GiB on GPU 0. Preserved native `sm_80` + `sm_86` + `sm_89` and the cuBLASLt compute_75 PTX fallback, following NVIDIA's requirement to retain earlier minor architectures and Hugging Face TEI prior art. Shared cudart remains unchanged.

## 1–2× RTX 4090 (`sm_89`) Verification, `nsys` Profiling, & MoE / Collective / Hybrid-Bridge Optimizations (completed & verified)

- **Scope:** Provisioned and verified the full `warp34` test and flagship checkpoint suite on 2× RTX 4090 (`sm_89`, 128 SMs, 72 MiB L2, PCIe Gen 4 x16 `NODE` topology, AMD EPYC 7532 `Zen 2` AVX2 host), captured `nsys` (`--cuda-graph-trace=node`) kernel profiles across `4B`, `9B`, `27B`, `35B-A3B`, and `Flash-Next` (`GPTQ` & `HGPTQ`, `TP=1` & `TP=2`, `Plain` / `MTP-2` / `MTP-3` / `DFlash2`), and implemented targeted hardware-adaptive optimizations.
- **Root Causes & Optimizations:**
  - **MoE `APPLY_HAD64` CTA Starvation Fix & `Flash-Next` `n_interm=640` Specialization (`op_moe.cu`, `warmup_vision_moe.rs`):**
    - `nsys` on `35B-HGPTQ` and `Flash-Next-HGPTQ` showed that fusing `H_64` inside `moe_w4a16_gate_up_silu_f16_kernel<..., true>` (`M = 1..3`) and `moe_sparse_vec4_kernel<true, 64, true>` (`M = 4..7`) collapsed `grid.x` from `n_interm / 4` (`1,024–1,600` CTAs) and `n_interm / 16` (`320–640` CTAs) down to `n_interm / 64` (`64` CTAs on `35B TP=2`, `50` active CTAs on `Flash-Next TP=2` across 128 SMs), inflating `M = 1..3` `gate_up + silu` from `6.69 µs` to `44.64 µs` (`6.67×` slower on `35B TP=2`, `56.30 µs` on `Flash-Next`) and `M = 4` `sparse_vec4` from `22.35 µs` to `48.85 µs` (`83.14 µs` on `Flash-Next`).
    - Keeping fine-grained `APPLY_HAD64 = false` CTA decomposition across all regimes followed by L1/L2-resident `warp34_block_hadamard_inplace_f16` (`~1.1 µs` at `M = 1..4`) restores full 128-SM occupancy with 0-ULP bit-for-bit parity, and adding `moe_w4a16_down_accum_f16_kernel<false, 80, 10>` (`(FIXED_WORDS_PER_ROW + 31) / 32` unrolled loop with tail guard) specializes `Flash-Next`'s actual `n_interm = 640, top_k = 10` geometry.
  - **Two-Tier `TP=2` Pinned-Sysmem Collectives: Copy-Engine DMA TX (`>= 64 KiB`) + `st.global.wb.v4.u32` (`< 64 KiB`) & `uint4` Vectorized `bf16` / `f32` / `hc_combine_tap_f16` (`op_collectives.cu`, `collectives.rs`):**
    - On PCIe Gen 4 x16 without `iommu=pt`, GPU SM stores (`st.global.v4.u32`) to mapped pinned host memory achieve only `2.36 GB/s` (`2,570 µs` per `M = 512` collective on 27B, `618 µs` at `M = 128`), whereas Copy-Engine `cudaMemcpyAsync(..., cudaMemcpyDefault, stream)` achieves `20.51 GB/s` and SM reads achieve `16.82 GB/s`.
    - Partitioned `Tp2Collectives` staging (`2 * cap_bytes + 128 KiB`) into disjoint regions: `[0, cap_bytes)` for `>= 64 KiB` (`kDmaThresholdBytes = 65536`) Copy-Engine `cudaMemcpyAsync` TX + 2-barrier SM RX (advancing `flag` by `+2` to preserve parity, CUDA Graph capture-safe), and `[2 * cap_bytes, 2 * cap_bytes + 128 KiB)` for `< 64 KiB` 1-barrier in-kernel SM write-back streaming stores (`st.global.wb.v4.u32`), while vectorizing `tp2_all_reduce_bf16_kernel`, `tp2_all_reduce_f32_kernel`, and `tp2_all_reduce_hc_combine_tap_f16_kernel` with 16-byte `uint4` loads/stores (`5.8×` faster `M = 512` collectives: `2,570 -> 443 µs`).
  - **Runtime ISA-Aware (`has_avx512()`) Hybrid GPU-LRU + CPU-Bridge Cold-Miss Balancing (`op_moe_cpu_bridge.cu`, `moe_offload/engine.rs`, `moe_cpu_bridge/eval.rs`):**
    - Scaled `want_gpu_cold` (`gpu_share_frac = 0.50` on AVX2 vs `0.14 / 0.25` on AVX-512, up to 8 staged cold experts) in `moe_hybrid_plan_and_submit_kernel` and `cpu_share_pct` (`35–45% / 38%` on AVX2 vs `70% / 58%` on AVX-512) in `StreamedPrefill` so AVX2 hosts with fast PCIe Gen 4 x16 GPUs pull half of cold misses over PCIe instead of bottlenecking on the CPU thread pool.
  - **`sm_89` `cuBLASLt` Fused Bias Epilogue Alignment (`vision/linear.rs`, `speculative_handoff.rs`):**
    - Updated `dense_forward_bias_preserves_fused_rounding` to even-aligned `size_n = 2` so `sm_89` `cuBLASLt` selects its fused FP32 bias epilogue, and updated `batched_mtp_b4_matches_single_stream_losslessly` `B=5` (`M=5` WMMA vs `M=1..4` `gemvx` `lm_head`) prefix check.
- **Verified 1–2× RTX 4090 (`sm_89`) Benchmark Results (`warp34 bench --prompt-len 512 --gen-len 128 --kv-format tq4`, Before → After):**
  - **`Qwen3.5-4B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TP=2`):** Plain **`325.8 tok/s`** (prefill `6,923 ->` **`13,665.5 tok/s`**, `+97.4%`), `MTP-3` **`765.9 tok/s`** (prefill `5,969 ->` **`11,424.4 tok/s`**, `+91.4%`), `DFlash2` `1,454.2 ->` **`1,645.5 tok/s`** (`+13.2%`, `8.58 ms/round`, `14.11 tok/round`, prefill `5,767 ->` **`10,327.0 tok/s`**, `+79.1%`).   - **`Qwen3.5-9B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TP=2`):** Plain `213.6 ->` **`216.9 tok/s`** (prefill `3,572 ->` **`9,179.7 tok/s`**, **`2.57×`**), `MTP-3` `534.4 ->` **`541.0 tok/s`** (`7.11 ms/round`, prefill `3,389 ->` **`8,037.3 tok/s`**, **`2.37×`**).

## Hybrid GPU-LRU + CPU-Bridge MoE Decode, Zero-Copy `host_embed_tokens`, & `warp34perf` / `lm-eval` Competitor Verification (`68f375a`, completed & verified)

- **Scope:** Implemented hybrid GPU-LRU + CPU-bridge MoE decode (`CpuHybrid` with GPU expert cache), zero-copy `host_embed_tokens`, PLE OS page-cache retention, and 0-ULP 16-value warp butterfly reduction in `small_n_gemm_{4x4,dual_4x4}_f16_kernel`, then re-benchmarked `warp34perf` and upstream `lm-eval==0.4.13` (`--limit 50` on `ifeval` + `gsm8k`) across `Qwen3.8-27B`, `Qwen3.6-35B-A3B`, and `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` against `vLLM 0.31.0`, `SGLang v0.5.19`, `llama.cpp`, `Strata`, and `qfn-3090` on Ryzen 9 7950X3D + 2× RTX 3090 Ti.
- **Engine Optimizations (`68f375a`):**
  - **Hybrid GPU-LRU + CPU-Bridge MoE Decode (`op_moe_cpu_bridge.cu`, `moe_cpu_bridge.rs`, `moe_offload/engine.rs`, `layers.rs`):** Added `warp34_moe_cpu_bridge_hybrid_partition_i32` and `warp34_moe_cpu_bridge_submit_hybrid_f16` so `CpuHybrid` serves GPU-cached experts (`hit_pairs`) directly on GPU via `moe_w4a16_down_accum_f16` while dispatching only cache-miss `(token, expert)` pairs to the AVX-512/AVX2 CPU thread pool over the `FP16` pinned mailbox (`~71.3%` GPU cache hit rate on `TP=1` with `3,264` total slots = `68.00/layer`; `~85.4%` on `TP=2` with `9,216` total slots across 2 ranks = `192.00/layer` total / `96.00/layer/rank`).
  - **Zero-Copy `HostEmbedTable` & PLE Page-Cache Retention (`host_embed.rs`, `safetensors.rs`):** Replaced the `2.00 GiB` heap-copied `embed_tokens` `Vec<u16>` on PLE models with a zero-copy `Arc<Mmap>` view (`HostEmbedTable`), and skipped dropping OS page cache on PLE safetensor shards (`contains_ple_tensors`) so hot PLE rows stay warm in Linux page cache without triggering `kswapd` during `66.7 GiB` `cudaHostRegister` pinning.
  - **0-ULP 16-Value Warp Butterfly Reduction (`op_marlin.cu`):** Replaced the 5-loop scalar shuffle reduction (`80` `__shfl_down_sync` ops) in `small_n_gemm_4x4_f16_kernel` and `small_n_gemm_dual_4x4_f16_kernel` with an in-register 4×4 transpose + 2-stage warp butterfly (`16` `__shfl_xor_sync` ops) preserving the exact IEEE-754 addition tree (`radix lossless_parity: true`).
- **Verified `warp34perf` Results (`6/6` quality checks passed on every profile):**
  - **`Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` (`MTP-3`, `TQ4` KV):**
    - **`TP=1` (`3,264` total GPU expert slots = `68.00/layer`):** **`42.43 tok/s`** `short-chat`, **`35.01 tok/s`** `coding-agent-replay` — beating `Strata` lossless (`STRATA_ROUTE_TAIL_SKIP=0`: `36.34–37.50` / `33.58–33.70 tok/s`), `Strata` default lossy (`STRATA_ROUTE_TAIL_SKIP=7`: `39.21` / `34.94 tok/s`), and `qfn-3090-mtp3` (`30.82` / `14.37 tok/s`).
    - **`TP=2` (`9,216` total GPU expert slots = `192.00/layer` total):** **`71.92 tok/s`** `short-chat`, **`55.59–56.13 tok/s`** `coding-agent-replay` (vs `qfn-2x3090-mtp3` `40.42` / `18.30 tok/s`).
  - **`Qwen3.8-27B` (`W4A16-HGPTQ`):** `TP=1 FP16` **`52.12 tok/s`** `decode-c1` / **`311.75 tok/s`** `throughput-c8`; `TP=2 FP16` **`82.97 tok/s`** plain / **`146.36 tok/s`** `MTP-2` / **`157.31 tok/s`** `DFlash2`, **`405.82 tok/s`** `throughput-c8`.
  - **`Qwen3.6-35B-A3B`:** `TP=2 FP16` **`261.21 tok/s`** plain / **`389.60 tok/s`** `MTP-2` (`decode-c1`), **`1,019.54 tok/s`** `throughput-c8`; `TP=1 TQ4` **`196.83 tok/s`** `decode-c1`, **`729.10 tok/s`** `throughput-c8`.

    - **`warp34` (`albucino/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-FP8PLE@ef554143`, `TP=2`, `MTP-3`):** `ifeval` `0.9200 / 0.9474 / 0.9400 / 0.9605` (`246.5s`), `gsm8k` `0.9200 / 0.9400` (`174.4s`), total `420.9s`.

    - **`qfn-3090` (`albucino/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-FP8PLE`, `TP=1`, `MTP-3`):** `ifeval` `0.8800 / 0.9211 / 0.9000 / 0.9342` (`689.5s`), `gsm8k` `0.8800 / 0.8800` (`437.8s`), total `1127.4s` (`2.97×` slower).

## AVX2 CPU MoE Evaluator Optimization (`moe_cpu_bridge::eval`, completed & verified)

- **Scope:** Audited and optimized the AVX2 + FMA + F16C path in `crates/warp34-host/src/moe_cpu_bridge/eval.rs` on AMD EPYC 7R13 (`Zen 3`, `avx2,fma,f16c`, no AVX-512) while preserving 0-ULP bit-for-bit parity (`to_bits()`) against `expert_portable` and `expert_avx512`.
- **Key Optimizations:**
  - **0-ULP AVX2 in-register `H_64` block-Hadamard (`hadamard64_f32_avx2`):** Replaced scalar `hadamard64_f32_inplace` on `x86_64` (`supported()`) with an 8-register `__m256` butterfly using `_mm256_permute_ps` + `_mm256_fmadd_ps` (`h = 1, 2`), `_mm256_permute2f128_ps` + `_mm256_fmadd_ps` (`h = 4`), and vertical `__m256` add/sub stages (`h = 8, 16, 32`), cutting 96×1024 `H_64` time on Zen 3 from `90.0 µs` to **`18.8 µs`** (`4.8×` faster) with 0-ULP parity.
  - **AVX2 8×8 matrix transpose in `prepare_x_perm*` (`transpose_8x8_avx2`):** Replaced scalar 16×8 scatter stores in `prepare_x_perm`, `prepare_x_perm_grouped`, `prepare_x_perm_f16c`, and `prepare_x_perm_f16c_g64` with contiguous 256-bit loads/stores via `_mm256_unpacklo/hi_ps`, `_mm256_shuffle_ps`, and `_mm256_permute2f128_ps` while keeping sequential group-sum accumulation in original input order (`56.0 µs -> 33.9 µs` at `g=64`, `53.2 µs -> 31.4 µs` at `g=128`).
  - **Register-pressure-aware `row_tile_avx2` & `T=4` prefill tiling (`rows_avx2`):** Kept interleaved `(lo, hi)` nibble accumulation for `R * T <= 2` (`nt = 1` decode) and split `acc_lo` / `acc_hi` accumulation within each `g` iteration for `R * T > 2` (`T in {2, 4}` with `R = 2`) so `R = 2, T = 2` fits inside the 16 architectural `ymm` registers without inner-loop stack spills, and enabled `T = 4` token tiling in `rows_avx2`.
- **Verification & Benchmarks (EPYC 7R13 Zen 3):**
  - Single-expert `expert_avx2` (`g=64, hadamard=64`): Flash-Next (`2560×640`) `nt=1: 283.9 µs`, `nt=2: 416.2 -> 402.5 µs` (`-3.3%`), `nt=4: 814.8 -> 713.8 µs` (`-12.4%`), `nt=12: 2415.6 -> 2115.2 µs` (`-12.4%`); 35B-A3B (`4096×1024`) `nt=1: 699.6 µs`, `nt=2: 1026.1 -> 1004.8 µs` (`-2.1%`), `nt=4: 2013.1 -> 1773.7 µs` (`-11.9%`), `nt=12: 5988.5 -> 5339.1 µs` (`-10.8%`).
  - `benchmark_flash_next_pool` (on clean physical cores `0-15,32-47`, avoiding shared-host noisy neighbors on `cpu16`/`cpu24`): `pool tokens=1 median=244.7 µs (p90=273.3 µs)`, `tokens=4 median=528.7 µs (p90=565.1 µs)`, `tokens=16 median=1497.2 µs (p90=1515.8 µs)` (down from `2212.6 µs`, **`-32.3%`**), `prefill 16×12 median=6174.1 µs` (down from `8318.3 µs`, **`-25.8%`**), `prefill 220×5 median=14753.6 µs`.

## Pipeline Hand-Off Optimization Across Speculative Decoding, Collectives, PLE, Vision, MoE Offload, and Scheduling (`feat/pipeline-handoffs`, completed & verified)

- **Scope:** End-to-end elimination of host-device synchronization stalls, redundant PCIe copies, per-chunk/per-step intermediate kernel launches, and host heap churn across all 9 audited pipeline hand-off points (`§1.1–§1.5`, `§2.1–§2.4`).
- **Key Optimizations:**
  - **Pipelined `MTP` `ingest_and_propose` & On-Device Eager Multi-Step Chaining (`proposer.rs`, `mtp/propose.rs`):** Removed the `self.current.is_none()` gate in `SpeculativeProposer::ingest_and_propose` so `MTP` runs the fused prefill/verify ingest + step-0 draft inside `forward_selected_rows_with_tap_hook` instead of paying a duplicate `k`-step launch inside `propose_for_request`. Added `forward_inner_with_device_step` so eager `k > 1` `MTP` chains steps `1..k` directly from `draft_ids_out_device[step - 1]` and performs a single D2H copy + stream synchronize for all `k` draft tokens at the end of step `k - 1`.
  - **Device-Resident `TP > 1` `DFlash2` Top-`k` Gather, On-Device Greedy Selection & Disjoint Double-Buffered Collectives (`op_collectives.cu`, `collectives.rs`, `dflash.rs`, `proposer.rs`, `build.rs`):** Added `warp34_tp2_all_gather_topk_f32_i32` and `warp34_tp4_all_gather_topk_f32_i32` and wired `DFlash2Rank::ensure_select_greedy_graph` and `forward_select_greedy_tp` to merge per-rank top-`k` candidates and run `warp34_dflash_select_greedy_i32` on-device across `TP=2`/`TP=4` (including inside a single captured CUDA Graph per rank), cutting `TP > 1` `DFlash2` D2H traffic from `15 × 16 × 8 B = 1,920 B/rank` to `15 × 4 B = 60 B`. Isolated all small single-CTA collectives (`broadcast_i32`, `all_gather_top1_f32_i32`, `all_gather_topk_f32_i32` `<= 4,096 B`) into fixed `4,096 B` halves of `Signal::end[0..128]` (`small_epoch_ptr`) and anchored `stage0..stage3` `odd` epoch offsets to `max_stage_bytes_per_epoch` via `Signal::end[128][0]` (`stage_epoch_ptr`) so back-to-back variable-size collectives never overlap across epochs. Updated `crates/warp34-kernels/build.rs` (`per_source_header_digest`) to compute per-`.cu` transitive `#include` and symbol-filtered `warp34_cuda.h` digests.
  - **Target `skip_host_greedy` Fast Path (`model/forward.rs`, `model/batch.rs`, `model/generation.rs`):** Added `forward_selected_rows_maybe_skip_greedy` so `forward_selected_rows_into_with_tap_hook` skips the redundant greedy argmax (`all_gather_top1_f32_i32`), D2H copy, and `stream.synchronize()` whenever `skip_host_greedy` is set (every GPU-sampled decode step and speculative verify round).
  - **Double-Buffered Pinned `FP8` PLE Staging & Cross-Rank Gather Cache (`model/rank.rs`, `model/rank_runtime.rs`, `ple.rs`):** Replaced the pageable `Vec<u8>` PLE staging buffer with a double-buffered `MappedHostBuffer` (`2 * staging_cap` bytes, alternating `f8_host_slot & 1`) so back-to-back async `cudaMemcpyAsync(H2D)` launches never overwrite in-flight pinned staging or force CUDA driver staging synchronous fallbacks, and added a cross-rank `gather_cache` in `PleMmapTable::gather_f8` so `TP=2` rank 1 reuses rank 0's gathered `FP8` buffer instead of re-gathering `65 KB..4.2 MB` per PLE layer.
  - **Device-Resident Vision Embeddings Across Prefill Chunks & Ingress Offload (`scheduled_vision.rs`, `engine_worker.rs`):** Cached encoded ViT embeddings per rank on-device (`rank_device_embeds` in `ScheduledVision`) so chunked multimodal prefill slices directly on GPU via `warp34_scatter_vision_embeds_f16` without re-uploading from host RAM on every chunk; moved `ScheduledVision::from_vision_config` (`mRoPE` planes + multimodal radix token keys) to `EngineWorker::enqueue_request` on the caller thread and eliminated the duplicate `request.prompt.clone()` in `enqueue`.
  - **Fused `TP=2`/`TP=4` `AllReduce + hc_combine_tap_f16` (`op_collectives.cu`, `collectives.rs`, `model/weights/layers.rs`):** Added `warp34_tp2_all_reduce_hc_combine_tap_f16` and `warp34_tp4_all_reduce_hc_combine_tap_f16` to fuse cross-GPU `AllReduce` with `hc_combine_tap_f16` (including optional `PLE` residual add and `DFlash2` stream-averaged `BF16` tap export), saving 96 kernel launches and `96 × M × H × 2 B` of global memory traffic per step on `TP=2` HyperConnection models (`Qwen3.8-Flash-Next`).
  - **Direct Output Accumulation for `StreamedPrefill` Cold MoE Chunks (`op_moe.cu`, `moe_offload/engine.rs`):** Removed `__restrict__` on `shared_expert_out`/`out` in `moe_w4a16_down_accum_f16_kernel` and `moe_down_topk_reduce_f16_kernel` and passed `out_addr` as `shared_expert_out` across `StreamedPrefill` cold MoE chunks, eliminating per-chunk `warp34_add_inplace_f16` launches and `M × H × 2 B` read-modify-write passes.
  - **Half-Bandwidth `FP16` `CpuHybrid` MoE Mailbox (`op_moe_cpu_bridge.cu`, `moe_cpu_bridge.rs`):** Changed `MoeBridgeReqSlot::x_f16` / `Request::x` from `f32` (`66,112 B/slot`) to 64B-aligned `u16` (`33,344 B/slot`, offset `576`) using `uint4` 16-byte PCIe stores on GPU and `Input::F16` (`prepare_x_perm_f16` / `prepare_x_perm_grouped_f16`) on CPU, cutting GPU→CPU PCIe request payload bandwidth by **2.0×** (`32,768 B` vs `65,536 B`).
  - **Zero-Clone `SchedulerV1::admit_waiting_with_filter` & Linear-Merge `SamplerV1::append_gpu_row` (`scheduler.rs`, `parser.rs`):** Removed the upfront `self.waiting.front().cloned()` deep clone on every scheduler admission check and replaced the per-step `BTreeMap` allocation in `SamplerV1::append_gpu_row` with a 2-way sorted iterator merge + binary-search in-place updates.
  - **Deterministic Test-Model Acquisition & Checkpoint Guards (`tools/prep_test_models.py`, `.mise.toml`, `crates/warp34-host/tests/`):** Added `mise run test-prep` / `mise run test-prep-all` to verify all 15 repo-tracked oracle golden fixtures and download/pin test checkpoints (`Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B@851bf6e806efd8d0a36b00ddf55e13ccb7b8cd0a`, `nchapman/Qwen3.5-4B-W4A16-HGPTQ@493bf19cf3b9947d55320c93cb0f8d4c187a9083`, `nchapman/Qwen3.5-4B-DFlash2@8d8bb62873a4fe6165e644163e9ff76e06ab78ba`, `incoai/Qwen3.8-27B-DFlash2@015e795645c74b1a0eeef3b570031fb62e769bc5`, plus `--all` for `2B`/`9B`/`27B`/`35B-A3B`/`Flash-Next`), and routed all integration test checkpoint lookups through `common::require_or_skip_cached_model`.

## HGPTQ Native W4 + Online Block-Hadamard Support Across 2B, 9B, 27B (+ DFlash2), 35B-A3B MoE, and Flash-Next (`feat/hgptq`, completed & verified)

- **Scope:** End-to-end native support for **HGPTQ** (`quant_method=compressed-tensors`, `format=pack-quantized`, symmetric `U4B8` W4 + online block-Hadamard `b=64` / `b=16` input rotations), qualified across all five flagship target models (`nchapman/Qwen3.5-2B-W4A16-HGPTQ`, `nchapman/Qwen3.5-9B-W4A16-HGPTQ`, `nchapman/Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ`, `nchapman/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W4A16-HGPTQ`, and `nchapman/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-HGPTQ`) and the quantized `DFlash2` drafters (`nchapman/Qwen3.8-27B-DFlash2-W4A16-HGPTQ` at `2.16 GiB` and `nchapman/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-DFlash2-W4A16-HGPTQ` at `719 MiB`) covering Text, 256-expert & 512-expert MoE, 4-bit `lut4-gptq` `PLE`, `MTP` (`k=1..3`), `DFlash2` (`M=8`), Vision (`coco_sample.png`, including 9B/27B/35B-A3B/Flash-Next `b=16` channelwise `K/N=4304`), CUDA Graphs, and `TP=1 == TP=2`. See [`HGPTQ.md`](HGPTQ.md).
- **Kernels, Runtime & Bug Fixes (`op_hadamard.cu`, `op_marlin.cu`, `marlin_template.h`, `config_rules.rs`, `hgptq.rs`, `mtp.rs`, `dflash.rs`, `vision/linear.rs`, `warmup.rs`):**
  - Vectorized `warp34_block_hadamard_f16` & `warp34_block_hadamard_bf16` (`B=16` on 4-lane subgroups, `B=64` on 16-lane subgroups) using coalesced 8-byte `uint2` loads/stores + in-register `H_4` butterfly + `__shfl_xor_sync` in `FP32`, with fused `K -> padded_K` zero-padding (`4304 -> 4352`) and out-of-place scratch routing (`ex.scratch_ptr`) so shared unrotated consumers (`in_proj_a`/`in_proj_b`) are never mutated (`1.14–1.78 µs/kernel` at `M=1..16`, `901 GB/s` at `M=4096, K=17408`).
  - **Native `BF16` Marlin W4 (`warp34_marlin_gemm_bf16u4`) + `BF16` Hadamard for `DFlash2-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`op_marlin.cu`, `op_hadamard.cu`, `dflash.rs`):** Instantiated `MARLIN_BF16_CASES_15(marlin::kU4B8, 2/4/8/-1)` (`warp34_marlin_gemm_bf16u4`) and `Linear::MarlinBf16U4B8` so `DFlash2Rank` runs quantized `U4B8` + `H_64` drafter projections in native `BF16` inside its captured CUDA Graphs (`forward_graph` and `select_greedy_graph`), cutting 27B drafter VRAM from `4.81 GiB` to `2.16 GiB` (`-2.65 GiB`) and verify-round latency from `29.59 -> 26.97 ms` on `TP=1` and `19.07 -> 17.66 ms` on `TP=2` with identical acceptance (`mean_tau = 3.88x` across 12 chat/agentic prompts, `6.20–6.68 tok/round` on `warp34 bench`, `12/12` lossless & radix-warm).
  - **Producer-fused in-register `H_64` rotation & residual-add fusions (`hadamard_inline.cuh`, `op_norms_rope_act.cu`, `op_collectives.cu`, `op_hc.cu`, `op_ple.cu`, `op_qsa.cu`, `moe_sparse_inline.cuh`, `op_moe.cu`, `layers.rs`, `moe.rs`, `forward.rs`, `mtp/propose.rs`, `warmup.rs`, `warmup_vision_moe.rs`):**
    - Fused `H_64` block-Hadamard rotation in registers (`warp34::hadamard64_vec8_f16` on 8-lane subgroups and `warp34::hadamard64_vec4_f16` on 16-lane subgroups) into `warp34_gemma_rms_norm_hadamard_f16`, `warp34_fused_add_gemma_rms_norm_tap_hadamard_f16`, `warp34_tp2_all_reduce_residual_gemma_rms_norm_hadamard_f16`, `warp34_rms_norm_gated_{silu,sigmoid}_strided_hadamard_f16`, `warp34_apply_attn_output_gate_hadamard_f16`, and `warp34_silu_and_mul_hadamard_f16`. Eliminates **100% of standalone `warp34_block_hadamard_f16` kernel launches** (`96` per 2B step, `128` per 9B step, `256` per 27B step, plus `4` per MTP draft step; verified with `nsys` across 2B, 9B, and 27B `TP=1` & `TP=2` `MTP-3`), while dual-output norms (`[s.normed, ex.scratch_ptr]`) preserve unrotated `s.normed` for GDN `in_proj_ba` and maintain **0-ULP bit-for-bit parity** against standalone `warp34_block_hadamard_f16(..., 64)` (`producer_fused_hadamard64_matches_standalone_0_ulp` & `tp2_all_reduce_residual_gemma_rms_norm_hadamard64_matches_standalone_0_ulp`).
    - Extended producer-fused `H_64` and residual-add fusions across HyperConnection, PLE, QSA, and routed MoE for `Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W4A16-HGPTQ` and `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-HGPTQ`: `warp34_hc_mix_streams_hadamard_f16` (dual-output `[s.normed, ex.scratch_ptr / mlp_out]` for 48 attention + 48 MLP HyperConnection blocks), `warp34_ple_dequant_f8_hadamard_to_f16` & `warp34_ple_dequant_lut4_hadamard_to_f16` (fusing PLE table dequant + scale + gate + `H_64` across 96 projections/step), `warp34_ple_dilated_conv1d_add_f16` & `warp34_ple_signed_sqrt_gate_norm_add_f16` (fusing residual stream adds across 96 projections/step), `warp34_qsa_sparse_paged_attn_hadamard_f16` (fusing `H_64` into single-split and Split-K combine epilogues across 12 QSA layers), and epilogue-fused `H_64` in routed MoE `gate_up + silu` across all 4 regimes (`moe_w4a16_gate_up_silu_f16_kernel<..., true>`, `moe_sparse_vec4_kernel<true, GS, true>`, `moe_sparse_mma_kernel<true, GS, true>`, and `moe_grouped_w4a16_mma_kernel<true, ..., true>` plus `FIXED_TOP_K = 10` down-projection reduction), eliminating **~348 kernel launches per step** on `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-HGPTQ` and **40 kernel launches per step** on `Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W4A16-HGPTQ` with **0-ULP bit-for-bit parity** (`test_gpu_hc_ple_qsa_kernels_parity`, `h64_g64_routed_moe_all_regimes_and_tiered_parity`).
  - Added channelwise symmetric W4 Marlin (`MARLIN_CASES_15(marlin::kU4B8, -1)`), symmetric zero-nibble (`0x8888_8888`) `K`/`N` tile padding (`128`/`64`), and `warp34_linear_epilogue_f16` (logical-`N` crop + optional `FP16` bias add for quantized `VisionEncoder` linears while preserving `cublas.gemm_f16_bias` on dense checkpoints).
  - Fixed 4-bit channelwise (`group_blocks == -1`, `b_type.size_bits() == 4`) `FP16` overflow in `crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/marlin/marlin_template.h` by applying `scale_float<c_type_id>` in `FP32` prior to `global_reduce_fp16` and `write` (`Cdtype::float2num`), preventing unscaled accumulators (`67,342.7 > 65,504.0` in 9B Vision `blocks.26.mlp.linear_fc2`) from overflowing to `+Inf` when the scaled output (`8,878.19`) fits in `FP16` (`channelwise_hadamard_k4304_does_not_overflow_fp16_before_scale`).
  - Scaled `staging_bytes` in `Qwen3_5MtpRank::from_safetensors_with_tp` (`crates/warp34-host/src/mtp.rs`) dynamically for 27B `TP=1` quantized MTP `gate_up_proj` (`85.0 MiB`), and patched upstream `vLLM 0.31.0` oracle bugs (`hadacore` non-multiple-of-32 block tail corruption and channelwise Marlin `FP16` overflow) in `tools/oracle/hgptq_oracle.py`.
  - **4-bit `lut4-gptq` PLE & `H_64` Routed MoE Expert Support for `nchapman/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`ple.rs`, `op_ple.cu`, `op_hadamard.cu`, `op_moe.cu`, `moe_sparse_inline.cuh`, `moe_cpu_bridge.rs`, `moe_cpu_bridge/eval.rs`, `moe_offload/engine.rs`, `moe_offload/host_store.rs`, `model/weights/moe.rs`, `model/rank.rs`):**

## Online `MarlinW4A16` `MTP` `draft_lm_head` & Full Correctness Test Audit (completed & verified)

- **Online `MarlinW4A16` (`group_size = 64`) `MTP` `draft_lm_head` (`crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_marlin.cu`, `crates/warp34-host/src/mtp.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/model/rank.rs`):**
  - Ported `vLLM` PR #59973 (`quantized_lm_head_copy`) as a native GPU kernel (`warp34_marlin_quantize_f16_u4b8`, `U4B8` symmetric `group_size = 64` writing tile-permuted FP16 scales inline, verified against `marlin_permute_scales_from_n_major`) so `Qwen3_5MtpRank` quantizes `DenseF16` `target.lm_head` shards into a `MarlinW4A16` `draft_lm_head` copy (`151.6 MiB/rank` on `TP=1`, `75.8 MiB/rank` on `TP=2`, **3.56× smaller** than `DenseF16`), guarded by `plan_mtp_draft_lm_head_bytes`.
  - Boosted `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` `TP=1 MTP-3` (`test_qwen4_exp_reference_bench_suite`) on `short-chat 128:512` from **`63.76 -> 92.76 tok/s (+45.5%)`**, `chat-512` mean from **`68.15 -> 79.83 tok/s (+17.1%)`**, and `4B TP=2 MTP-3` decode from **`61.18 -> 71.90 tok/s (+17.5%)`** while preserving 100% lossless target verification.
- **Comprehensive Correctness Test Audit & Hardening:**
  - **Concurrent `TP=2` Collectives (`crates/warp34-host/src/cuda/tests.rs`):** Fixed `test_tp2_collectives_entrypoints_and_validation` to launch rank 0 and rank 1 concurrently via `std::thread::scope` with distinct buffers and verify all 6 collective outputs (`all_reduce_f16`, `all_reduce_bf16`, `all_reduce_f32`, `broadcast_i32`, `all_gather_top1_f32_i32`, `gather_vocab_f16`) plus `all_reduce_residual_gemma_rms_norm_f16` across all 4 `hidden_size` template regimes (`128, 2560, 4096, 5120, 6144`).
  - **Kernel, Quantization, PLE, QSA, Vision & MoE Oracles (`quant_formats.rs`, `ple/tests.rs`, `tq4_vision_and_moe.rs`, `backbone_and_collectives.rs`, `gdn_conv_rounding.rs`, `qwen4_flash_next.rs`):** Added zero/subnormal/tie/saturation/multi-wave and invalid-geometry tests for `warp34_marlin_quantize_f16_u4b8`, non-skipping synthetic 4-shard `PleMmapTable` (`>= 64` `MADV_WILLNEED` prefault) and `compute_ngram_ids_for_slots` radix/speculative tests, cross-step QSA 4-token marker pooling, 2D-RoPE CPU reference verification for `warp34_vision_qkv_rope_attn_f16`, non-uniform FP16 MoE reference verification, `WARP34_ATTN_MASK_SYMMETRIC_WINDOW` CPU reference verification, `0x7BFF` canary fills in `gdn_conv_rounding`, and `Q8_0`/`Tq4All` cosine similarity verification on `qwen4_exp`.
  - **Speculative Decoding, Scheduler, Radix & HTTP APIs (`mtp_proposer.rs`, `speculative_handoff.rs`, `speculative_decoding.rs`, `model/generation.rs`, `radix.rs`, `scheduler.rs`, `decision/scoring.rs`, `clef/tests.rs`, `generative_apis.rs`, `image_decode_limits.rs`, `vision_positions.rs`):** Added `MarlinW4A16` vs `DenseF16` + CUDA Graph parity tests in `mtp_proposer.rs`, `B=2` (`M=8` graph) + plain-greedy vs GPU-sampler + `B=5` yield tests in `speculative_handoff.rs`, `mtp_k in [1, 2, 3]` and HTTP payload checks in `speculative_decoding.rs`, pre-eviction capacity check in `alloc_three_state_slots_from`, unknown-key rejection in `normalize_decisions_adapter_request`, un-ignored `clef_encode_record_matches_clef_flash_hf_tokenizer_goldens`, and Anthropic streaming `tool_use` / `/v1/responses/{id}/cancel` / `/tokenize`+`/detokenize` roundtrip assertions.

## `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` Hot Expert Cache (`k_hot`), `MTP` Residency & Parallel Weight Loading (completed & verified)

- **Parallel Model Weight Loading (`crates/warp34-host/src/model/rank.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/model/weights.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/moe_offload/host_store.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/ple.rs`):**
  - Replaced sequential per-layer projection repacking (`MarlinW4A16`, `MarlinW8A16`, `DenseF16`) with `PreparedLinearShard` and per-layer `std::thread::scope` parallel preparation across all attention/GDN/shared-expert projections before ordered `DeviceArena` upload, cutting `Qwen3.8-27B` load time by **3.09×** (`33.15s -> 10.72s`) and `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` load time to **`~29.5s`** while keeping peak host RAM bounded to a single layer's projections.
  - Parallelized `HostExpertStore::load_with_pin` across worker chunks and `PleWeightStore::load` across configured `ple_layer_ids` / 128 FP8 PLE shards.
  - Serialized device-wide synchronization/allocation entrypoints (`warp34_device_synchronize`, `warp34_cublas_destroy`, `warp34_malloc`, `warp34_free`, `warp34_host_alloc_mapped`, `warp34_host_free`, `warp34_stream_create`, `warp34_stream_destroy`, `warp34_graph_destroy`, `warp34_cublas_create`) against active `warp34_graph_begin_capture` (`cudaStreamCaptureModeRelaxed`) via `std::shared_mutex` in `crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_init.cu`.
- **`Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` (`qwen4_exp`) GPU Expert Cache & `MTP` Residency (`crates/warp34-host/src/model/rank.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/mtp.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/speculative.rs`):**
  - Centralized tiered-MoE, `MTP`, and `scratch_arena` VRAM auto-planning in `crates/warp34-host/src/model/rank.rs` (`plan_tiered_moe_hot_per_layer`, `default_tiered_moe_hot_ceiling`, `plan_mtp_tiered_moe_hot_experts`, and `plan_scratch_arena_bytes`), `crates/warp34-host/src/scheduler.rs`, `crates/warp34-host/src/moe_offload.rs`, and `crates/warp34-host/src/dflash.rs` with named constants and joint reserve/scratch unit tests (`crates/warp34-host/src/model/tests.rs`).
  - Expanded `plan_tiered_moe_hot_per_layer` default hot experts per layer to **`96`** on `TP=1` (`<= 1.5 GiB` KV+headroom), **`88`** on `TP=1` with `4,096`-page (`64K`) serving KV pools, and **`112`** per rank on `TP=2 / EP=2` (`224 / 512 = 43.75%` of target experts resident in VRAM across 2 GPUs).
  - Updated `Qwen3_5MtpRank::from_safetensors_with_tp` (`crates/warp34-host/src/mtp.rs`) via `plan_mtp_tiered_moe_hot_experts` so `TP >= 2` loads all `local_experts` (`256/rank = 100%` of `MTP` routed experts in VRAM, skipping `cudaHostAllocMapped` and releasing temporary host storage) when `free_vram >= slots_all_need`, and `TP=1` retains `k_hot = 192` (`MTP_TP1_STREAMED_PREFILL_MAX_HOT`) whenever `free_vram >= slots_tp1_max_need` (`762 MiB`).
  - Added lossless prompt/context suffix n-gram continuation lookup (`lookup_suffix_continuation` in `crates/warp34-host/src/speculative.rs`).
  - **2× RTX 3090 Ti (`sm_86`) `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` Verification:**
    - `test_qwen4_exp_real_checkpoint_tp1_and_tp2`: `TP=1` warm target decode **32.04 tok/s**, `TP=1 MTP-3` warm decode **46.80 tok/s** (`3.00 tok/round`); `TP=2` warm target decode **68.04 tok/s**, `TP=2 MTP-3` cold decode **112.10 tok/s**, `TP=2 MTP-3` warm decode **134.05 tok/s** (`2.86 tok/round`, 32-token prefix parity + 64-token cold/warm determinism PASS).
    - `flash-next-serving.toml` (`4,096` TQ4 pages): `warp34-flash-tp1-mtp3` reaches **31.51 tok/s** on `short-chat` (`587 ms` TTFT, **`1.91×` vs prior `16.50 tok/s`** and faster than `qfn-3090-mtp3` `30.60 tok/s`) and **28.67 tok/s** on `coding-agent-replay` (**`2.29×` vs prior `12.51 tok/s`**); `warp34-flash-tp2-mtp3` reaches **65.29 tok/s** on `short-chat` (`13.34 ms` TPOT, vs `qfn-2x3090-mtp3` `55.61 tok/s`) and **47.98 tok/s** on `coding-agent-replay`.

## 1–2× RTX 4090 (`sm_89`) Verification, `nsys` Profiling & Portable CPU MoE Bridge (completed & verified)

- **Target Hardware:** 2× NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090 (`sm_89`, `24,564 MiB` each, 128 SMs, 72 MiB L2, `NODE` PCIe 4.0 x16 topology) + AMD EPYC 7542 32-Core Processor (`Zen 2`, `32C/64T`, `avx2 + fma + f16c`, `251 GiB` RAM), cross-verified alongside local AMD Ryzen 9 7950X3D (`16C/32T`, `avx512f + avx512vl`, 96 MiB 3D V-Cache) + 2× RTX 3090 Ti (`sm_86`, PCIe 4.0 x8).
- **`sm_89` & Host CPU Portability Optimizations:**
  1. **Three-tier 0-ULP CPU MoE evaluator & canonical multi-CCD/multi-die SMT topology (`crates/warp34-host/src/moe_cpu_bridge.rs`, `moe_cpu_bridge/eval.rs`, `moe_offload.rs`):**
     - Runtime dispatch selects `expert_avx512` (`avx512f + avx512vl`), `expert_avx2` (`avx2 + fma + f16c` with `R = 2, T = 2` register-resident YMM tiles), or `expert_portable` (pure scalar Rust fallback), all sharing the exact same 16-lane `[0..8] + [8..16]` pairwise FMA reduction tree for 0-ULP bit-identity across all x86_64 and non-x86_64 CPUs.
     - `HotExpertCache::cpu_bridge_supported()` delegates to `moe_cpu_bridge::supported()` so explicit `CpuHybrid` works on any CPU, while `HotExpertCache::auto_cpu_bridge_preferred()` gates automatic `TP=1` static-slot override on `avx512f && avx512vl` so AVX2 + PCIe 4.0 x16 hosts keep the faster dynamic `GpuLru` path by default.
     - `parse_canonical_core_key` uses `thread_siblings_list` (`min_sibling_cpu`) when available and includes `die_id` in the `(physical_package_id, die_id, core_id)` fallback so multi-CCD/multi-die CPUs that reset `core_id` per CCD/die never alias distinct physical cores, while preserving natural OS CPU order (`cpus[0]` on CCD 0 / 3D V-Cache).
  2. **Payload-proportional `TP=2` / `TP=4` collective CTA sizing (`crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_collectives.cu`):** Sizing `warp34_tp2_all_reduce_{f16,bf16,f32}` and `warp34_tp4_all_reduce_{f16,bf16,f32}` by `elems_per_block` (`4096` for `vec8` FP16/BF16, `2048` for `vec4` FP32) and capping small payloads (`<= 36 * 5120`) at `16` CTAs cut `tp2_all_reduce_f16_kernel` GPU time on `Flash-Next TP=2` by **47%** (`504 µs/tok → 268 µs/tok`) and boosted `Flash-Next TP=2` decode from **62.11 → 72.34 tok/s (`+16.5%`)**.
  3. **Fused dual small-N GEMM for HyperConnection & 512-expert routers (`crates/warp34-host/src/model/weights.rs`):** Fused `HcBlockWeights::forward_mix` (`input_mix_weight_down [320, 10240]` + `block_inject_weight [4, 10240]`) via `warp34_small_n_gemm_dual_f16` for decode/spec-verify (`num_tokens <= 16`) and extended `LinearProj::DenseF16` / `FfnBlockWeights::Moe` small-N dispatch to `size_n <= 256 || (size_n <= 512 && m <= 16)`, eliminating 240 separate cuBLAS split-K launches per step on `Flash-Next` while preserving 0-ULP parity between `M = 1` decode and `M <= 16` speculative verification.
  4. **`27B TP=1` state-slot budget for `DFlash2` on 24 GiB cards (`crates/warp34-host/src/model/rank.rs`):** Lowering the large-model state-slot threshold to `15 GiB` (`default_state_slots = 12`, `1.72 GiB`) retains all `11` state slots required by `DFlash2` (`block_size = 8`, `k = 7` verify schedule) while leaving `>1.6 GiB` free VRAM for `DFlash2Rank::load` + KV pages on a single 24 GiB GPU.
- **1–2× RTX 4090 (`sm_89`) vs 1–2× RTX 3090 Ti (`sm_86`) Benchmark Matrix (`prompt_len=512`, `gen_len=128`, `--kv-format tq4`, all `--verify-lossless` PASS):**
  - **`nchapman/Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ` (`TQ4` KV):**
    - `TP=1` (1× RTX 4090 vs 1× RTX 3090 Ti):
      - Plain decode (`M=1`): **55.01 tok/s** (`18.18 ms/tok`, 512-tok TTFT **162.91 ms** / **3,142.8 tok/s**) vs **51.59 tok/s** (`361.96 ms` TTFT).
      - `MTP (k=3)`: **125.84 tok/s** (`7.95 ms/tok`, `30.58 ms/round`, `mean_tokens_per_round = 3.85`, **`2.29×` vs plain**) vs **120.73 tok/s**.
      - `DFlash2 (k=7)`: **253.02 tok/s** (`3.95 ms/tok`, `27.89 ms/round`, `mean_tokens_per_round = 7.06`, **`4.60×` vs plain**) vs **240.63 tok/s**.
    - `TP=2` (2× RTX 4090 vs 2× RTX 3090 Ti, PCIe):
      - Plain decode (`M=1`): **92.51 tok/s** (`10.81 ms/tok`, 512-tok TTFT **229.96 ms** / **2,226.5 tok/s**) vs **86.37 tok/s** (`320.08 ms` TTFT).
      - `MTP (k=3)`: **175.37 tok/s** (`5.70 ms/tok`, `18.57 ms/round`, `mean_tokens_per_round = 3.26`, **`1.90×` vs plain**) vs **163.92 tok/s**.
      - `DFlash2 (k=7)`: **204.29 tok/s** (`4.89 ms/tok`, `18.83 ms/round`, `mean_tokens_per_round = 3.85`, **`2.21×` vs plain**; **411.24 tok/s** at `gen_len=32`) vs **192.15 tok/s** (**396.61 tok/s** at `gen_len=32`).
  - **`albucino/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-FP8PLE@ef554143369a706525336f6b42a09094835dc077` (`512` experts, `top-10`):**
    - `TP=1` (1× RTX 4090 `GpuLru` vs 1× RTX 3090 Ti `CpuHybrid`):
      - Plain decode (`M=1`): **33.86 tok/s** (`29.53 ms/tok`, 512-tok TTFT **846.56 ms**) vs **28.50 tok/s** (`1,022.41 ms` TTFT).
      - `MTP (k=2)`: **39.50 tok/s** (`25.32 ms/tok`, `60.67 ms/round`, `mean_tokens_per_round = 2.40`) vs **36.25 tok/s**.
    - `TP=2` (2× RTX 4090 vs 2× RTX 3090 Ti, `EP=2`, `48` hot experts/layer/rank):
      - Plain decode (`M=1`): **72.34 tok/s** (`13.82 ms/tok`, 512-tok TTFT **371.27 ms** / **1,379.1 tok/s**) vs **52.43 tok/s** (`556.83 ms` TTFT).
      - `MTP (k=2)`: **104.91 tok/s** (`9.53 ms/tok`, `23.74 ms/round`, `mean_tokens_per_round = 2.49`, **`+57.4%` vs 3090 Ti**) vs **66.64 tok/s**.
      - `MTP (k=3)`: **90.10 tok/s** (`11.10 ms/tok`, `30.64 ms/round`, `mean_tokens_per_round = 2.76`) vs **61.18 tok/s**.

## NVLink P2P Collectives & TP=4 / EP=4 Execution (completed & verified)

- **P2P/NVLink VRAM collectives (`Tp2Collectives::new_for_tp_with_p2p`):** `warp34_device_can_enable_p2p(dev_a, dev_b, require_native_atomic, out_can_p2p)` probes `cudaDeviceCanAccessPeer` + `cudaDeviceGetP2PAttribute` and enables bidirectional `cudaDeviceEnablePeerAccess` across TP peer pairs. When all pairs support direct P2P (`TP=2` over bonded `NV4` NVLink `112.5 GB/s`), `Tp2Collectives` allocates `sigs` and `stages` in `CommBuffer::DeviceVram` (`DeviceBuffer`) so peer kernels perform 1-hop NVLink VRAM reads/writes with `threadfence_system()` release/acquire flags; when any pair lacks P2P (`TP=2` over non-P2P PCIe or `TP=4` across dual NVLink pairs connected by `NODE`), it transparently falls back to `CommBuffer::MappedHost` (`MappedHostBuffer`).
- **`TP=4 / EP=4` kernels & runtime (`op_collectives.cu`, `crates/warp34-host`):**
  - Added `warp34_tp4_all_reduce_f16`, `warp34_tp4_all_reduce_residual_gemma_rms_norm_f16`, `warp34_tp4_all_reduce_bf16`, `warp34_tp4_all_reduce_f32`, `warp34_tp4_broadcast_i32`, `warp34_tp4_all_gather_top1_f32_i32`, and `warp34_tp4_gather_vocab_f16`, all CUDA-Graph capturable with monotonic epoch barriers (`barrier_tp4_epoch_release` + `flag_counter`).
  - Generalized `ModelConfig` sharding (`Qwen3_5ModelRank::validate_tp_geometry`, `local_kv_heads`, `kv_head_shard_index` replicating `2` KV heads across rank pairs `(0,1)` and `(2,3)` on `TP=4` when `num_key_value_heads < tp_size`), `PleWeightStore` `TP=4` shard loading (`d_ple / 4 = 512`), `Qwen3_5MTP` (`tp_size = 1 | 2 | 4`), `DFlash2` (`broadcast_from_rank0_i32`), and `Qwen3_5Engine` worker synchronization across `1`, `2`, or `4` ranks.
  - **100% VRAM-resident `EP=4` MoE fast path (`moe_offload.rs`):** On `TP=4 / EP=4` for `Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-FP8PLE`, all `512` routed experts (`128` local experts/rank = `3.50 GiB/rank`) fit in the `k_hot = 128` GPU hot cache (`k_hot == num_local_experts`). `HostExpertStore::load_with_pin(..., pin_host = false)` uses unpinned heap memory during weight loading and calls `release_host_storage()` immediately after `DeviceLruExpertCache::populate_initial` + `stream.synchronize()`, avoiding `74 GiB` of locked host memory. `execute_layer_device_lru` dispatches `warp34_moe_remap_topk_ids_i32` + `launch_moe_w4a16` directly on the GPU for both prefill and decode without CPU synchronization or LRU traffic.
- **4× RTX 3090 Dual-NVLink Pod (`GPU0<->GPU3` and `GPU1<->GPU2` bonded `NV4`, AMD EPYC 7B13) Verification & Benchmarks (`prompt_len=512`, `gen_len=128`, `--kv-format tq4`):**
  - **Unit & Parity Checks:** `test_tp2_p2p_and_tp4_collectives` in `tests/backbone_and_collectives.rs` passes on both `TP=2` NVLink (`CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0,3`, `is_p2p_vram() == true`) and `TP=4` (`0,3,1,2`), and `--verify-lossless` passes on `TP=4` for both `27B + DFlash2` and `Flash-Next + MTP (k=3)`.
  - **`nchapman/Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ`:**
    - `TP=2` PCIe (`0,1`) vs `TP=2` NVLink P2P (`0,3`):
      - Plain decode (`M=1`): 512-tok TTFT **329.93 ms → 205.73 ms (`1,551.8 → 2,488.7 tok/s`, `+60.4%`)**; decode **90.25 → 91.29 tok/s** (`10.95 ms/tok`).
      - `MTP (k=3)`: TTFT **362.99 ms → 216.34 ms (`+67.8%`)**; verify **18.80 → 17.86 ms/round**; decode **146.88 → 154.61 tok/s** (`6.47 ms/tok`).
      - `DFlash2 (M=16)`: TTFT **389.49 ms → 217.03 ms (`1,314.5 → 2,359.1 tok/s`, `+79.5%`)**; verify **19.75 → 17.42 ms/round (`-2.32 ms/round`)**; decode **173.81 → 197.00 tok/s (`+13.3%`, `5.08 ms/tok`)** with bit-identical output tokens.
    - `TP=4` (`0,3,1,2`):
      - Plain decode (`M=1`): **129.10 tok/s** (`7.75 ms/tok`, **`+41.4%`** over `TP=2` NVLink).
      - `MTP (k=3)`: **13.22 ms/verify_round** (**291.04 tok/s**, `3.44 ms/tok` at `3.85 tok/round`).
      - `DFlash2 (M=16)`: **15.25 ms/verify_round** (**438.29 tok/s**, `2.28 ms/tok` at `6.68 tok/round`, `--verify-lossless` PASS).
  - **`albucino/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-FP8PLE` (`512` experts, `top-10`):**
    - `TP=2` PCIe (`0,1`) vs `TP=2` NVLink P2P (`0,3`):
      - Plain decode (`M=1`): TTFT **420.84 ms → 372.30 ms (`1,216.6 → 1,375.2 tok/s`, `+13.0%`)**; decode **62.02 → 62.65 tok/s** (`15.96 ms/tok`).
      - `MTP (k=3)`: TTFT **453.53 ms → 393.80 ms (`1,128.9 → 1,300.2 tok/s`, `+15.2%`)**; verify **31.61 → 31.05 ms/round**; decode **89.28 → 90.89 tok/s** (`11.00 ms/tok`).
    - `TP=4 / EP=4` (`0,3,1,2`, 100% VRAM-resident 512 experts, zero CPU offload):
      - 512-tok Prefill: **186.80 ms (`2,740.9 tok/s`, `2.00×` vs `TP=2` NVLink, `2.25×` vs `TP=2` PCIe)**.
      - Plain decode (`M=1`): **90.64 tok/s** (`11.03 ms/tok`, **`+44.7%`** over `TP=2`).
      - `MTP (k=3)`: **17.97 ms/verify_round (`1.73×` faster round vs `TP=2`) → 147.23 tok/s (`6.79 ms/tok`, `+62.0%` over `TP=2`, `--verify-lossless` PASS)**.

## Rust CPU MoE Bridge Migration & Performance (completed & verified)

- **Architecture:** Host SIMD `u4b8` evaluation, dynamic physical-core worker pool, responder, and mapped mailbox ownership live in `crates/warp34-host/src/moe_cpu_bridge.rs` and `crates/warp34-host/src/moe_cpu_bridge/eval.rs`. `crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_moe_cpu_bridge.cu` contains only stateless GPU mailbox kernels and explicit-pointer launch wrappers (`warp34_moe_hybrid_submit_and_pull`, `warp34_moe_hybrid_wait_and_add`).
- **Dynamic core topology & lock-free dispatch:** `detect_worker_cpus()` queries `sched_getaffinity` and deduplicates SMT siblings via `/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu*/topology/{thread_siblings_list,physical_package_id,die_id,core_id}` (capped at `32` workers, with `available_parallelism` fallback), pinning the responder as worker `0` on `cpus[0]` and spawning `cpus.len() - 1` background workers on `cpus[1..]`. `Wake` tracks `sleeping` workers with `SeqCst` ordering so `FUTEX_WAKE` syscalls are skipped while workers are in their user-space spin window.
- **Active-token compaction & register-resident L1 weight tiling:** `evaluate_into` compacts sparse prefill routing into `RoutingScratch` (`active_toks` + `tok_to_compact`), preparing only tokens routed to at least one CPU cold expert, reducing expert outputs with AVX2/F16C (or portable fallback), and parking `moe-responder` via `PrefillGuard` so all physical cores execute prefill jobs 1-to-1.
- **Pool microbenchmark (`benchmark_flash_next_pool` on Ryzen 9 7950X3D):** `tokens=1` median **152.4 µs** (`p90 165.7 µs`), `tokens=4` median **427.6 µs** (`p90 451.9 µs`), `tokens=16` median **1,215.1 µs** (`p90 1,233.5 µs`), `prefill tokens=6144 (16x12 active)` median **2,097.2 µs** (`45×` faster than the `94,709.7 µs` uncompacted baseline).
- **Safe failure propagation:** Decode evaluation errors acknowledge the mailbox with `status = 1` (skipping stale output addition on the GPU) and latch a sticky host failure on the owning `CudaStream`. At serving tick boundaries (`engine_worker.rs`), `require_healthy_host_compute` escalates to the supervised worker boundary, closing ingress and notifying active and queued requests rather than continuing to serve from corrupted KV/recurrent state.

## Core Engine Capabilities & Verification Summary

`warp34` is feature-complete and verified across **1–4× RTX 30-series (`sm_86`) and 40-series (`sm_89`) GPUs**:

- **Foundational Contracts & Multi-Format Loader (`docs/contracts/`, `crates/warp34-host/src/{cuda,loader}.rs`):**
  - Native download-on-first-use via `hf-hub 1.0.0` (including Xet transfers, `--revision`, `--draft-revision`, and `--offline`, documented in [`MODELS.md`](MODELS.md)).
  - Supports Qwen 3.5 dense (`0.8B`, `4B`, `9B`), Qwen 3.8 dense (`27B`), Qwen 3.6 MoE (`35B-A3B`), Qwen 3.8 Flash-Next (`qwen4_exp` 512-expert MoE + `PLE` + `QSA` + `HC`, documented in [`QWEN_FLASH_NEXT.md`](QWEN_FLASH_NEXT.md)), Qwen 3.5-VL (`4B`, `27B`, `35B-A3B`), and `DFlash2` drafters (`4B`, `27B`) across symmetric/asymmetric `W4A16` (`GPTQ`, `AWQ`, `compressed-tensors`, `AutoRound`), `W8A16`, FP8 `PLE`, and unquantized `FP16`/`BF16`.
- **Hybrid Backbone, Continuous Batching, & `MambaRadixCache` (`crates/warp34-host/src/{cache,model,radix,scheduler}.rs`):**
  - All 5 core cross-subsystem invariant tests pass in `crates/warp34-host/tests/backbone_and_collectives.rs` and `crates/warp34-host/tests/radix_cache_and_scheduler.rs`.
  - `MambaRadixCache` achieves a **95.2% warm prefix TTFT reduction** (`145.68 ms → 7.05 ms` on 4B; `570 ms` p50 TTFT on `Flash-Next` `prefix-primed-r16` with `94.8%` prefix cache hit ratio).
- **Speculative Decoding (`MTP` & `DFlash2`: `crates/warp34-host/src/{speculative,mtp,dflash,proposer}.rs`, `tests/{speculative_decoding,dflash_proposer,mtp_proposer,speculative_handoff}.rs`):**
  - Per-rank CUDA-Graphed `Qwen3_5MTP` (dense, `35B-A3B` MoE, and `Flash-Next` HC MoE, including `B = 2..4` batched multi-sequence verify) and native-`BF16` `DFlash2` (`M=16` on `4B` reaching **`1,228–1,388 tok/s`**, `M=8` on `27B` reaching **`240.6–253.0 tok/s` on `TP=1`** and **`438.3 tok/s` on `TP=4`**).
- **Paged `TQ4` (`3.07×`), `TQ4_All` (`3.88×`), & `Q8_0` (`1.88×`) KV Compression (`crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/op_kv_cache.cu`, `crates/warp34-host/src/cache.rs`, `tests/tq4_vision_and_moe.rs`):**
  - Tail-anchored `tq4` (`4` unrotated `Q8_0` tail layers `N-4..=N-1` + `12` rotated LSQ `TQ4` layers `0..=N-5` = `21,376 B/tok` on 27B) and `tq4_all` (`16,896 B/tok`) use per-128 post-WHT sub-block RMS normalization (`0.70710678f * rsqrtf(s_sq_b)`) before `Beta(256)` bucketization + per-128 least-squares scaling (`vc_dot_b / c_sq_b`), boosting 128-token `greedy-diff` token parity (`12/12` oracle adjudicated on all models) on `27B tq4` from `906/1536 (59.0%)` to **`1322/1536 (86.1%)`**, `9B tq4` from `978/1536 (63.7%)` to **`1287/1536 (83.8%)`**, `4B tq4` from `693/1536 (45.1%)` to **`913/1536 (59.4%)`**, and `4B tq4_all` from `460/1536 (29.9%)` to **`777/1536 (50.6%)`**, while passing **8/8** single-GPU `27B` (`GPTQ` & `HGPTQ`) needle-in-a-haystack evaluations across `32K / 64K / 128K / 262K` context windows (`tools/oracle/needle_matrix.py`, exact answer `"73918462"`) and live multi-turn `252K`-token HTTP serving (`29/29` requests).
- **Multimodal Vision (`Qwen3_VisionTransformer`: `crates/warp34-host/src/vision.rs`, `tests/{vision_positions,vision_rounding,multimodal_images}.rs`):**
  - Passes all **6/6** canonical PNG/JPEG `vLLM` oracle cases (`tools/oracle/vision_matrix.py`) across `4B` (`.003849 / .005223`), `27B` (`.049868 / .012703`), and `35B-A3B` (`0.0` bit-exact visual/embed frontend, `6.82e-5..1.25e-4` layer-0 MoE parity, `8/8` greedy exact).
- **Structured Outputs, Multi-Turn ChatML, & Concurrent HTTP/SSE Serving (`crates/warp34-host/src/{constraints,chatml,server,responses_api,anthropic_api,clef,decision}.rs`):**
  - `<think>`-aware `xgrammar-rs` JSON Schema / Regex / CFG / Structural Tag bitmasks and native Qwen3-XML `tool_choice` enforcement (`tests/{structured_constraints,http_constraints,chatml_formatting}.rs`).
  - Concurrent `N=8` and `N=16` live SSE serving (`tools/oracle/serve_bench.py`) beats both `vLLM 0.31.0` and `SGLang v0.5.19` across TTFT, ITL, and aggregate throughput on `4B` (`N=8` **`1,388.14 tok/s`** vs `SGLang` `1,354.85` / `vLLM` `1,256.04`; `N=16` **`2,213.71 tok/s`** vs `SGLang` `2,194.17` / `vLLM` `2,038.01`), `27B` (`TP=1` & `TP=2`), `35B-A3B` (`TP=2` **`1,009.72 tok/s`** at `C=8`), and `Flash-Next` (`5/5` sweep on both `TP=1` and `TP=2`).
  - Full OpenAI (`/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/completions`, `/v1/responses`, `/v1/models`), Anthropic (`/v1/messages`, `/v1/messages/count_tokens`), Decision (`/v1/systemone`, `/v1/decisions` supporting `Clef`, `Kev`, `Nimble`, `Tev1`, `OpenJev`, `Xor`/`Lev`), Prometheus `/metrics`, `/api/metrics`, optional Bearer/`x-api-key` authentication ([`SERVING.md`](SERVING.md)), and embedded React/Vite dashboard ([`METRICS.md`](METRICS.md)).
