## Standalone Benchmark Campaigns (`benchmarks/perf/`)

- **Campaign & Qualification Reports:** See [`benchmarks/perf/README.md`](../benchmarks/perf/README.md), [`benchmarks/perf/campaigns/standings.md`](../benchmarks/perf/campaigns/standings.md), and [`benchmarks/perf/campaigns/longctx-coding-agent.md`](../benchmarks/perf/campaigns/longctx-coding-agent.md).
- **27B (`nchapman/Qwen3.8-27B-W4A16-HGPTQ@0df81ead...`):**
  - 1-GPU `FP16` campaign (`27b-fp16-first.toml`, `6/6` quality passed): `decode-c1` **`52.12 tok/s`** (`140.03 ms` TTFT, `18.71 ms` TPOT vs `vLLM` `45.32 tok/s`, `SGLang` `26.86 tok/s`, `llama.cpp` `39.17 tok/s`), `throughput-c8` **`311.75 tok/s`** (`859.41 ms` TTFT vs `vLLM` `222.74 tok/s`, `SGLang` `152.93 tok/s`), `agent-c1` **`49.21 tok/s`** (`152.21 ms` TTFT vs `vLLM` `37.65 tok/s`), `prefill-primed-c1` **`51.98 tok/s`** (`31.22 ms` TTFT, `100%` prefix-cache hit vs `vLLM` `44.71 tok/s`) — ahead of all competitors across all 4 workloads.
  - 2-GPU `TP=2` speculative & long-context campaigns (`27b-spec-and-tp2.toml`, `6/6` quality passed): `decode-c1` **`82.97 tok/s`** plain (`109.27 ms` TTFT) / **`146.36 tok/s`** (`MTP k=2`, `6.00 ms` TPOT vs `vLLM MTP-2` `103.30 tok/s`) / **`157.31 tok/s`** (`DFlash2 M=8`, `5.52 ms` TPOT vs `SGLang DFlash` `31.99 tok/s`), `spec-c2` **`152.90 tok/s`** plain / **`206.41 tok/s`** (`MTP k=2`, `124.94 ms` p50 TTFT vs `vLLM MTP-2` `140.42 tok/s`), `throughput-c8` **`405.82 tok/s`** (`706.35 ms` TTFT vs `vLLM TP=2` `290.96 tok/s`, `SGLang TP=2` `172.76 tok/s`), `prefill-3k-c1` **`27.34 tok/s`** plain (`1,609.10 ms` TTFT vs `vLLM TP=2` `23.51 tok/s` / `2,217.50 ms` TTFT) / **`31.29 tok/s`** (`MTP k=2`) / **`31.76 tok/s`** (`DFlash2`), 59K coding-agent **`13.60 tok/s`** (warm p50 TTFT **`837 ms`** vs `vLLM` `1,231 ms` / `SGLang` `1,339 ms`), and 118K coding-agent **`6.96 tok/s`**.
- **35B-A3B MoE (`nchapman/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-W4A16-HGPTQ@4f5de21c...`):**
  - 0-ULP 16-value warp butterfly reduction (`reduce_store_tile_4x4_f16` & `reduce_store_rows4_f16` in `op_norms_rope_act.cu`, cutting warp shuffles from `80` to `16`) cuts `small_n_gemm_dual_4x4_f16_kernel` (`router_gate + shared_expert_gate`) latency by **`1.67×–1.81×`** (`180.5 -> 107.8 µs` at `M=512`, `27.2 -> 15.0 µs` at `M=64`), lifting `warp34 bench --tp 2` 512-token prefill to **`7,579.18 tok/s`** (`67.55 ms` TTFT, `+20.2%`) and `M=1` decode to **`280.78–281.32 tok/s`** (`527.81 tok/s` with `MTP k=2`, `2,647.05 tok/s` at `B=8`, `radix` & `vision` `lossless_parity: true`).
  - 2-GPU `TP=2` (`35b-a3b-moe-serving.toml`, `6/6` quality passed): `decode-c1` **`261.21 tok/s`** plain (`33.96 ms` TTFT) / **`389.60 tok/s`** (`MTP k=2`, `36.13 ms` TTFT, `2.30 ms` TPOT) vs `vLLM TP=2` `163.95–191.42 tok/s`; `spec-c2` **`440.39 tok/s`** plain (`49.89 ms` TTFT) / **`469.66 tok/s`** (`MTP k=2`, `44.80 ms` TTFT) vs `vLLM TP=2` `302.54 tok/s`; `spec-c4` **`654.52 tok/s`** plain (`88.01 ms` TTFT) / **`653.07 tok/s`** (`MTP k=2`, `68.80 ms` TTFT) vs `vLLM TP=2` `457.58 tok/s`; `throughput-c8` **`1,019.54 tok/s`** plain (`168.00 ms` TTFT) / **`1,018.68 tok/s`** (`MTP k=2`, `170.58 ms` TTFT) vs `vLLM TP=2` `771.26–856.95 tok/s`; `mixed-poisson` **`100.86 tok/s`** plain (`96.21 ms` mean TTFT) / **`102.33 tok/s`** (`MTP k=2`, `2.60 ms` TPOT) vs `vLLM TP=2` `92.76 tok/s` (`501.36 ms` TTFT).
  - 1-GPU `TP=1 TQ4` (`warp34-35b-tp1-tq4`, `6/6` quality passed): fits on a single 24 GiB RTX 3090 Ti at **`196.83 tok/s`** (`decode-c1`, `40.06 ms` TTFT — outperforming 2-GPU `vLLM TP=2` by `+20.1%`), **`325.84 tok/s`** (`spec-c2`, `58.03 ms` TTFT), **`485.54 tok/s`** (`spec-c4`, `100.07 ms` TTFT), **`729.10 tok/s`** (`throughput-c8`, `189.49 ms` TTFT), and **`98.72 tok/s`** (`mixed-poisson`, `115.74 ms` TTFT).
- **Qwen3.8-Flash-Next (`albucino/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-W4A16-FP8PLE`):**
  - Non-lossy hybrid GPU-LRU (`k_static..k_hot`, `24` LRU slots/layer) + CPU-bridge decode (`warp34_moe_hybrid_submit_and_pull`), zero-copy `MappedHostBuffer` `host_embed_tokens` (freeing `1.21 GiB/rank` VRAM for `+384` `TP=1` / `+768` `TP=2` GPU expert cache slots: **`3,264` total slots across all 48 layers = `68.00/layer`** on `TP=1`; **`9,216` total slots across 2 ranks and 48 layers = `192.00/layer` total / `96.00/layer/rank`** on `TP=2`), `MAX_PROCESS_PINNED_BYTES = 80 GiB` + `posix_memalign`/`cudaHostRegister` fallback (so the full `66.7 GiB` `HostExpertStore` stays pinned in RAM), and preserving OS page cache for `model-plefp8-*` shards (`SafetensorsArchive::drop_page_cache`).
  - Reaches **`136.02 tok/s`** (`TP=2 MTP-3` warm 64-token greedy decode), **`92.76 tok/s`** (`short-chat 128:512 WARM`), **`79.83 tok/s`** (`chat-512` 5-prompt diverse suite mean), **`68.92 tok/s`** (`4096:256`), **`73.00 tok/s`** (`8192:1024`), and **`71.90 tok/s`** (`warp34 bench --tp 2 --speculative mtp --mtp-k 3 --prompt-len 512 --gen-len 128 --kv-format tq4`, up from `61.18 tok/s`).
  - Sweeps **5/5** HTTP serving workloads (`flash-next-serving.toml`, `6/6` quality passed) on both `TP=1` (`warp34-flash-tp1-mtp3`: `short-chat` **`42.43 tok/s`** / `566.19 ms` TTFT / `21.44 ms` TPOT vs `33.56` baseline, `qfn-3090-mtp3` `30.82`, and `Strata` `36.34–37.50` lossless `STRATA_ROUTE_TAIL_SKIP=0` / `39.21` lossy `STRATA_ROUTE_TAIL_SKIP=7`; `coding-agent-replay` **`35.01 tok/s`** / `934.62 ms` TTFT vs `29.78` baseline, `qfn-3090-mtp3` `14.37`, and `Strata` `33.58–33.70` lossless / `34.94` lossy; `prefill-context-r16` `18.37 vs 10.23`, `code-context-r64` `21.90 vs 21.23`, `prefix-primed-r16` `28.67 vs 10.17`) and `TP=2` (`warp34-flash-tp2-mtp3`: `short-chat` **`71.92 tok/s`** vs `qfn-2x3090-mtp3` `56.20` and `Strata TP=2` `42.20`, `coding-agent-replay` **`55.59–56.13 tok/s`** vs `qfn-2x3090-mtp3` `34.94` and `Strata TP=2` `34.23`, `prefill-context-r16` `27.09 vs 21.27`, `code-context-r64` `28.81 vs 28.20`, `prefix-primed-r16` `45.90 vs 20.52`), plus `6/6` long-context retrieval checks through `~252K` input tokens on both `TP=1` and `TP=2`.

---

## Codebase Modularity, Lint Hygiene & Coverage Audit (completed & verified)

- **Documentation Consolidation (`docs/`, `README.md`):** Folded the supported model architecture, quantization scheme (`W4A16`/`W8A16`/`FP8 PLE`/`FP16`/`BF16`), KV policy (`fp16`/`bf16`/`q8_0`/`tq4`/`tq4_all`), and TP/EP/offload reference matrix into [`MODELS.md`](MODELS.md), pruned obsolete research surveys (`docs/API_LANDSCAPE.md` and `docs/FEATURE_AUDIT.md`), and expanded [`README.md`](../README.md) with quickstart CLI/HTTP examples and the canonical documentation index.
- **CUDA Translation Unit Splits (`crates/warp34-kernels/csrc/ops/`):**
  - `op_gdn.cu` (`1,168` lines: conv1d, gated RMSNorm, single-token decode) + `op_gdn_wy.cu` (`1,956` lines: fused WY chunked-64 prefill, recurrent continuation, multi-token verify).
  - `op_moe_router.cu` (`999` lines: top-k softmax/sigmoid routers, expert alignment/gather, small-N GEMM) + `op_moe.cu` (`1,910` lines: `W4A16` / `FP16` sparse MoE GEMV & grouped-MMA kernels).
  - `csrc/tq/tq4_kv_common.cuh` (`122` lines) + `op_kv_cache.cu` (`1,141` lines: `FP16`/`BF16`/`Q8_0`/`TQ4` paged KV write & `DFlash2` context copy) + `op_paged_attn.cu` (`1,473` lines: FlashAttention-2 prefill & paged GQA decode/split-KV).
- **Host Module Decomposition (`crates/warp34-host/src/`):** Split `main.rs` (`27` lines) + `cli/{options,commands,oracle,tests}.rs`, `cuda/{collectives,warmup,tests}.rs`, `loader/{config,quant,tests}.rs`, `clef/{encoding,gpu,tests}.rs`, `decision/{scoring,batch,tests}.rs`, `moe_offload/{host_store,engine,tests}.rs`, `model/{rank,forward,generation,weights}` (`weights/{layers,moe}.rs`), `ple/{tests}.rs`, `mtp/{propose}.rs`, `proposer/{scheduled}.rs`, `server/{http,decision_http}.rs`, `responses_api/{tests}.rs`, `anthropic_api.rs`, and `engine_worker/{runner,decode,tests}.rs` so that **no Rust (`.rs`) or CUDA (`.cu`/`.cuh`) file in the repository exceeds 2,000 lines**.
- **Lint & Rustdoc Hygiene (`crates/warp34-host/{src,tests}/`, `.mise.toml`):** Eliminated all `#[allow(dead_code)]`, all file-level `#![allow(...)]` in `src/`, and all `allow(clippy::undocumented_unsafe_blocks)` across both `src/` and `tests/` (documenting every `unsafe` block with a `// SAFETY:` invariant), and wired `mise run doc-check` (`RUSTDOCFLAGS='-D warnings' cargo doc --no-deps --features cuda`) into `check-quick` and `check`.
- **CLI/HTTP Request Parameter Parity & Parallel Weight Loading (`crates/warp34-host/src/{loader,model,moe_offload,proposer,server,responses_api,anthropic_api}`):**
  - Added `vLLM 0.31.0` / `SGLang v0.5.19` CLI and HTTP sampling/constraint parameter parity across `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/completions`, `/v1/responses`, and `/v1/messages` (`repetition_penalty`, `presence_penalty`, `frequency_penalty`, `min_p`, `min_tokens`, `ignore_eos`, `stop_token_ids`, `seed`, `logit_bias`, `top_logprobs`, `reasoning_effort` / `reasoning.effort` / `output_config.effort`, `chat_template_kwargs.enable_thinking`, and `response_format` / `structured_outputs` / `guided_*`).
  - Replaced intermediate `Vec<u8>` allocations in `SafetensorsArchive::{read_u32,read_i32,read_f32,read_f16_bits}` with direct `pread` (`read_exact_at`, chunk-parallel for tensors `>= 8 MiB`), L1 cache-blocked `32×32` GPTQ `qweight`/`scales` transposes, shared `OnceLock` `embed_tokens`/`lm_head` host buffers across TP ranks, and scoped multi-thread rank/layer/expert loading — cutting warm `warp34 generate --max-tokens 1` startup time from **`13.12 s → 7.25 s` (`1.81×`)** on `27B TP=1`, **`16.06 s → 7.58 s` (`2.12×`)** on `27B TP=2`, **`16.00 s → 8.00 s` (`2.00×`)** on `27B TP=2 + MTP`, and **`22.50 s → 6.78 s` (`3.32×`)** on `35B-A3B MoE TP=2`.
- **Test Coverage (`mise run coverage` & `mise run coverage-full`):** Raised `--lib` line coverage from **49.54%** to **>66%** (`225` host unit tests + `4` `warp34-kernels` unit tests + `31` integration test binaries + `7` doctests + `89` `perf-check` tests) and added `mise run coverage-full` (`--lib --tests`) in `.mise.toml`.
